	public static class NavBarControlStrategyRegistrator
	{
		public static void RegisterNavBarControlStrategy()
		{
			StrategyManager.Default.RegisterStrategy<NavBarControl, NavBarControlStrategy>();
		}

		public static void RegisterNavBarGroupStrategy()
		{
			StrategyManager.Default.RegisterStrategy<NavBarGroup, NavBarGroupStrategy>();
		}
	}
	public abstract class NavBarGroupWrapperBase
	{
		private NavBarGroup target;

		public NavBarGroup Target
		{
			get
			{
				return target;
			}
			set
			{
				if (target != value)
				{
					NavBarGroup oldTarget = target;
					target = value;
					OnTargetChanged(oldTarget, target);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnTargetChanged(NavBarGroup oldTarget, NavBarGroup newTarget)
		{
		}
	}
	public class NavBarGroup_DisplaySourceContentWrapper : NavBarGroupWrapperBase, IContentPresenterWrapper<NavBarGroup>, ITargetWrapper<NavBarGroup>
	{
		public object Content
		{
			get
			{
				return base.Target.Content;
			}
			set
			{
				if (base.Target.Content != value)
				{
					object content = base.Target.Content;
					base.Target.Content = value;
					OnContentChanged(content, value);
				}
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate ContentTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return base.Target.ContentTemplate;
			}
			set
			{
				base.Target.ContentTemplate = value;
			}
		}

		public DataTemplateSelector ContentTemplateSelector { get; set; }

		private void OnContentChanged(object oldContent, object newContent)
		{
			ContentTemplate = ContentTemplateSelector.SelectTemplate(newContent, (DependencyObject)(object)base.Target);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarGroup_DisplaySourceItemsWrapper : NavBarGroupWrapperBase, ISelectorWrapper<NavBarGroup>, IItemsControlWrapper<NavBarGroup>, ITargetWrapper<NavBarGroup>
	{
		public DataTemplate ItemTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return base.Target.ItemTemplate;
			}
			set
			{
				base.Target.ItemTemplate = value;
			}
		}

		public DataTemplateSelector ItemTemplateSelector
		{
			get
			{
				return base.Target.ItemTemplateSelector;
			}
			set
			{
				base.Target.ItemTemplateSelector = value;
			}
		}

		public object ItemsSource
		{
			get
			{
				return base.Target.ItemsSource;
			}
			set
			{
				base.Target.ItemsSource = (IEnumerable)value;
			}
		}

		public object SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				return GetSelectedItem();
			}
			set
			{
				SetSelectedItem(value);
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler SelectionChanged;

		protected override void OnTargetChanged(NavBarGroup oldTarget, NavBarGroup newTarget)
		{
			base.OnTargetChanged(oldTarget, newTarget);
			if (oldTarget != null)
			{
				DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(NavBarGroup.SelectedItemProperty, typeof(NavBarGroup)).RemoveValueChanged(oldTarget, OnSelectedItemChanged);
			}
			if (newTarget != null)
			{
				DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(NavBarGroup.SelectedItemProperty, typeof(NavBarGroup)).AddValueChanged(newTarget, OnSelectedItemChanged);
			}
		}

		private void OnSelectedItemChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.SelectionChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectionChanged(sender, e);
			}
		}

		private object GetSelectedItem()
		{
			if (base.Target.SelectedItem == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return ((FrameworkContentElement)(object)base.Target.SelectedItem).DataContext;
		}

		private void SetSelectedItem(object value)
		{
			base.Target.SelectedItem = base.Target.Items.OfType<NavBarItem>().FirstOrDefault((NavBarItem x) => object.Equals(((FrameworkContentElement)(object)x).DataContext, value));
		}
	}
	public class NavBarGroup_DisplaySourceContentStrategy : ContentPresenterStrategy<NavBarGroup, NavBarGroup_DisplaySourceContentWrapper>
	{
	}
	public class NavBarGroup_DisplaySourceItemsStrategy : SelectorStrategy<NavBarGroup, NavBarGroup_DisplaySourceItemsWrapper>
	{
		protected override void InitItemTemplate()
		{
		}
	}
	public class NavBarGroupStrategy : AggregateStrategy<NavBarGroup>
	{
		protected override IStrategy SelectStrategy()
		{
			if (((BaseStrategy<NavBarGroup>)(object)this).Target.DisplaySource == DisplaySource.Content)
			{
				return (IStrategy)(object)new NavBarGroup_DisplaySourceContentStrategy();
			}
			return (IStrategy)(object)new NavBarGroup_DisplaySourceItemsStrategy();
		}
	}
	public abstract class NavBarControlWrapperBase : ISelectorWrapper<NavBarControl>, IItemsControlWrapper<NavBarControl>, ITargetWrapper<NavBarControl>
	{
		private NavBarControl target;

		private object selectedItem;

		public NavBarControl Target
		{
			get
			{
				return target;
			}
			set
			{
				if (target != value)
				{
					NavBarControl oldTarget = target;
					target = value;
					OnTargetChanged(oldTarget, target);
				}
			}
		}

		public object ItemsSource
		{
			get
			{
				return Target.ItemsSource;
			}
			set
			{
				Target.ItemsSource = (IEnumerable)value;
			}
		}

		public object SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				return selectedItem;
			}
			set
			{
				if (selectedItem != value)
				{
					object oldValue = selectedItem;
					selectedItem = value;
					OnSelectedItemChanged(oldValue, selectedItem);
				}
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate ItemTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException();
			}
			set
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException();
			}
		}

		public DataTemplateSelector ItemTemplateSelector
		{
			get
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException();
			}
			set
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException();
			}
		}

		public event EventHandler SelectionChanged;

		protected virtual void OnTargetChanged(NavBarControl oldTarget, NavBarControl newTarget)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedItemChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			if (this.SelectionChanged != null)
			{
				this.SelectionChanged(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavBarControl_StandardWrapper : NavBarControlWrapperBase
	{
		private bool lockSelection;

		protected override void OnTargetChanged(NavBarControl oldTarget, NavBarControl newTarget)
		{
			base.OnTargetChanged(oldTarget, newTarget);
			if (oldTarget != null)
			{
				DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(NavBarControl.SelectedGroupProperty, typeof(NavBarControl)).RemoveValueChanged(oldTarget, OnNavBarSelectedGroupChanged);
			}
			if (newTarget != null)
			{
				DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(NavBarControl.SelectedGroupProperty, typeof(NavBarControl)).AddValueChanged(newTarget, OnNavBarSelectedGroupChanged);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnSelectedItemChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			lockSelection = true;
			base.Target.SelectedGroup = newValue;
			lockSelection = false;
			base.OnSelectedItemChanged(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		private void OnNavBarSelectedGroupChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!lockSelection)
			{
				base.SelectedItem = base.Target.SelectedGroup;
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavBarControl_GroupDescriptionWrapper : NavBarControlWrapperBase
	{
		private bool lockSelection;

		protected override void OnTargetChanged(NavBarControl oldTarget, NavBarControl newTarget)
		{
			base.OnTargetChanged(oldTarget, newTarget);
			if (oldTarget != null)
			{
				DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(NavBarControl.SelectedItemProperty, typeof(NavBarControl)).RemoveValueChanged(oldTarget, OnNavBarSelectedItemChanged);
			}
			if (newTarget != null)
			{
				DependencyPropertyDescriptor.FromProperty(NavBarControl.SelectedItemProperty, typeof(NavBarControl)).AddValueChanged(newTarget, OnNavBarSelectedItemChanged);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnSelectedItemChanged(object oldValue, object newValue)
		{
			lockSelection = true;
			base.Target.SelectedItem = newValue;
			lockSelection = false;
			base.OnSelectedItemChanged(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		private void OnNavBarSelectedItemChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!lockSelection)
			{
				base.SelectedItem = base.Target.SelectedItem;
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavBarControl_StandardStrategy : SelectorStrategy<NavBarControl, NavBarControl_StandardWrapper>
	{
		private class InjectionNavBarGroup : NavBarGroup
		{
			public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ContentTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(InjectionNavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((InjectionNavBarGroup)(object)d).OnContenTemplateSelectorChanged();
			}));

			public DataTemplateSelector ContentTemplateSelector
			{
				get
				{
					return (DataTemplateSelector)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(ContentTemplateSelectorProperty);
				}
				set
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)this).SetValue(ContentTemplateSelectorProperty, (object)value);
				}
			}

			protected override void OnContentChanged(object oldValue)
			{
				base.OnContentChanged(oldValue);
				UpdateContentTemplate();
			}

			private void OnContenTemplateSelectorChanged()
			{
				UpdateContentTemplate();
			}

			private void UpdateContentTemplate()
			{
				if (ContentTemplateSelector != null)
				{
					base.ContentTemplate = ContentTemplateSelector.SelectTemplate(base.Content, (DependencyObject)(object)this);
				}
			}
		}

		private DataTemplate navBarGroupTemplate;

		private DataTemplate NavBarGroupTemplate => navBarGroupTemplate ?? (navBarGroupTemplate = CreateNavBarGroupTemplate());

		protected override void InitItemTemplate()
		{
			if (((BaseStrategy<NavBarControl>)(object)this).Target.ItemTemplate == null && ((BaseStrategy<NavBarControl>)(object)this).Target.ItemTemplateSelector == null)
			{
				((BaseStrategy<NavBarControl>)(object)this).Target.ItemTemplate = NavBarGroupTemplate;
			}
		}

		private DataTemplate CreateNavBarGroupTemplate()
		{
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(InjectionNavBarGroup));
			frameworkElementFactory.SetBinding(NavBarGroup.ContentProperty, new Binding());
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(InjectionNavBarGroup.ContentTemplateSelectorProperty, ((BaseStrategy<NavBarControl>)(object)this).ViewSelector);
			frameworkElementFactory.SetValue(NavBarGroup.DisplaySourceProperty, DisplaySource.Content);
			FrameworkElementFactory frameworkElementFactory2 = new FrameworkElementFactory(typeof(ContentControl));
			frameworkElementFactory2.AppendChild(frameworkElementFactory);
			DataTemplate dataTemplate = new DataTemplate
			{
				VisualTree = frameworkElementFactory2
			};
			dataTemplate.Seal();
			return dataTemplate;
		}
	}
	public class NavBarControl_GroupDescriptionStrategy : SelectorStrategy<NavBarControl, NavBarControl_GroupDescriptionWrapper>
	{
		protected override void InitItemTemplate()
		{
		}
	}
	public class NavBarControlStrategy : AggregateStrategy<NavBarControl>
	{
		protected override IStrategy SelectStrategy()
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(((BaseStrategy<NavBarControl>)(object)this).Target.GroupDescription))
			{
				return (IStrategy)(object)new NavBarControl_StandardStrategy();
			}
			return (IStrategy)(object)new NavBarControl_GroupDescriptionStrategy();
		}
	}
}
namespace DevExpress.Xpf.NavBar
{
	public enum ShowMode
	{
		DefaultItem,
		Items,
		All,
		MaximizedDefaultItem
	}
	public class CollapsedActiveGroupControl : Control
	{
		private NavigationPaneView view;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultItemProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultIsCheckedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CollapsedNavPaneItemsSourceProperty;

		public IEnumerable<object> CollapsedNavPaneItemsSource
		{
			get
			{
				return (IEnumerable<object>)GetValue(CollapsedNavPaneItemsSourceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(CollapsedNavPaneItemsSourceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public NavBarGroup Group
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarGroup)GetValue(GroupProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GroupProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ItemsControl Items
		{
			get
			{
				return (ItemsControl)GetValue(ItemsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Control DefaultItem
		{
			get
			{
				return (Control)GetValue(DefaultItemProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DefaultItemProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ShowMode ShowMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (ShowMode)GetValue(ShowModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool DefaultIsChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(DefaultIsCheckedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DefaultIsCheckedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public NavigationPaneView View
		{
			get
			{
				if (view == null)
				{
					view = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<NavigationPaneView>((DependencyObject)this);
				}
				return view;
			}
			set
			{
				view = value;
			}
		}

		public Control LegacyDefaultItem { get; set; }

		static CollapsedActiveGroupControl()
		{
			ItemsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Items", typeof(ItemsControl), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollapsedActiveGroupControl)d).OnItemsChanged((ItemsControl)e.OldValue);
			}));
			DefaultItemProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DefaultItem", typeof(Control), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollapsedActiveGroupControl)d).OnDefaultItemChanged((Control)e.OldValue);
			}));
			ShowModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowMode", typeof(ShowMode), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ShowMode.MaximizedDefaultItem, OnShowModeChanged));
			DefaultIsCheckedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DefaultIsChecked", typeof(bool), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			GroupProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Group", typeof(NavBarGroup), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollapsedActiveGroupControl)d).OnGroupChanged((NavBarGroup)e.OldValue, (NavBarGroup)e.NewValue);
			}));
			CollapsedNavPaneItemsSourceProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CollapsedNavPaneItemsSource", typeof(IEnumerable<object>), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
		}

		private static void OnShowModeChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((CollapsedActiveGroupControl)d).OnShowModeChanged();
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemsChanged(ItemsControl oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnDefaultItemChanged(Control oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnDefaultItemActualIsVisibleChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemsActualIsVisibleChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupChanged(NavBarGroup oldGroup, NavBarGroup newGroup)
		{
			if (newGroup == null)
			{
				ClearValue(CollapsedNavPaneItemsSourceProperty);
				ClearValue(ShowModeProperty);
				return;
			}
			SetBinding(CollapsedNavPaneItemsSourceProperty, new Binding("CollapsedNavPaneItemsSource")
			{
				Source = newGroup
			});
			SetBinding(ShowModeProperty, new Binding("NavPaneShowMode")
			{
				Source = newGroup
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowModeChanged()
		{
			if (DefaultItem != null)
			{
				((CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement)DefaultItem).IsChecked = false;
				switch (ShowMode)
				{
				case ShowMode.MaximizedDefaultItem:
					LegacyDefaultItem.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					DefaultItem.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					Items.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					break;
				case ShowMode.DefaultItem:
					DefaultItem.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					Items.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					LegacyDefaultItem.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					break;
				case ShowMode.Items:
					DefaultItem.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					Items.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					LegacyDefaultItem.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					break;
				case ShowMode.All:
					DefaultItem.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					Items.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					LegacyDefaultItem.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal void UpdateShowMode()
		{
			if (View != null && View.NavBar != null && View.NavBar.ActiveGroup != null)
			{
				ShowMode = View.NavBar.ActiveGroup.NavPaneShowMode;
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			DefaultItem = LayoutHelper.FindElementByName((FrameworkElement)this, "defaultItem") as CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement;
			LegacyDefaultItem = LayoutHelper.FindElementByName((FrameworkElement)this, "legacyDefaultItem") as CollapsedActiveGroup;
			Items = LayoutHelper.FindElementByName((FrameworkElement)this, "items") as CollapsedActiveGroupItemsControl;
			UpdateShowMode();
			OnShowModeChanged();
			SetBinding(UIElement.OpacityProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.Expander.AnimationProgress")
			{
				Converter = new DoubleInvertConverter
				{
					MaxValue = 1.0
				}
			});
			SetBinding(DefaultIsCheckedProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.IsPopupOpen")
			{
				Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
			});
		}
	}
	public class CollapsedActiveGroupItemsPanel : StackPanel
	{
		public CollapsedActiveGroupControl ParentControl { get; set; }

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return MeasureHorizontal(constraint);
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size arrangeSize)
		{
			if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return ArrangeHorizontal(arrangeSize);
			}
			return base.ArrangeOverride(arrangeSize);
		}

		protected virtual Size MeasureHorizontal(Size constraint)
		{
			ParentControl.DefaultItem.Measure(constraint);
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = ParentControl.DefaultItem.DesiredSize.Height;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.Children.Count; i++)
			{
				base.Children[i].Opacity = 1.0;
				base.Children[i].IsHitTestVisible = true;
				base.Children[i].Measure(constraint);
				if (num + base.Children[i].DesiredSize.Width + ParentControl.DefaultItem.DesiredSize.Width <= constraint.Width)
				{
					num += base.Children[i].DesiredSize.Width;
					num2 = Math.Max(num2, base.Children[i].DesiredSize.Height);
				}
				else
				{
					base.Children[i].Opacity = 0.0;
					base.Children[i].IsHitTestVisible = false;
				}
			}
			return new Size(num, num2);
		}

		protected virtual Size ArrangeHorizontal(Size arrangeSize)
		{
			Point point = new Point(arrangeSize.Width, 0.0);
			for (int i = 0; i < base.Children.Count; i++)
			{
				if (base.Children[i].IsHitTestVisible || base.Children[i].Opacity != 0.0)
				{
					base.Children[i].Arrange(new Rect(new Point(point.X - base.Children[i].DesiredSize.Width, 0.0), base.Children[i].DesiredSize));
					point.X -= base.Children[i].DesiredSize.Width;
				}
			}
			ParentControl.DefaultItem.Arrange(new Rect(new Point(point.X - ParentControl.DefaultItem.DesiredSize.Width, 0.0), ParentControl.DefaultItem.DesiredSize));
			return arrangeSize;
		}

		protected override void OnVisualParentChanged(DependencyObject oldParent)
		{
			base.OnVisualParentChanged(oldParent);
			ParentControl = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<CollapsedActiveGroupControl>((DependencyObject)this);
		}
	}
	public class CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement : CollapsedActiveGroupItem
	{
		static CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement()
		{
			EventManager.RegisterClassHandler(typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement), UIElement.MouseDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnLMouseDown), handledEventsToo: true);
		}

		public CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement()
		{
			base.AllowUncheck = true;
		}

		private static void OnLMouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement collapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement)
			{
				collapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement.OnLMouseDown(e);
			}
		}

		private void OnLMouseDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.IsChecked.Value)
			{
				e.Handled = true;
			}
		}
	}
	public class CollapsedActiveGroupItemsControl : ItemsControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultIsCheckedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DefaultElementProperty;

		private CollapsedActiveGroupItem selectedItem;

		public ShowMode ShowMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (ShowMode)GetValue(ShowModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool DefaultIsChecked
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(DefaultIsCheckedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DefaultIsCheckedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement DefaultElement
		{
			get
			{
				return (CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement)GetValue(DefaultElementProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DefaultElementProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static CollapsedActiveGroupItemsControl()
		{
			ShowModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowMode", typeof(ShowMode), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupItemsControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ShowMode.MaximizedDefaultItem));
			DefaultIsCheckedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DefaultIsChecked", typeof(bool), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupItemsControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			DefaultElementProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DefaultElement", typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupItemsControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
		}

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			return new CollapsedActiveGroupItem();
		}

		protected override void OnItemsSourceChanged(IEnumerable oldValue, IEnumerable newValue)
		{
			base.OnItemsSourceChanged(oldValue, newValue);
		}

		protected override void PrepareContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.PrepareContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			CollapsedActiveGroupItem collapsedActiveGroupItem = element as CollapsedActiveGroupItem;
			collapsedActiveGroupItem.Group = base.DataContext as NavBarGroup;
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(collapsedActiveGroupItem, ToggleButton.IsCheckedProperty, new Binding("IsSelected")
			{
				Source = item,
				Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
			});
			if (collapsedActiveGroupItem != null)
			{
				collapsedActiveGroupItem.Checked += OnContainerChecked;
				if (collapsedActiveGroupItem.IsChecked.HasValue && collapsedActiveGroupItem.IsChecked.Value)
				{
					ContainerChecked(collapsedActiveGroupItem);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnContainerChecked(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			CollapsedActiveGroupItem newItem = sender as CollapsedActiveGroupItem;
			ContainerChecked(newItem);
		}

		private void ContainerChecked(CollapsedActiveGroupItem newItem)
		{
			selectedItem.Do(delegate(CollapsedActiveGroupItem x)
			{
				x.Uncheck();
			});
			selectedItem = newItem;
			((base.DataContext as NavBarGroup).NavBar.View as NavigationPaneView).CollapsedNavPaneSelectionStrategy.UpdateSelectedItem(base.DataContext as NavBarGroup);
		}

		protected override void ClearContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.ClearContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			BindingOperations.ClearAllBindings(element);
			selectedItem = null;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}
	}
	public class CollapsedActiveGroupItem : ToggleButton
	{
		private readonly Locker uncheckLocker = new Locker();

		protected bool AllowUncheck { get; set; }

		public NavBarGroup Group { get; set; }

		static CollapsedActiveGroupItem()
		{
			ToggleButton.IsCheckedProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(CollapsedActiveGroupItem), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, null, (DependencyObject d, object e) => ((CollapsedActiveGroupItem)d).CoerceChecked((bool?)e)));
		}

		public CollapsedActiveGroupItem()
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			AllowUncheck = false;
		}

		private object CoerceChecked(bool? newValue)
		{
			if (AllowUncheck)
			{
				return newValue;
			}
			if (uncheckLocker.IsLocked || !newValue.HasValue || newValue.Value == base.IsChecked)
			{
				return newValue;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void Uncheck()
		{
			uncheckLocker.DoLockedAction((Action)delegate
			{
				base.IsChecked = false;
			});
		}
	}

			}
		}
	}

	public class NavBarGroupToNavBarItemConverter : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			NavBarGroup navBarGroup = value as NavBarGroup;

				navBarItem.ImageSource = navBarGroup.ImageSource;
			}
			return navBarItem;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}

	public class SourceToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (parameter.ToString() == "Visible")
			{
				return (value != null) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
			}
			return (value == null) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}

	public class NavBarViewKindToObjectConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object ExplorerBarValue { get; set; }

		public object NavigationPaneValue { get; set; }

		public object SideBarValue { get; set; }

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value is NavBarViewKind)
			{
				switch ((NavBarViewKind)value)
				{
				case NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar:
					return ExplorerBarValue;
				case NavBarViewKind.NavigationPane:
					return NavigationPaneValue;
				case NavBarViewKind.SideBar:
					return SideBarValue;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class DoubleInvertConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public double MaxValue { get; set; }

		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return ConvertCore(value);
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return ConvertCore(value);
		}

		private object ConvertCore(object value)
		{
			if (value is double num)
			{
				return MaxValue - num;
			}
			return -1;
		}
	}
	public class NavBarViewToAllowScrollingConverter : IValueConverter, IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (value is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (value is ExplorerBarView)
			{
				ExplorerBarView explorerBarView = value as ExplorerBarView;
				return explorerBarView.Orientation != explorerBarView.ItemsPanelOrientation;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		object IMultiValueConverter.Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (values[0] is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (values[0] is ExplorerBarView)
			{
				ExplorerBarView explorerBarView = values[0] as ExplorerBarView;
				return explorerBarView.Orientation != explorerBarView.ItemsPanelOrientation;
			}
			return false;
		}

		object[] IMultiValueConverter.ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class DoubleDivisionConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return (double)value / (double)parameter;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return (double)value * (double)parameter;
		}
	}

	public class ExplorerBarView : NavBarViewBase, IScrollMode
	{
		public static RoutedEvent GroupExpandedChangingEvent;

		public static RoutedEvent GroupExpandedChangedEvent;

		ScrollControl IScrollMode.ScrollControl => (ScrollControl)GetTemplateChild("scrollControl");

		public event NavBarGroupExpandedChangingEventHandler GroupExpandedChanging
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(GroupExpandedChangingEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(GroupExpandedChangingEvent, value);
			}
		}

		public event NavBarGroupExpandedChangedEventHandler GroupExpandedChanged
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(GroupExpandedChangedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(GroupExpandedChangedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		static ExplorerBarView()
		{
			GroupExpandedChangingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("GroupExpandedChangingEvent", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(NavBarGroupExpandedChangingEventHandler), typeof(ExplorerBarView));
			GroupExpandedChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("GroupExpandedChangedEvent", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(NavBarGroupExpandedChangedEventHandler), typeof(ExplorerBarView));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<ExplorerBarView>();
		}

		public ExplorerBarView()
		{
			base.NavBarViewKind = NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			UpdateScrollModeStates();
		}

		private void UpdateScrollModeStates()
		{
			ScrollingSettings.UpdateScrollModeStates(this);
		}
	}
	public class ExplorerBarExpandButton : ExpandButtonBase
	{
		static ExplorerBarExpandButton()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<ExplorerBarExpandButton>();
		}

		public ExplorerBarExpandButton()
		{
			base.Click += ExplorerBarExpandButtonClick;
		}

		private void ExplorerBarExpandButtonClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.DataContext is NavBarGroup navBarGroup)
			{
				navBarGroup.ChangeGroupExpanded();
			}
			e.Handled = true;
		}

		protected internal override void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(ExpandButtonBase.IsExpandedProperty, new Binding("IsExpanded"));
		}
	}
	public enum NavBarStringId
	{
		NavPaneMenuShowMoreButtons,
		NavPaneMenuShowFewerButtons,
		NavPaneMenuAddRemoveButtons,
		GroupIsAlreadyAddedToAnotherNavBarException,
		ItemIsAlreadyAddedToAnotherGroupException
	}
	public class NavBarLocalizer : DXLocalizer<NavBarStringId>
	{
		static NavBarLocalizer()
		{
			XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>.SetActiveLocalizerProvider((ActiveLocalizerProvider<NavBarStringId>)(object)new DefaultActiveLocalizerProvider<NavBarStringId>(CreateDefaultLocalizer()));
		}

		protected override void PopulateStringTable()
		{
			((XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>)(object)this).AddString(NavBarStringId.NavPaneMenuShowMoreButtons, "Show More Buttons");
			((XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>)(object)this).AddString(NavBarStringId.NavPaneMenuShowFewerButtons, "Show Fewer Buttons");
			((XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>)(object)this).AddString(NavBarStringId.NavPaneMenuAddRemoveButtons, "Add or Remove Buttons");
			((XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>)(object)this).AddString(NavBarStringId.GroupIsAlreadyAddedToAnotherNavBarException, "The group is already added to another NavBar.");
			((XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>)(object)this).AddString(NavBarStringId.ItemIsAlreadyAddedToAnotherGroupException, "The item is already added to another group.");
		}

		public static XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId> CreateDefaultLocalizer()
		{
			return (XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>)(object)new NavBarResXLocalizer();
		}

		public static string GetString(NavBarStringId id)
		{
			return XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>.Active.GetLocalizedString(id);
		}

		public override XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId> CreateResXLocalizer()
		{
			return (XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>)(object)new NavBarResXLocalizer();
		}
	}
	public class NavBarResXLocalizer : DXResXLocalizer<NavBarStringId>
	{
		public NavBarResXLocalizer()
			: base((XtraLocalizer<NavBarStringId>)(object)new NavBarLocalizer())
		{
		}

		protected override ResourceManager CreateResourceManagerCore()
		{
			return XtraLocalizer.CreateResourceManager<NavBarStringId>((XtraResXLocalizer<NavBarStringId>)(object)this, "DevExpress.Xpf.NavBar.LocalizationRes", typeof(NavBarResXLocalizer));
		}
	}
	public class NavBarAnimationOptions : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty HorizontalExpandProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VerticalExpandProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty StretchChildProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MinWidthProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MinHeightProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ExpandStoryboardProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty CollapseStoryboardProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsExpandedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AnimationProgressProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsAnimationEnabledProperty;

		static NavBarAnimationOptions()
		{
			HorizontalExpandProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("HorizontalExpand", typeof(HorizontalExpandMode), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata((object)(HorizontalExpandMode)0));
			VerticalExpandProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("VerticalExpand", typeof(VerticalExpandMode), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata((object)(VerticalExpandMode)0));
			StretchChildProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("StretchChild", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			MinWidthProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("MinWidth", typeof(double), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0));
			MinHeightProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("MinHeight", typeof(double), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0));
			ExpandStoryboardProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("ExpandStoryboard", typeof(Storyboard), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			CollapseStoryboardProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("CollapseStoryboard", typeof(Storyboard), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			IsExpandedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("IsExpanded", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), new PropertyMetadata(false, OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged));
			AnimationProgressProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("AnimationProgress", typeof(double), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(1.0));
			IsAnimationEnabledProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("IsAnimationEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarAnimationOptions), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
		}

		public static void OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged(DependencyObject sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Storyboard storyboard = (((bool)e.NewValue) ? GetExpandStoryboard(sender) : GetCollapseStoryboard(sender));
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = (FrameworkElement)sender;
			ValueSource valueSource = DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(sender, e.Property);
			if (storyboard != null && valueSource.BaseValueSource != BaseValueSource.Default)
			{
				storyboard.Begin(frameworkElement, isControllable: true);
				if (!GetIsAnimationEnabled(sender))
				{
					storyboard.SkipToFill(frameworkElement);
				}
				else if (!frameworkElement.IsLoaded)
				{
					storyboard.SkipToFill(frameworkElement);
				}
			}
		}

		public static bool GetIsAnimationEnabled(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return (bool)obj.GetValue(IsAnimationEnabledProperty);
		}

		public static void SetIsAnimationEnabled(DependencyObject obj, bool value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(IsAnimationEnabledProperty, value);
		}

		public static void SetAnimationProgress(DependencyObject d, double progress)
		{
			d.SetValue(AnimationProgressProperty, progress);
		}

		public static double GetAnimationProgress(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(AnimationProgressProperty);
		}

		public static void SetHorizontalExpand(DependencyObject d, HorizontalExpandMode expander)
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			d.SetValue(HorizontalExpandProperty, expander);
		}

		public static HorizontalExpandMode GetHorizontalExpand(DependencyObject d)
		{
			//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			return (HorizontalExpandMode)d.GetValue(HorizontalExpandProperty);
		}

		public static void SetVerticalExpand(DependencyObject d, VerticalExpandMode expander)

			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			d.SetValue(VerticalExpandProperty, expander);

	public class NavBarGroupCollection : ObservableCollection<NavBarGroup>
	{
		private NavBarControl navBar;

		public NavBarGroup this[string name]
		{
			get
			{
				using (IEnumerator<NavBarGroup> enumerator = GetEnumerator())
				{
					while (enumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						NavBarGroup current = enumerator.Current;
						if (((FrameworkContentElement)(object)current).Name == name)
						{
							return current;
						}
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal NavBarControl NavBar => navBar;

		public NavBarGroupCollection(NavBarControl navBar)
		{
			this.navBar = navBar;
		}

		protected override void OnCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.NewItems != null)
			{
				foreach (NavBarGroup newItem in e.NewItems)
				{
					if (newItem.NavBar != null && newItem.NavBar != navBar)
					{
						throw new ArgumentException(NavBarLocalizer.GetString(NavBarStringId.GroupIsAlreadyAddedToAnotherNavBarException));
					}
					newItem.NavBar = navBar;
					newItem.IsActive = navBar != null && navBar.ActiveGroup == newItem;
					navBar.AddChild(newItem);
					newItem.OwnerCollection = this;
					((DependencyObject)(object)newItem).CoerceValue(ContentElement.IsEnabledProperty);
				}
			}
			base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
			if (e.OldItems != null)
			{
				foreach (NavBarGroup oldItem in e.OldItems)
				{
					ClearGroupProperties(oldItem);
				}
			}
			navBar.SelectionStrategy.SelectFirstVisibleGroup();
			if (navBar.View != null)
			{
				navBar.View.UpdateViewForce();
			}
			if (navBar.View != null && navBar.View is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				navBar.RaisePropertyChanged("Items");
			}
		}

		protected override void ClearItems()
		{
			while (navBar.Groups.Count != 0)
			{
				RemoveAt(0);
			}
		}

		private void ClearGroupProperties(NavBarGroup group)
		{
			navBar.RemoveChild(group);
			group.NavBar = null;
			group.IsActive = false;
			NavBar.SelectionStrategy.OnGroupRemoved(group);
			((DependencyObject)(object)group).CoerceValue(ContentElement.IsEnabledProperty);
		}
	}
