		private NavigationPaneView NavigationPaneView => navigationPaneView ?? (navigationPaneView = Group.With((NavBarGroup x) => x.NavBar.With((NavBarControl n) => n.View as NavigationPaneView)));

		private NavPaneItemsControlPanel OverflowPanel => overflowPanel_ ?? (overflowPanel_ = (NavPaneItemsControlPanel)NavigationPaneView.With((NavigationPaneView x) => LayoutHelper.FindElementByType((FrameworkElement)x, typeof(NavPaneItemsControlPanel), (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)null)));

		private NavPaneGroupButtonPanel CollapsedPanel => collapsedPanel_ ?? (collapsedPanel_ = (NavPaneGroupButtonPanel)NavigationPaneView.With((NavigationPaneView x) => LayoutHelper.FindElementByType((FrameworkElement)x, typeof(NavPaneGroupButtonPanel), (Func<FrameworkElement, bool>)null)));

		private DispatcherTimer ShowPeekFormTimer => showPeekFormTimer ?? (showPeekFormTimer = new DispatcherTimer().Do(delegate(DispatcherTimer x)
		{
			x.Tick += OnShowPeekFormTimerTick;
		}));

		private DispatcherTimer HidePeekFormTimer => hidePeekFormTimer ?? (hidePeekFormTimer = new DispatcherTimer().Do(delegate(DispatcherTimer x)
		{
			x.Tick += OnHidePeekFormTimerTick;
		}));

		private DataTemplate ActualPeekFormTemplate => Group.With((NavBarGroup x) => x.GetActualPeekFormTemplate());

		private bool HasPeekFormTemplate => Group.Return((NavBarGroup x) => x.HasPeekFormTemplate, () => false);

		private bool IsInOverflowPanel
		{
			get
			{
				if (OverflowPanel != null)
				{
					return OverflowPanel.Children.Contains(this);
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool IsInCollapsedPanel
		{
			get
			{
				if (CollapsedPanel != null && CollapsedPanel.Children.Contains(this))
				{
					return !NavigationPaneView.IsExpanded;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool CanShowPeekForm
		{
			get
			{
				if (HasPeekFormTemplate && NavigationPaneView != null)
				{
					if (!NavigationPaneView.PeekFormShowMode.HasFlag(PeekFormShowMode.Collapsed) || !IsInCollapsedPanel)
					{
						if (NavigationPaneView.PeekFormShowMode.HasFlag(PeekFormShowMode.OverflowPanel))
						{
							return IsInOverflowPanel;
						}
						return false;
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private bool IsFlyoutOpen { get; set; }

		RequestContainersWeakEventHandler INavBarContainer.RequestContainerHandler => requestContainerHandler;

		static NavBarGroupControl()
		{
			ShowBorderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupControl)d).OnShowBorderChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			ActualGroupContentTemplatePropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ActualGroupContentTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			ActualGroupContentTemplateProperty = ActualGroupContentTemplatePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			DisplaySourceProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DisplaySource", typeof(DisplaySource), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), new PropertyMetadata(DisplaySource.Items, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupControl)d).OnDisplaySourceChanged();
			}));
			GroupProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Group", typeof(NavBarGroup), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupControl)d).OnGroupChanged(e);
			}));
			GroupContentPresenterTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupContentPresenterTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupControl)d).UpdateGroupContentTemplate();
			}));
			GroupItemsControlTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupItemsControlTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupControl)d).UpdateGroupContentTemplate();
			}));
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupControl)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			NavBarProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavBar", typeof(NavBarControl), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupControl)d).OnNavBarChanged((NavBarControl)e.OldValue);
			}));
			DisplayModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DisplayMode", typeof(DisplayMode), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DisplayMode.Default, OnDisplayModePropertyChanged));
			LayoutSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("LayoutSettings", typeof(LayoutSettings), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnLayoutSettingsPropertyChanged));
			ImageSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ImageSettings", typeof(ImageSettings), typeof(NavBarGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnImageSettingsPropertyChanged));
			InitNavBarGroupControl();
			TextElementHelper<NavBarGroupControl>.OverrideMetadata((NavBarGroupControl gControl) => (DependencyObject)(object)gControl.Group);
		}

		protected static void OnDisplayModePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroupControl)d).OnDisplayModeChanged((DisplayMode)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected static void OnLayoutSettingsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroupControl)d).OnLayoutSettingsChanged((LayoutSettings)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected static void OnImageSettingsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroupControl)d).OnImageSettingsChanged((ImageSettings)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnNavBarChanged(NavBarControl oldValue)
		{
			this.OnNavBarChanged(oldValue, NavBar);
		}

		public NavBarGroupControl()
		{
			requestContainerHandler = new RequestContainersWeakEventHandler(this, delegate(INavBarContainer container, object sender, RequestContainersEventArgs args)
			{
				args.AddContainer(container);
			});
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, GroupProperty, new Binding
			{
				Converter = new FrameworkElementInfoSLCompatibilityConverterExtension
				{
					ConvertToInfo = false,
					IgnoreWrongValue = true
				}
			});
			base.DataContextChanged += OnDataContextChanged;
			IsFlyoutOpen = false;
		}

		private void OnDisplaySourceChanged()
		{
			UpdateGroupContentTemplate();
		}

		private void OnGroupChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OldValue is NavBarGroup && (e.OldValue as NavBarGroup).GroupControl == this)
			{
				(e.OldValue as NavBarGroup).GroupControl = null;
			}
			if (e.NewValue is NavBarGroup)
			{
				(e.NewValue as NavBarGroup).GroupControl = this;
			}
			SetBinding(UIElement.IsEnabledProperty, new Binding
			{
				Source = Group,
				Path = new PropertyPath("IsEnabled"),
				Mode = BindingMode.OneWay
			});
		}

		internal void AddLogicalChildCore(object obj)
		{
			AddLogicalChild(obj);
		}

		internal void RemoveLogicalChildCore(object obj)
		{
			RemoveLogicalChild(obj);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0084: Expected O, but got Unknown
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			navigationPaneView = null;
			overflowPanel_ = null;
			collapsedPanel_ = null;
			SetFlyoutIsOpen(value: false);
			Flyout = GetTemplateChild("PART_NavPaneFlyout") as NavPaneFlyoutControl;
			UpdateAnimations();
			SetBindings();
			UpdateOrientationStates();
			UpdateGroupToolTip();
			UpdateScrollModeStates();
			if (Expander != null)
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)Expander).ClearValue(UIElement.IsHitTestVisibleProperty);
			}
			Expander = (DXExpander)GetTemplateChild("PART_DXExpander");
			if (Expander != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding((DependencyObject)(object)Expander, UIElement.IsHitTestVisibleProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarAnimationOptions.IsExpandedProperty),
					Source = Expander
				});
			}
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			ShowPeekOnMouseEnter();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			HidePeekFormOnMouseLeave();
		}

		internal void ShowPeekOnMouseEnter()
		{
			if (CanShowPeekForm)
			{
				ShowPeekFormTimer.Stop();
				HidePeekFormTimer.Stop();
				if (!IsFlyoutOpen)
				{
					int peekFormShowDelay = (Group.NavBar.View as NavigationPaneView).PeekFormShowDelay;
					ShowPeekFormTimer.Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(peekFormShowDelay);
					ShowPeekFormTimer.Start();
				}
			}
		}

		internal void HidePeekFormOnMouseLeave()
		{
			if (CanShowPeekForm)
			{
				ShowPeekFormTimer.Stop();
				HidePeekFormTimer.Stop();
				if (IsFlyoutOpen)
				{
					int peekFormHideDelay = (Group.NavBar.View as NavigationPaneView).PeekFormHideDelay;
					HidePeekFormTimer.Interval = TimeSpan.FromMilliseconds(peekFormHideDelay);
					HidePeekFormTimer.Start();
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnHidePeekFormTimerTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			HidePeekFormTimer.Stop();
			SetFlyoutIsOpen(value: false);
		}

		private void OnShowPeekFormTimerTick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_004b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			ShowPeekFormTimer.Stop();
			if (!CanShowPeekForm || Flyout == null || ((FlyoutBase)Flyout).IsOpen)
			{
				return;
			}
			FlyoutPlacement val;
			if (IsInOverflowPanel && OverflowPanel.IsMouseOver)
			{
				val = (FlyoutPlacement)1;
			}
			else
			{
				if (!IsInCollapsedPanel)
				{
					return;
				}
				val = (FlyoutPlacement)2;
			}
			if (ActualPeekFormTemplate != null)
			{
				Flyout flyout = ActualPeekFormTemplate.LoadContent() as Flyout;
				if (flyout != null)
				{
					((ContentControl)(object)Flyout).Content = flyout;
				}
				else
				{
					((ContentControl)(object)Flyout).ContentTemplate = ActualPeekFormTemplate;
				}
				((DependencyObject)(object)((FlyoutBase)Flyout).Settings).SetValue(FlyoutSettings.PlacementProperty, (object)val);
				if (flyout != null)
				{
					((DependencyObject)(object)((FlyoutBase)Flyout).Settings).SetValue(FlyoutSettings.ShowIndicatorProperty, (object)flyout.ShowIndicator);
				}
				SetFlyoutIsOpen(value: true);
			}
		}

		private void SetFlyoutIsOpen(bool value)
		{
			if (value)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)(object)Flyout).DataContext = Group.With((NavBarGroup x) => ((FrameworkContentElement)(object)x).DataContext);
				((FlyoutBase)Flyout).PlacementTarget = this;
				((FlyoutBase)Flyout).IsOpen = true;
				IsFlyoutOpen = true;
			}
			else if (Flyout != null)
			{
				((FlyoutBase)Flyout).IsOpen = false;
				Flyout.Popup.IsOpen = false;
				IsFlyoutOpen = false;
			}
		}

		private void OnDataContextChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateGroupToolTip();
		}

		protected internal virtual void SetBindingOnAnimationProgress()
		{
			if (base.DataContext is NavBarGroup navBarGroup && VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(this) != 0 && navBarGroup.NavBar != null && navBarGroup.NavBar.View != null)
			{
				DXExpander dXExpander = GetDXExpander(this, navBarGroup.NavBar.View);
				if (dXExpander != null)
				{
					BindingOperations.SetBinding((DependencyObject)(object)navBarGroup, NavBarAnimationOptions.AnimationProgressProperty, new Binding("AnimationProgress")
					{
						Source = dXExpander
					});
				}
			}
		}

		private DXExpander GetDXExpander(DependencyObject reference, NavBarViewBase view)
		{
			DXExpander dXExpanderInChildrens = GetDXExpanderInChildrens(reference);
			if (dXExpanderInChildrens == null)
			{
				return GetDXExpanderInChildrens(view);
			}
			return dXExpanderInChildrens;
		}

		private DXExpander GetDXExpanderInChildrens(DependencyObject reference)
		{
			//IL_0044: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0023: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0029: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0035: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_003b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			int childrenCount = VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(reference);
			if (childrenCount == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < childrenCount; i++)
			{
				dependencyObject = VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(reference, i);
				if (dependencyObject is DXExpander)
				{
					return (DXExpander)dependencyObject;
				}
				dependencyObject = (DependencyObject)(object)GetDXExpanderInChildrens(dependencyObject);
				if (dependencyObject != null)
				{
					return (DXExpander)dependencyObject;
				}
			}
			return (DXExpander)dependencyObject;
		}

		private void OnOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		protected virtual void OnImageSettingsChanged(ImageSettings oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnLayoutSettingsChanged(LayoutSettings oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnDisplayModeChanged(DisplayMode oldValue)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowBorderChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, ShowBorder ? "HasBorder" : "NoBorder", useTransitions: false);
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateOrientationStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? "Horizontal" : "Vertical", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(DisplaySourceProperty, new Binding("DisplaySource"));
			SetBinding(OrientationProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.Orientation"));
			SetBinding(ShowBorderProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.ShowBorder"));
			SetBinding(ScrollingSettings.ScrollModeProperty, new Binding("ActualScrollMode"));
			SetBindingOnAnimationProgress();
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				//IL_0069: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_006e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_007a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_008b: Expected O, but got Unknown
				if (Group != null && Group.NavBar != null)
				{
					ClearValue(KeyboardNavigation.TabNavigationProperty);
					if (Group.NavBar.View is ExplorerBarView)
					{
						SetValue(KeyboardNavigation.TabNavigationProperty, KeyboardNavigationMode.Continue);
					}
					else
					{
						SetBinding(KeyboardNavigation.TabNavigationProperty, new Binding("Group.IsActive")
						{
							RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.Self),
							Converter = (IValueConverter)new BoolToObjectConverter
							{
								FalseValue = KeyboardNavigationMode.None,
								TrueValue = KeyboardNavigationMode.Contained
							}
						});
					}
				}
			});
		}

		private void UpdateAnimations()
		{
			NavBarVisualStateHelper.UpdateStates(this, "OrientationStates");
		}

		private void UpdateGroupContentTemplate()
		{
			if (GroupContentPresenterTemplate != null && GroupItemsControlTemplate != null)
			{
				ActualGroupContentTemplate = ((DisplaySource == DisplaySource.Content) ? GroupContentPresenterTemplate : GroupItemsControlTemplate);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateScrollModeStates()
		{
			ScrollingSettings.UpdateScrollModeStates(this);
		}

		private static void InitNavBarGroupControl()
		{
			NavigationAutomationPeersCreator.Default.RegisterObject(typeof(NavBarGroupControl), typeof(NavBarGroupControlAutomationPeer), (CreateObjectMethod<AutomationPeer>)((object owner) => (AutomationPeer)(object)new NavBarGroupControlAutomationPeer((NavBarGroupControl)owner)));
			FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarGroupControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsParentMeasure));
			FrameworkElement.MinWidthProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarGroupControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure | FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsParentMeasure));
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return NavigationAutomationPeersCreator.Default.Create((object)this);
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateGroupToolTip()
		{
			if (base.DataContext is NavBarGroup { NavBar: not null } navBarGroup && navBarGroup.NavBar.View != null && navBarGroup.NavBar.View.NavBarViewKind == NavBarViewKind.NavigationPane)
			{
				NavigationPaneView navigationPaneView = (NavigationPaneView)navBarGroup.NavBar.View;
				if (!navigationPaneView.IsExpanded)
				{
					base.ToolTip = navBarGroup.Header;
				}
				else
				{
					base.ToolTip = null;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				base.ToolTip = null;
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneActiveGroupControl : NavBarGroupControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsExpandedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsContainerOpacityProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty VisibilityCoreProperty;

		internal Size desiredInfiniteSize = new Size(0.0, 0.0);

		public Visibility VisibilityCore
		{
			get
			{
				return (Visibility)GetValue(VisibilityCoreProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(VisibilityCoreProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsExpanded
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsExpandedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsExpandedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double OpacityCore
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(ItemsContainerOpacityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsContainerOpacityProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected CollapsedActiveGroup CollapsedActiveGroup { get; private set; }

		protected internal CollapsedActiveGroupControl CollapsedActiveGroupControl { get; private set; }

		protected NavPanePopup NavPanePopup { get; private set; }

		protected static void OnVisibilityCorePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavPaneActiveGroupControl)d).OnVisibilityCoreChanged((Visibility)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnVisibilityCoreChanged(Visibility oldValue)
		{
		}

		static NavPaneActiveGroupControl()
		{
			VisibilityCoreProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("VisibilityCore", typeof(Visibility), typeof(NavPaneActiveGroupControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Visibility.Visible, OnVisibilityCorePropertyChanged));
			IsExpandedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsExpanded", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneActiveGroupControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneActiveGroupControl)d).OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ItemsContainerOpacityProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsContainerOpacity", typeof(double), typeof(NavPaneActiveGroupControl), new PropertyMetadata(0.0, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneActiveGroupControl)d).OnItemsContainerOpacityPropertyChanged();
			}));
		}

		public NavPaneActiveGroupControl()
		{
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
			base.Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
			base.DataContextChanged += OnDataContextChanged;
		}

		private void OnDataContextChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Size old = desiredInfiniteSize;
			InvalidateMeasure();
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				NavBarGroup navBarGroup = base.DataContext as NavBarGroup;
				UpdateDesiredInfiniteSize(base.DesiredSize);
				if (!old.Equals(desiredInfiniteSize))
				{
					FrameworkElement frameworkElement = this;
					while (!(frameworkElement is NavigationPaneView) && !(frameworkElement is NavBarControl) && frameworkElement != null)
					{
						frameworkElement.InvalidateMeasure();
						frameworkElement = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(frameworkElement) as FrameworkElement;
					}
				}
			});
		}

		private void OnUnloaded(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UnsubscribeTemplateEvents();
		}

		protected void SubscribeTemplateEvents()
		{
			if (CollapsedActiveGroup != null)
			{
				CollapsedActiveGroup.Checked += OnCollapsedActiveGroupChecked;
				CollapsedActiveGroup.Unchecked += OnCollapsedActiveGroupUnchecked;
			}
		}

		private void OnCollapsedActiveGroupUnchecked(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateIsPopupOpened();
		}

		private void OnCollapsedActiveGroupChecked(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateIsPopupOpened();
		}

		private void UpdateIsPopupOpened()
		{
			if (CollapsedActiveGroup != null && base.Group != null && base.Group.NavBar != null && base.Group.NavBar.View is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				((NavigationPaneView)base.Group.NavBar.View).IsPopupOpened = CollapsedActiveGroup.IsChecked.HasValue && CollapsedActiveGroup.IsChecked.Value;
			}
		}

		protected void UnsubscribeTemplateEvents()
		{
			if (CollapsedActiveGroup != null)
			{
				CollapsedActiveGroup.Checked -= OnCollapsedActiveGroupChecked;
				CollapsedActiveGroup.Unchecked -= OnCollapsedActiveGroupUnchecked;
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			UnsubscribeTemplateEvents();
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			CollapsedActiveGroup = GetTemplateChild("collapsedActiveGroup") as CollapsedActiveGroup;
			CollapsedActiveGroupControl = GetTemplateChild("collapsedActiveGroupControl") as CollapsedActiveGroupControl;
			NavPanePopup = GetTemplateChild("popup") as NavPanePopup;
			SubscribeTemplateEvents();
			SetBindings();
			OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged();
			OnItemsContainerOpacityPropertyChanged();
		}

		private void OnLoaded(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged();
			OnItemsContainerOpacityPropertyChanged();
			UnsubscribeTemplateEvents();
			SubscribeTemplateEvents();
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(IsExpandedProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.IsExpanded"));
			SetBinding(ScrollingSettings.ScrollModeProperty, new Binding("ActualScrollMode"));
			SetBinding(ItemsContainerOpacityProperty, new Binding("(0)")
			{
				Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarAnimationOptions.AnimationProgressProperty)
			});
		}

		protected internal override void UpdateOrientationStates()
		{
			base.UpdateOrientationStates();
			if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				ClearValue(FrameworkElement.MinWidthProperty);
				SetBinding(FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty, new Binding("DataContext.View.ActiveGroupMinHeight")
				{
					RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.TemplatedParent)
				});
			}
			else
			{
				ClearValue(FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty);
				SetBinding(FrameworkElement.MinWidthProperty, new Binding("DataContext.View.ActiveGroupMinHeight")
				{
					RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.TemplatedParent)
				});
			}
		}

		private void OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged()
		{
			string stateName = (IsExpanded ? "Expanded" : "Collapsed");
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, stateName, useTransitions: false);
			UpdateGroupToolTip();
		}

		private void OnItemsContainerOpacityPropertyChanged()
		{
			string stateName = ((OpacityCore == 0.0) ? "ShowContent" : "ShowItems");
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, stateName, useTransitions: false);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			return base.MeasureOverride(constraint);
		}

		private void UpdateDesiredInfiniteSize(Size constraint)
		{
			Size constraint2 = new Size((base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? double.PositiveInfinity : constraint.Width, (base.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? double.PositiveInfinity : constraint.Height);
			desiredInfiniteSize = base.MeasureOverride(constraint2);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarGroupContentPresenter : ContentPresenter
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsDataTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsDataTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroupContentPresenter), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroupContentPresenter)d).UpdateTemplate();
		}));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentDataTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ContentDataTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroupContentPresenter), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroupContentPresenter)d).UpdateTemplate();
		}));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DisplaySourceProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DisplaySource", typeof(DisplaySource), typeof(NavBarGroupContentPresenter), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroupContentPresenter)d).UpdateTemplate();
		}));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowContentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowContent", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarGroupContentPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, OnShowContentPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActualContentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActualContent", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarGroupContentPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnActualContentPropertyChanged));

		public object ActualContent
		{
			get
			{
				return GetValue(ActualContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ActualContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShowContent
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShowContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate ItemsDataTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(ItemsDataTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsDataTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DataTemplate ContentDataTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (DataTemplate)GetValue(ContentDataTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ContentDataTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DisplaySource DisplaySource
		{
			get
			{
				return (DisplaySource)GetValue(DisplaySourceProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DisplaySourceProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected static void OnActualContentPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroupContentPresenter)d).OnActualContentChanged(e.OldValue);
		}

		protected static void OnShowContentPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroupContentPresenter)d).OnShowContentChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
		}

		private void UpdateTemplate()
		{
			base.ContentTemplate = null;
			base.ContentTemplate = ((DisplaySource == DisplaySource.Items) ? ItemsDataTemplate : ContentDataTemplate);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnShowContentChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			base.Content = (ShowContent ? ActualContent : null);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnActualContentChanged(object oldValue)
		{
			if (ShowContent)
			{
				base.Content = ActualContent;
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ExplorerBarFirst", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ExplorerBarFirst0", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ExplorerBarMiddle", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ExplorerBarMiddle0", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ExplorerBarLast", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ExplorerBarLast0", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ExplorerBarSingle", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "ExplorerBarSingle0", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "IsActiveTrue", GroupName = "IsActiveStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "IsActiveFalse", GroupName = "IsActiveStates")]
	public class NavBarGroupHeader : Button
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty NavBarViewKindProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AnimationProgressProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupPositionProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsActiveProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowBorderProperty;

		private bool emulateClick;

		private TouchPoint point;

		private bool isMouseOver;

		private bool isMousePressed;

		public double AnimationProgress
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(AnimationProgressProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AnimationProgressProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public GroupPosition GroupPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return (GroupPosition)GetValue(GroupPositionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GroupPositionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public NavBarViewKind NavBarViewKind
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarViewKind)GetValue(NavBarViewKindProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(NavBarViewKindProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal bool IsActive
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsActiveProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsActiveProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShowBorder
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShowBorderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowBorderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected internal ImageAndTextContentPresenter GroupHeaderLabel { get; private set; }

		protected internal ExplorerBarExpandButton ExplorerBarExpandButton { get; private set; }

		protected internal NavPaneExpandButton NavPaneExpandButton { get; private set; }

		static NavBarGroupHeader()
		{
			AnimationProgressProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("AnimationProgress", typeof(double), typeof(NavBarGroupHeader), new PropertyMetadata(0.0, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupHeader)d).OnAnimationProgressPropertyChanged();
			}));
			GroupPositionProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupPosition", typeof(GroupPosition), typeof(NavBarGroupHeader), new PropertyMetadata(GroupPosition.First, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupHeader)d).OnGroupPositionPropertyChanged();
			}));
			IsActiveProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsActive", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarGroupHeader), new PropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupHeader)d).OnIsActivePropertyChanged();
			}));
			NavBarViewKindProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavBarViewKind", typeof(NavBarViewKind), typeof(NavBarGroupHeader), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupHeader)d).OnNavBarViewKindPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ShowBorderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarGroupHeader), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupHeader)d).OnShowBorderChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			UIElement.IsManipulationEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarGroupHeader), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarGroupHeader), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		protected override void OnTouchDown(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTouchDown(e);
			point = e.GetTouchPoint(this);
			emulateClick = true;
		}

		protected override void OnTouchMove(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTouchMove(e);
			if (point == null || !object.Equals(e.GetTouchPoint(this).Position, point.Position))
			{
				emulateClick = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnTouchUp(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTouchUp(e);
			if (!emulateClick)
			{
				return;
			}
			point = null;
			emulateClick = false;
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				NavBarGroup group = base.DataContext as NavBarGroup;
				group.With((NavBarGroup x) => x.NavBar).Do(delegate(NavBarControl x)
				{
					x.SelectionStrategy.SelectGroup(group);
				});
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			Initialize();
		}

		public NavBarGroupHeader()
		{
			NavBarGroupHelper.SetIsGroupHeader(this, value: true);
		}

		protected internal virtual void Initialize()
		{
			ExplorerBarExpandButton = (ExplorerBarExpandButton)GetTemplateChild("explorerBarExpandButton");
			NavPaneExpandButton = (NavPaneExpandButton)GetTemplateChild("navPaneExpandButton");
			GroupHeaderLabel = (ImageAndTextContentPresenter)(object)GetTemplateChild("groupHeaderLabel");
			SetBindings();
			OnNavBarViewKindChanged();
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateVisualState()
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			switch (NavBarViewKind)
			{
			case NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar:
				text = "ExplorerBar";
				break;
			case NavBarViewKind.NavigationPane:
				text = "NavigationPane";
				break;
			case NavBarViewKind.SideBar:
				text = "SideBar";
				break;
			}
			if (ShowBorder)
			{
				if (GroupPosition == GroupPosition.First)
				{
					text += "First";
				}
				else if (GroupPosition == GroupPosition.Middle)
				{
					text += "Middle";
				}
				else if (GroupPosition == GroupPosition.Last)
				{
					text += "Last";
				}
				else if (GroupPosition == GroupPosition.Single)
				{
					text += "Single";
				}
				if (AnimationProgress == 0.0)
				{
					text += "0";
				}
			}
			else
			{
				text += "NoBorder";
			}
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, text, useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void OnAnimationProgressPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void OnGroupPositionPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowBorderChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void OnNavBarViewKindPropertyChanged()
		{
			OnNavBarViewKindChanged();
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			isMouseOver = true;
			UpdateCommonVisualState();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
			isMouseOver = false;
			UpdateCommonVisualState();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			isMousePressed = true;
			UpdateCommonVisualState();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonUp(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonUp(e);
			isMousePressed = false;
			UpdateCommonVisualState();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateCommonVisualState()
		{
			if (FrameworkElementExtensions.GetTemplatedParent((FrameworkElement)this) is NavPaneActiveGroupControl && base.DataContext is NavBarGroup && ((NavBarGroup)base.DataContext).NavBar != null && ((NavBarGroup)base.DataContext).NavBar.View is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "NormalHeader", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else if (!base.IsEnabled)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "DisabledHeader", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else if (isMousePressed)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "PressedHeader", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else if (isMouseOver)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "MouseOverHeader", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "NormalHeader", useTransitions: false);
			}
		}

		internal void SetBindings()
		{
			if (ExplorerBarExpandButton != null && NavPaneExpandButton != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, NavBarViewKindProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.NavBarViewKind"));
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, ShowBorderProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.ShowBorder"));
			}
			SetBinding(GroupPositionProperty, new Binding("(0)")
			{
				Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarPositionPanel.GroupPositionProperty)
			});
			SetBinding(IsActiveProperty, new Binding("IsActive"));
		}

		protected internal void OnIsActivePropertyChanged()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, IsActive ? "IsActiveTrue" : "IsActiveFalse", useTransitions: false);
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnNavBarViewKindChanged()
		{
			if (base.DataContext is NavBarGroup && ExplorerBarExpandButton != null && NavPaneExpandButton != null && (base.DataContext as NavBarGroup).NavBar != null)
			{
				ExplorerBarExpandButton.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Visibility.Collapsed);
				NavPaneExpandButton.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Visibility.Collapsed);
				switch (NavBarViewKind)
				{
				case NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar:
					ExplorerBarExpandButton.SetValue(UIElement.VisibilityProperty, Visibility.Visible);
					((UIElement)(object)GroupHeaderLabel).Opacity = 1.0;
					break;
				case NavBarViewKind.NavigationPane:
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(NavPaneExpandButton, UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.IsExpandButtonVisible")
					{
						Converter = new BooleanToVisibilityConverter()
					});
					BindingOperations.SetBinding((DependencyObject)(object)GroupHeaderLabel, UIElement.OpacityProperty, new Binding
					{
						Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarAnimationOptions.AnimationProgressProperty)
					});
					break;
				case NavBarViewKind.SideBar:
					((UIElement)(object)GroupHeaderLabel).Opacity = 1.0;
					break;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			Size result = base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
			DependencyObject templatedParent = FrameworkElementExtensions.GetTemplatedParent((FrameworkElement)this);
			if (templatedParent is NavBarGroupControl)
			{
				((NavBarGroupControl)templatedParent).MinDesiredSize = new Size(double.IsNaN(base.Width) ? result.Width : base.Width, double.IsNaN(base.Height) ? result.Height : base.Height);
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class NavBarGroupItemsControl : ItemsControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ViewKindProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowViewBorderProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsSourceCoreProperty;

		private NavBarControl navBar;

		public INotifyCollectionChanged ItemsSourceCore
		{
			get
			{
				return (INotifyCollectionChanged)GetValue(ItemsSourceCoreProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsSourceCoreProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public NavBarViewKind ViewKind
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarViewKind)GetValue(ViewKindProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ViewKindProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShowViewBorder
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShowViewBorderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowViewBorderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private NavBarControl NavBar
		{
			get
			{
				if (navBar == null)
				{
					navBar = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<NavBarControl>((DependencyObject)this);
				}
				return navBar;
			}
		}

		static NavBarGroupItemsControl()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavBarGroupItemsControl>();
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarGroupItemsControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			ItemsSourceCoreProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemsSourceCore", typeof(INotifyCollectionChanged), typeof(NavBarGroupItemsControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnItemsSourceCorePropertyChanged));
			ViewKindProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ViewKind", typeof(NavBarViewKind), typeof(NavBarGroupItemsControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupItemsControl)d).UpdateBorderState();
			}));
			ShowViewBorderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowViewBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarGroupItemsControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroupItemsControl)d).UpdateBorderState();
			}));
		}

		protected static void OnItemsSourceCorePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroupItemsControl)d).ItemsSource = ((NavBarGroupItemsControl)d).ItemsSourceCore as IEnumerable;
		}

		public NavBarGroupItemsControl()
		{
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
		}

		private void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateBorderState();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateBorderState()
		{
			if (!ShowViewBorder)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Hidden", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, ViewKind.ToString(), useTransitions: false);
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, ItemsSourceCoreProperty, new Binding("Groups"));
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, ShowViewBorderProperty, new Binding("View.ShowBorder"));
		}

		protected override void PrepareContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.PrepareContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			if (element is NavBarGroupControl)
			{
				((NavBarGroupControl)element).NavBar = NavBar;
			}
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(element, UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new Binding("IsVisible")
			{
				Source = item,
				Converter = new BooleanToVisibilityConverter()
			});
		}

		protected override void ClearContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.ClearContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			(element as NavBarGroupControl).Do(delegate(NavBarGroupControl x)
			{
				x.NavBar = null;
			});
		}

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			NavBarGroupControl navBarGroupControl = new NavBarGroupControl();
			navBarGroupControl.SetValue(NavigationPaneView.ElementProperty, GetValue(NavigationPaneView.ElementProperty));
			return navBarGroupControl;
		}
	}
	public static class NavBarCommands
	{
		private static readonly RoutedCommand changeGroupExpanded = new RoutedCommand("ChangeGroupExpanded", typeof(NavBarCommands));

		private static readonly RoutedCommand setActiveGroup = new RoutedCommand("SetActiveGroup", typeof(NavBarCommands));

		private static readonly RoutedCommand selectItem = new RoutedCommand("SelectItem", typeof(NavBarCommands));

		[Description("Toggles the specified group’s expanded state.")]
		public static RoutedCommand ChangeGroupExpanded => changeGroupExpanded;

		[Description("Activates the specified group.")]
		public static RoutedCommand SetActiveGroup => setActiveGroup;

		[Description("Selects the specified item.")]
		public static RoutedCommand SelectItem => selectItem;
	}
	public static class Categories

		public const string Templates = "Templates";

		public const string OptionsView = "OptionsView";
	}
	[LicenseProvider(typeof(DX_WPF_LicenseProvider))]
	[DXToolboxBrowsable]

	public class NavBarControl : Control, IEventArgsConverterSource, IWeakEventListener, ISupportInitialize, INavigatorClient, INotifyPropertyChanged, IExpandableChild
	{
		private class EventArgsConverter : IDataRowEventArgsConverter
		{
			private readonly NavBarControl navBar;

			public EventArgsConverter(NavBarControl navBar)
			{
				this.navBar = navBar;
			}

			object IDataRowEventArgsConverter.GetDataRow(RoutedEventArgs e)
			{
				if (navBar == null || navBar.View == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				NavBarItem navBarItem = navBar.View.GetNavBarItem(e);
				if (navBarItem == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return navBarItem.SourceObject ?? ((FrameworkContentElement)(object)navBarItem).DataContext;
			}
		}

		bool INavigatorClient.AcceptsCompactNavigation
		{
			get
			{
				if (View is NavigationPaneView)
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

			}
		}

		{
			get
			{
				return compact;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != compact)
				{
					SetCompact(value);
					compact = value;
					RaisePropertyChanged("Compact");
				}
			}
		}

		internal event RequestContainersEventHandler RequestContainers;

		event PropertyChangedEventHandler INotifyPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged
		{
			add
			{
				propertyChanged = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(propertyChanged, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				propertyChanged = (PropertyChangedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(propertyChanged, value);
			}
		}

		event ValueChangedEventHandler<bool> IExpandableChild.IsExpandedChanged
		{
			add
			{
				isExpandedChanged = (ValueChangedEventHandler<bool>)(object)Delegate.Combine((Delegate)(object)isExpandedChanged, (Delegate)(object)value);
			}
			remove
			{
				isExpandedChanged = (ValueChangedEventHandler<bool>)(object)Delegate.Remove((Delegate)(object)isExpandedChanged, (Delegate)(object)value);
			}
		}

		protected internal void GetContainers(out List<NavBarGroupControl> groups, out List<NavBarItemControl> items)
		{
			if (this.RequestContainers == null)
			{
				groups = new List<NavBarGroupControl>();
				items = new List<NavBarItemControl>();
				return;
			}
			RequestContainersEventArgs e = new RequestContainersEventArgs();
			this.RequestContainers(this, e);
			groups = e.Groups;
			items = e.Items;
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemsSourceChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			(e.OldValue as INotifyCollectionChanged).Do(delegate(INotifyCollectionChanged x)
			{
				CollectionChangedEventManager.RemoveListener(x, this);
			});
			(e.NewValue as INotifyCollectionChanged).Do(delegate(INotifyCollectionChanged x)
			{
				CollectionChangedEventManager.AddListener(x, this);
			});
			(e.OldValue as IBindingList).Do(delegate(IBindingList x)
			{
				ListChangedEventManager.RemoveListener(x, (IWeakEventListener)this);
			});
			(e.NewValue as IBindingList).Do(delegate(IBindingList x)
			{
				ListChangedEventManager.AddListener(x, (IWeakEventListener)this);
			});
			UpdateActualItemsSource();
		}

		protected virtual void OnActualItemsSourceChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.Lock();
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarControl, NavBarGroup>.OnItemsSourcePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty, ItemTemplateProperty, ItemTemplateSelectorProperty, ItemStyleProperty, (Func<NavBarControl, IList>)((NavBarControl navBarControl) => navBarControl.Groups), (Func<NavBarControl, NavBarGroup>)((NavBarControl navBarControl) => new NavBarGroup()), (Action<int, object>)null, (Action<NavBarGroup>)delegate
			{
			}, (ISupportInitialize)null, (Action<NavBarGroup, object>)LinkGroup, true, true, (Func<NavBarGroup, bool>)null, false);
			SelectionStrategy.Unlock();
		}

		protected virtual void LinkGroup(NavBarGroup group, object source)
		{
			group.SourceObject = source;
			group.NavBar = this;
			if (source is CollectionViewGroup)
			{
				source = ((CollectionViewGroup)source).Name;
			}
			if (View != null)
			{
				View.RaiseGroupAdding(group, source);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupDescriptionChanged()
		{
			UpdateActualItemsSourceAsync();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateActualItemsSourceAsync()
		{
			if (GetBindingExpression(GroupDescriptionProperty) == null || GetBindingExpression(GroupDescriptionProperty).Status == BindingStatus.Active)
			{
				UpdateActualItemsSource();
				return;
			}
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				UpdateActualItemsSource();
			});
		}

		private void UpdateActualItemsSource()
		{
			try
			{
				if (ItemsSource == null)
				{
					ActualItemsSource = null;
					return;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				if (adapder != null)
				{
					((ObservableCollectionConverter<object, object>)adapder).Source = null;
				}
				adapder = null;
			}
			ICollectionView collectionView = null;
			ListCollectionView listCollectionView = null;
			BindingListCollectionView bindingListCollectionView = null;
			if (ItemsSource is ICollectionView)
			{
				collectionView = ItemsSource as ICollectionView;
			}
			else if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(GroupDescription))
			{
				if (ItemsSource is IBindingList)
				{
					bindingListCollectionView = new BindingListCollectionView(ItemsSource as IBindingList);
					bindingListCollectionView.GroupDescriptions.Add(new PropertyGroupDescription(GroupDescription));
					ActualItemsSource = bindingListCollectionView.Groups;
					return;
				}
				adapder = new ItemSourceAdapter
				{
					Source = ItemsSource,
					Selector = (object x) => x,
					GroupDescription = GroupDescription
				};
				listCollectionView = new ListCollectionView((IList)adapder);
				listCollectionView.GroupDescriptions.Add(new PropertyGroupDescription(GroupDescription));
				ActualItemsSource = listCollectionView.Groups;
				return;
			}
			if (collectionView != null)
			{
				ActualItemsSource = ((collectionView.GroupDescriptions.Count == 1) ? collectionView.Groups : null);
			}
			else if (ItemsSource != null)
			{
				IEnumerable<object> source = ItemsSource as IEnumerable<object>;
				ActualItemsSource = source.ToList();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemTemplateChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.Lock();
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarControl, NavBarGroup>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
			SelectionStrategy.Unlock();
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemStyleChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.Lock();
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarControl, NavBarGroup>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
			SelectionStrategy.Unlock();
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemTemplateSelectorChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.Lock();
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarControl, NavBarGroup>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
			SelectionStrategy.Unlock();
		}

		static NavBarControl()
		{
			NavBarControlStrategyRegistrator.RegisterNavBarControlStrategy();
			About.CheckLicenseShowNagScreen(typeof(NavBarControl));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			ViewProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("View", typeof(NavBarViewBase), typeof(NavBarControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnViewChanged(d, e);
			}));
			ActiveGroupProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActiveGroup", typeof(NavBarGroup), typeof(NavBarControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnActiveGroupChanged, CoerceActiveGroup));
			UIElement.IsEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, OnIsEnabledChanged));
			ItemsSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemsSource", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(NavBarControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnItemsSourceChanged(e);
			}));
			GroupDescriptionProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupDescription", typeof(string), typeof(NavBarControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(string.Empty, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnGroupDescriptionChanged();
			}));
			ItemTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavBarControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnItemTemplateSelectorChanged(e);
			}));
			ItemStyleProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(NavBarControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnItemStyleChanged(e);
			}));
			ItemTemplateProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ItemTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnItemTemplateChanged(e);
			}));
			ActualItemsSourceProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ActualItemsSource", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(NavBarControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnActualItemsSourceChanged(e);
			}));
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("ItemsAttachedBehavior", typeof(ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarControl, NavBarGroup>), typeof(NavBarControl), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			SelectedGroupProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("SelectedGroup", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.BindsTwoWayByDefault, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnSelectedGroupChanged(e.OldValue);
			}));
			SelectedItemsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("SelectedItems", typeof(IEnumerable), typeof(NavBarControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnSelectedItemsChanged((IEnumerable)e.OldValue);
			}));
			SelectedItemProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("SelectedItem", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.BindsTwoWayByDefault, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarControl)d).OnSelectedItemChanged(e.OldValue);
			}, (DependencyObject d, object v) => ((NavBarControl)d).OnSelectedItemChanging(v)));
			AllowSelectItemProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("AllowSelectItem", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, OnAllowSelectItemPropertyChanged));
			AllowSelectDisabledItemProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("AllowSelectDisabledItem", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarControl), new PropertyMetadata(false, OnAllowSelectDisabledItemPropertyChanged));
			EachGroupHasSelectedItemProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("EachGroupHasSelectedItem", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarControl), new PropertyMetadata(false, OnEachGroupHasSelectedItemPropertyChanged));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavBarControl>();
		}

		public static NavBarViewBase CreateDefaultView()
		{
			return new ExplorerBarView();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			SetCompact(compact);
		}

		private void OnViewChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OldValue != null)
			{
				if (e.OldValue is NavigationPaneView)
				{
					((NavigationPaneView)e.OldValue).IsExpanded = true;
				}
				RemoveChild(e.OldValue);
				((NavBarViewBase)e.OldValue).SetNavBar(null);
			}
			if (e.NewValue != null)
			{
				NavBarViewBase navBarViewBase = (NavBarViewBase)e.NewValue;
				navBarViewBase.SetNavBar((NavBarControl)d);
			}
			else
			{
				View = CreateDefaultView();
			}
			if (View != null)
			{
				AddChild(View);
			}
			if (ActiveGroup != null)
			{
				ActiveGroup.UpdateScrollMode();
			}
			UpdateGroups(delegate(NavBarGroup group)
			{
				group.UpdateUseCustomIsVisible();
			});
			if (View != null)
			{
				View.UpdateViewForce();
			}
		}

		private static void OnActiveGroupChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarControl)d).OnActiveGroupChanged((NavBarGroup)e.OldValue);
		}

		private static object CoerceActiveGroup(DependencyObject d, object value)
		{
			return ((NavBarControl)d).CoerceActiveGroup((NavBarGroup)value);
		}

		private static void OnAllowSelectItemPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarControl)d).SelectionStrategy.UpdateSelectionSettings();
		}

		private static void OnAllowSelectDisabledItemPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarControl)d).SelectionStrategy.UpdateSelectionSettings();
		}

		private static void OnEachGroupHasSelectedItemPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarControl)d).SelectionStrategy.UpdateSelectionSettings();
		}

		private static void OnIsEnabledChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarControl)d).OnIsEnabledChanged();
		}

		private void CheckAcceptCompactNavigation()
		{
			if (View is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				RaisePropertyChanged("AcceptCompactNavigation");
			}
		}

		public NavBarControl()
		{
			SelectionStrategy = new SelectionStrategy(this);
			SelectionStrategy.UpdateSelectionSettings();
			groups = new NavBarGroupCollection(this);
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
			SelectedItems = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<NavBarItem>(new ObservableCollection<NavBarItem>());
			eventArgsConverter = new EventArgsConverter(this);
			SetLocalBinding();
			compact = true;
		}

		public void ReloadGroups()
		{
			UpdateActualItemsSource();
		}

		void ISupportInitialize.BeginInit()
		{
			SelectionStrategy.Lock();
			base.BeginInit();
		}

		void ISupportInitialize.EndInit()
		{
			base.EndInit();
			SelectionStrategy.Unlock();
		}

		protected override void OnInitialized(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnInitialized(e);
			if (View == null)
			{
				View = CreateDefaultView();
			}
			UpdateGroups(delegate(NavBarGroup group)
			{
				group.isNavBarInitialized = true;
			});
		}

		private void SetLocalBinding()
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding("IsEnabled")
			{
				Source = this,
				Converter = new EnabledToOpacityConverter()
			};
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, UIElement.OpacityProperty, binding);
		}

		private void OnActiveGroupChanged(NavBarGroup prevGroup)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.SelectGroup(ActiveGroup);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedGroupChanged(object oldValue)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.SelectGroup(SelectedGroup);
			RaisePropertyChanged("SelectedItem");
		}

		private object OnSelectedItemChanging(object value)
		{
			return SelectionStrategy.CoerceSelection(value);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedItemChanged(object oldValue)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.SelectItem(SelectedItem);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedItemsChanged(IEnumerable oldValue)
		{
			SelectionStrategy.SelectItems(SelectedItems);
		}

		private void OnIsEnabledChanged()
		{
			foreach (NavBarGroup group in Groups)
			{
				((DependencyObject)(object)group).CoerceValue(ContentElement.IsEnabledProperty);
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual bool CanActivateGroup(NavBarGroup newGroup)
		{
			return CoerceActiveGroup(newGroup) == newGroup;
		}

		private NavBarGroup CoerceActiveGroup(NavBarGroup newGroup)
		{
			return CoerceActiveGroupCore(newGroup, ActiveGroup);
		}

		private NavBarGroup CoerceActiveGroupCore(NavBarGroup newGroup, NavBarGroup oldGroup)
		{
			if (Groups.Count == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (newGroup == null || !newGroup.ActualIsVisible || Groups.IndexOf(newGroup) == -1 || AnimationInProgress())
			{
				return oldGroup;
			}
			if (View != null && oldGroup != newGroup && SelectionStrategy.AllowGroupChangingEvent && !RaiseActiveGroupChangingEvent(oldGroup, newGroup))
			{
				return oldGroup;
			}
			return newGroup;
		}

		protected internal virtual bool RaiseActiveGroupChangingEvent(NavBarGroup oldGroup, NavBarGroup newGroup)
		{
			NavBarActiveGroupChangingEventArgs e = new NavBarActiveGroupChangingEventArgs(oldGroup, newGroup);
			e.RoutedEvent = NavBarViewBase.ActiveGroupChangingEvent;
			View.RaiseEvent(e);
			return !e.Cancel;
		}

		protected virtual void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (View != null)
			{
				View.UpdateView();
				UpdateGroups(delegate(NavBarGroup group)
				{
					group.isNavBarInitialized = true;
				});
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateGroups(Action<NavBarGroup> action)
		{
			foreach (NavBarGroup group in Groups)
			{
				action(group);
			}
		}

		internal bool AnimationInProgress()
		{
			foreach (NavBarGroup group in Groups)
			{
				if (group.IsCollapsing || group.IsExpanding)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		bool IWeakEventListener.ReceiveWeakEvent(Type managerType, object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (ActualItemsSource == null || (ItemsSource is ICollectionView && ((ICollectionView)ItemsSource).GroupDescriptions.Count != 1) || string.IsNullOrEmpty(GroupDescription))
			{
				UpdateActualItemsSource();
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void SetCompact(bool hide)
		{
			if (View != null && View is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				((NavigationPaneView)View).IsOverflowPanelVisibleInternal = hide;
				((NavigationPaneView)View).IsSplitterVisibleInternal = hide;
				((NavigationPaneView)View).IsCompact = !hide;
			}
		}

		protected internal void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{
			if (propertyChanged != null)
			{
				propertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		protected virtual double GetCollapseWidth()
		{
			if (!(View is NavigationPaneView))
			{
				return double.NaN;
			}
			if (((NavigationPaneView)View).Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return 36.0 + base.Margin.Left + base.Margin.Right;
			}
			return 36.0 + base.Margin.Top + base.Margin.Bottom;
		}

		protected internal virtual void RaiseIsExpandedChanged(bool oldValue, bool newValue)
		{
			if (isExpandedChanged != null)
			{
				isExpandedChanged.Invoke((object)this, new ValueChangedEventArgs<bool>(oldValue, newValue));
			}
		}

		internal void AddChild(object child)
		{
			AddLogicalChild(child);
		}

		internal void RemoveChild(object child)
		{
			RemoveLogicalChild(child);
		}
	}
	public class ConstantHelper
	{
		public const int InvalidIndex = -1;
	}
	internal delegate void RequestContainersEventHandler(object sender, RequestContainersEventArgs args);
	internal class RequestContainersEventArgs : EventArgs
	{
		public List<NavBarItemControl> Items { get; private set; }

		public List<NavBarGroupControl> Groups { get; private set; }

		public RequestContainersEventArgs()
		{
			Items = new List<NavBarItemControl>();
			Groups = new List<NavBarGroupControl>();
		}

		public void AddContainer(INavBarContainer container)
		{
			if (container is NavBarItemControl)
			{
				Items.Add(container as NavBarItemControl);
			}
			if (container is NavBarGroupControl)
			{
				Groups.Add(container as NavBarGroupControl);
			}
		}
	}
	internal interface INavBarContainer
	{
		RequestContainersWeakEventHandler RequestContainerHandler { get; }
	}
	internal static class INavBarContainerHelper
	{
		public static void OnNavBarChanged(this INavBarContainer container, NavBarControl oldValue, NavBarControl newValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				oldValue.RequestContainers -= ((WeakEventHandler<INavBarContainer, RequestContainersEventArgs, RequestContainersEventHandler>)container.RequestContainerHandler).Handler;
			}
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				newValue.RequestContainers += ((WeakEventHandler<INavBarContainer, RequestContainersEventArgs, RequestContainersEventHandler>)container.RequestContainerHandler).Handler;
			}
		}
	}
	internal class RequestContainersWeakEventHandler : WeakEventHandler<INavBarContainer, RequestContainersEventArgs, RequestContainersEventHandler>
	{
		private static Action<WeakEventHandler<INavBarContainer, RequestContainersEventArgs, RequestContainersEventHandler>, object> onDetachAction = delegate(WeakEventHandler<INavBarContainer, RequestContainersEventArgs, RequestContainersEventHandler> h, object o)
		{
			((NavBarControl)o).RequestContainers -= h.Handler;
		};

		private static Func<WeakEventHandler<INavBarContainer, RequestContainersEventArgs, RequestContainersEventHandler>, RequestContainersEventHandler> createHandlerFunction = (WeakEventHandler<INavBarContainer, RequestContainersEventArgs, RequestContainersEventHandler> h) => h.OnEvent;

		public RequestContainersWeakEventHandler(INavBarContainer owner, Action<INavBarContainer, object, RequestContainersEventArgs> onEventAction)
			: base(owner, onEventAction, onDetachAction, createHandlerFunction)
		{
		}
	}
	public enum DisplaySource
	{
		Items,
		Content
	}
	internal class ConditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector : DataTemplateSelector
	{
		private List<object> templatesAndSelectors;

		private Func<DataTemplate, bool> condition;

		public ConditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector(Func<DataTemplate, bool> condition)
		{
			templatesAndSelectors = new List<object>();
			this.condition = condition;
		}

		public override DataTemplate SelectTemplate(object item, DependencyObject container)
		{
			DataTemplate dataTemplate = null;
			foreach (object templatesAndSelector in templatesAndSelectors)
			{
				if (dataTemplate != null)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (templatesAndSelector is DataTemplate)
				{
					dataTemplate = ((DataTemplate)templatesAndSelector).If((DataTemplate x) => condition(x));
				}
				if (templatesAndSelector is DataTemplateSelector)
				{
					dataTemplate = ((DataTemplateSelector)templatesAndSelector).SelectTemplate(item, container).If((DataTemplate x) => condition(x));
				}
			}
			return dataTemplate ?? base.SelectTemplate(item, container);
		}

		public void Add(DataTemplate template, DataTemplateSelector selector)
		{
			if (selector != null)
			{
				templatesAndSelectors.Add(selector);
			}
			if (template != null)
			{
				templatesAndSelectors.Add(template);
			}
		}
	}
	public class ItemForegroundWrapper : Control
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty OwnerForegroundProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("OwnerForeground", typeof(Brush), typeof(ItemForegroundWrapper), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OwnerProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Owner", typeof(Control), typeof(ItemForegroundWrapper), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((ItemForegroundWrapper)d).OnOwnerChanged((Control)e.OldValue);
		}));

		public Control Owner
		{
			get
			{
				return (Control)GetValue(OwnerProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OwnerProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Brush OwnerForeground
		{
			get
			{
				return (Brush)GetValue(OwnerForegroundProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OwnerForegroundProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnOwnerChanged(Control oldValue)
		{
			ClearValue(OwnerForegroundProperty);
			if (Owner != null)
			{
				NavBarItemControl navBarItemControl = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<NavBarItemControl>((DependencyObject)this);
				if (navBarItemControl == null || navBarItemControl.Style == null || !navBarItemControl.Style.Setters.Any((SetterBase x) => x is Setter && ((Setter)x).Property == Control.ForegroundProperty))
				{
					Owner.SetBinding(Control.ForegroundProperty, new Binding("OwnerForeground")
					{
						Source = this,
						Mode = BindingMode.TwoWay
					});
				}
			}
		}
	}

		[Description("Gets the actual template selector that chooses a template for an item representing the group within the overflow panel, based on custom logic.\nThis is a dependency property.")]

		public NavBarGroupControl GroupControl
		{
			get
			{
				if (groupControlWR != null)
				{
					return groupControlWR.Target as NavBarGroupControl;
				}
				return null;
			}
			set
			{
				NavBarGroupControl groupControl = GroupControl;
				groupControlWR = new WeakReference(value);
				if (value != groupControl)
				{
					OnGroupControlChanged(groupControl);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal bool HasPeekFormTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				if (!PeekFormTemplate.ReturnSuccess())
				{
					return PeekFormTemplateSelector.ReturnSuccess();
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		[Description("Gets the collection of items within the group and provides indexed access to them.")]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Content)]
		[Category("Data")]
		public NavBarItemCollection Items => (NavBarItemCollection)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ItemsProperty);

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		internal bool IsNavPaneGroup
		{
			get
			{
				if (ActualIsVisible)
				{
					return NavigationPaneVisible;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		private EventHandler OnCanExecuteChangedEventHandler => onCanExecuteChangedEventHandler.Value;

		protected override IEnumerator LogicalChildren
		{
			get
			{
				List<object> list = new List<object>();
				list.AddRange(Items);
				if (Content != null)
				{
					list.Add(Content);
				}
				return list.GetEnumerator();
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual BarButtonItem MenuGroupItem
		{
			get
			{
				if (menuGroupItem == null)
				{
					menuGroupItem = CreateMenuGroupItem();
				}
				return menuGroupItem;
			}
		}

		protected internal bool IsOverflowGroup
		{
			get
			{
				return isItemsControlGroup;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != isItemsControlGroup)
				{
					bool flag = isItemsControlGroup;
					isItemsControlGroup = value;
					UpdateMenuGroupItem();
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal bool IsVisibleInOverflowPanel
		{
			get
			{
				return isVisibleInOverflowPanel;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != isVisibleInOverflowPanel)
				{
					bool flag = isVisibleInOverflowPanel;
					isVisibleInOverflowPanel = value;
					UpdateMenuGroupItem();
				}
			}
		}

		string INavigationItem.Header => Header.ToString();

		DataTemplate INavigationItem.PeekFormTemplate => GetActualPeekFormTemplate();

		public event EventHandler Activate;

		public event EventHandler Click;

		internal event EventHandler InternalIsActiveChanged
		{
			add
			{
				handlersInternalIsActiveChanged.Add(value);
			}
			remove
			{
				handlersInternalIsActiveChanged.Remove(value);
			}
		}

		private event PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChanged;

		event PropertyChangedEventHandler INotifyPropertyChanged.PropertyChanged
		{
			add
			{
				propertyChanged += value;
			}
			remove
			{
				propertyChanged -= value;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnItemsSourceChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateActualItemsSource();
			NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy).Do(delegate(SelectionStrategy x)
			{
				x.UpdateSelectedItem();
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnNavPaneShowModeChanged(ShowMode oldValue)
		{
			if (NavBar != null && NavBar.View is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				NavBar.View.With((NavBarViewBase x) => ((NavigationPaneView)x).Panel).With((NavigationPanePanel x) => x.ActiveGroup as NavPaneActiveGroupControl).With((NavPaneActiveGroupControl x) => x.CollapsedActiveGroupControl)
					.Do(delegate(CollapsedActiveGroupControl x)
					{
						x.UpdateShowMode();
					});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateActualItemsSource()
		{
			((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ActualItemsSourceProperty, (object)ItemsSource);
		}

		protected virtual void OnActualItemsSourceChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy).Do(delegate(SelectionStrategy x)
			{
				x.Lock();
			});
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarGroup, NavBarItem>.OnItemsSourcePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)(object)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty, EmptyItemTemplateProperty, AttachedItemTemplateSelectorProperty, ItemStyleProperty, (Func<NavBarGroup, IList>)((NavBarGroup navBarGroup) => navBarGroup.Items), (Func<NavBarGroup, NavBarItem>)((NavBarGroup navBarGroup) => new NavBarItem()), (Action<int, object>)null, (Action<NavBarItem>)delegate
			{
			}, (ISupportInitialize)null, (Action<NavBarItem, object>)LinkItem, true, true, (Func<NavBarItem, bool>)null, false);
			NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy).Do(delegate(SelectionStrategy x)
			{
				x.Unlock();
			});
		}

		protected virtual void LinkItem(NavBarItem item, object source)
		{
			item.SourceObject = source;
			NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.View).Do(delegate(NavBarViewBase x)
			{
				x.RaiseItemAdding(item, source);
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemStyleChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			RecreateItems(e);
		}

		private void RecreateItems(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy).Do(delegate(SelectionStrategy x)
			{
				x.Lock();
			});
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarGroup, NavBarItem>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)(object)this, e, ItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
			NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy).Do(delegate(SelectionStrategy x)
			{
				x.Unlock();
			});
		}

		static NavBarGroup()
		{
			NavBarControlStrategyRegistrator.RegisterNavBarGroupStrategy();
			ContentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Content", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).OnContentChanged(e.OldValue);
			}));
			CommandProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Command", typeof(ICommand), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).OnCommandChanged((ICommand)e.OldValue, (ICommand)e.NewValue);
			}));
			CommandParameterProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CommandParameter", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).UpdateCanExecute();
			}));
			CommandTargetProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CommandTarget", typeof(IInputElement), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).UpdateCanExecute();
			}));
			IsVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(true, OnIsVisibleChanged));
			TemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Template", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			HeaderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Header", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(string.Empty, OnHeaderPropertyChanged));
			HeaderTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("HeaderTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).UpdateActualHeaderTemplateSelector();
			}));
			ActualHeaderTemplateSelectorPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ActualHeaderTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			NavPaneGroupButtonTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavPaneGroupButtonTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).UpdateActualNavPaneGroupButtonTemplateSelector();
			}));
			NavPaneOverflowGroupTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavPaneOverflowGroupTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).UpdateActualNavPaneOverflowGroupTemplateSelector();
			}));
			ItemTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).UpdateSelectors();
			}));
			ItemTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).UpdateSelectors();
			}));
			ActualItemTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("ActualItemTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			IsExpandedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsExpanded", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).OnIsExpandedChanged();

			ItemsProperty = DependencyProperty.Register("Items", typeof(NavBarItemCollection), typeof(NavBarGroup), new PropertyMetadata(null, StartSycnronization));
			CollapsedNavPaneItemsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CollapsedNavPaneItems", typeof(ObservableCollection<NavBarItem>), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			CollapsedNavPaneItemsStyleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CollapsedNavPaneItemsStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)

			}));
			CollapsedNavPaneItemsTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CollapsedNavPaneItemsTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).OnCollapsedNavPaneItemsPropertiesChanged(e);
			}));
			CollapsedNavPaneItemsTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CollapsedNavPaneItemsTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
