			CollapsedNavPaneItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CollapsedNavPaneItemsAttachedBehavior", typeof(ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarGroup, NavBarItem>), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			CollapsedNavPaneSelectedItemProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.BindsTwoWayByDefault, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarGroup)(object)d).OnCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItemChanged(e.OldValue);
			}, CoerceCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem));
			PeekFormTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("PeekFormTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			PeekFormTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("PeekFormTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavBarGroup), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));

		private static object CoerceCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem(DependencyObject d, object baseValue)
		{
			if (((NavBarGroup)(object)d).NavBar == null || !(((NavBarGroup)(object)d).NavBar.View is NavigationPaneView) || !(((NavBarGroup)(object)d).NavBar.View as NavigationPaneView).CollapsedNavPaneSelectionStrategy.IsValidItem(baseValue))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return baseValue;
		}

		private static void OnHeaderPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroup)(object)d).RaisePropertyChanged("Header");
		}

		private void OnCollapsedNavPaneItemsPropertiesChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarGroup, NavBarItem>.OnItemsGeneratorTemplatePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)(object)this, e, CollapsedNavPaneItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty);
		}

		private void OnCollapsedNavPaneItemsSourceChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ItemsAttachedBehaviorCore<NavBarGroup, NavBarItem>.OnItemsSourcePropertyChanged((DependencyObject)(object)this, e, CollapsedNavPaneItemsAttachedBehaviorProperty, CollapsedNavPaneItemsTemplateProperty, CollapsedNavPaneItemsTemplateSelectorProperty, CollapsedNavPaneItemsStyleProperty, (Func<NavBarGroup, IList>)((NavBarGroup navBarGroup) => navBarGroup.CollapsedNavPaneItems), (Func<NavBarGroup, NavBarItem>)((NavBarGroup navBarGroup) => new NavBarItem()), (Action<int, object>)null, (Action<NavBarItem>)delegate
			{
			}, (ISupportInitialize)null, (Action<NavBarItem, object>)delegate(NavBarItem it, object obj)
			{
				it.SourceObject = obj;
				DependencyObjectExtensions.SetCurrentValueIfDefault((DependencyObject)(object)it, NavBarItem.ContentProperty, obj);
			}, true, true, (Func<NavBarItem, bool>)null, false);
		}

		private static void StartSycnronization(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroup)(object)d).Do(delegate(NavBarGroup x)
			{
				x.synchronizedItems = new SynchronizedItemCollection(x, x.Items);
			});
		}

		private static void OnIsVisibleChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarGroup)(object)d).CustomIsVisible = (bool)e.NewValue;
			((NavBarGroup)(object)d).UpdateActualIsVisible();
		}

		protected virtual void OnSourceObjectChanged()
		{
			if (sourceObject is CollectionViewGroup source)
			{
				((FrameworkContentElement)this).SetBinding(ItemsSourceProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("Items")
				{
					Source = source
				});
				((FrameworkContentElement)this).SetBinding(FrameworkContentElement.DataContextProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("Name")
				{
					Source = source
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItemChanged(object oldValue)
		{
			if (NavBar != null && NavBar.View is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				if (CollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem == null)
				{
					(NavBar.View as NavigationPaneView).CollapsedNavPaneSelectionStrategy.UnselectItem(this);
				}
				else
				{
					(NavBar.View as NavigationPaneView).CollapsedNavPaneSelectionStrategy.SelectItem(CollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem);
				}
				if (NavBar.ActiveGroup == this)
				{
					(NavBar.View as NavigationPaneView).ActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem = CollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem;
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemFontSettingsChanged(FontSettings oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualItemFontSettings();
		}

		protected virtual void OnFontSettingsChanged(FontSettings oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualFontSettings();
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemImageSettingsChanged(ImageSettings oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualItemImageSettings();
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemLayoutSettingsChanged(LayoutSettings oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualItemLayoutSettings();
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemDisplayModeChanged(DisplayMode oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualItemDisplayMode();
		}

		protected virtual void OnImageSettingsChanged(ImageSettings oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualImageSettings();
		}

		protected virtual void OnLayoutSettingsChanged(LayoutSettings oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualLayoutSettings();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDisplayModeChanged(DisplayMode oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualDisplayMode();
		}

		protected internal DataTemplate GetActualPeekFormTemplate()
		{
			return PeekFormTemplate ?? PeekFormTemplateSelector.With((DataTemplateSelector x) => x.SelectTemplate(this, GroupControl));
		}

		private object CoerceSelectedItem(NavBarItem navBarItem)
		{
			if (NavBar != null)
			{
				return NavBar.SelectionStrategy.CoerceSelection(this, navBarItem);
			}
			return navBarItem;
		}

		private object CoerceSelectedItemIndex(int newIndex)
		{
			if (NavBar != null)
			{
				return NavBar.SelectionStrategy.CoerceSelection(this, newIndex);
			}
			return newIndex;
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedItemIndexChanged(int oldIndex, int newIndex)
		{
			selectedItemIndex = newIndex;
			NavBar.SelectionStrategy.SelectItem(this, SelectedItemIndex);
		}

		protected virtual void OnSelectedItemChanged(NavBarItem oldItem, NavBarItem newItem)
		{
			if (NavBar != null)
			{
				selectedItem = newItem;
				NavBar.SelectionStrategy.SelectItem(SelectedItem, this);
				NavBar.SelectionStrategy.UpdateSelectedItemIndexByItem(this, SelectedItem);
			}
		}

		public NavBarGroup()
		{
			onCanExecuteChangedEventHandler = new Lazy<EventHandler>(() => OnCanExecuteChanged);
			((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ItemsProperty, (object)new NavBarItemCollection(this));
			CollapsedNavPaneItems = new ObservableCollection<NavBarItem>();
			CollapsedNavPaneItems.CollectionChanged += OnCollapsedNavPaneItemsCollectionChanged;
		}

		private void OnCollapsedNavPaneItemsCollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (NavBar != null && NavBar.View is NavigationPaneView)
			{
				(NavBar.View as NavigationPaneView).CollapsedNavPaneSelectionStrategy.UpdateSelectedItem(this);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnCommandChanged(ICommand oldCommand, ICommand newCommand)
		{
			if (oldCommand != null)
			{
				oldCommand.CanExecuteChanged -= OnCanExecuteChangedEventHandler;
			}
			if (newCommand != null)
			{
				newCommand.CanExecuteChanged += OnCanExecuteChangedEventHandler;
			}
			UpdateCanExecute();
		}

		private void OnActualVisualStyleChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnContentChanged(object oldValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null)
			{
				RemoveChild(oldValue);
			}
			if (Content != null)
			{
				AddChild(Content);
			}
		}

		internal void AddChild(object child)
		{
			((FrameworkContentElement)this).AddLogicalChild(child);
		}

		internal void RemoveChild(object child)
		{
			((FrameworkContentElement)this).RemoveLogicalChild(child);
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupControlChanged(NavBarGroupControl oldValue)
		{
		}

		private void OnGroupHeaderTemplateChanged()
		{
			UpdateActualGroupHeaderTemplate();
		}

		protected virtual void OnActualIsVisibleChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			if (NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy).With((SelectionStrategy x) => x.SelectedGroup) == this && !ActualIsVisible)
			{
				NavBar.SelectionStrategy.SelectFirstVisibleGroup(ifExists: true);
			}
			UpdateNavPaneGroups();
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualIsVisible()
		{
			ActualIsVisible = (UseCustomIsVisible ? CustomIsVisible : IsVisible);
		}

		private void UpdateNavPaneGroups()
		{
			if (NavBar != null && NavBar.View != null)
			{
				NavBar.View.UpdateViewForce();
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateActualVisualStyle()
		{
			ActualVisualStyle = ((VisualStyle == null && NavBar != null && NavBar.View != null) ? NavBar.View.GroupVisualStyle : VisualStyle);
		}

		internal void UpdateUseCustomIsVisible()
		{
			UseCustomIsVisible = NavBar != null && NavBar.View is NavigationPaneView;
		}

		internal void UpdateScrollMode()
		{
			if (NavBar != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding((DependencyObject)(object)this, ViewScrollModeProperty, new Binding("(0)")
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(ScrollingSettings.ScrollModeProperty),
					Source = NavBar.View
				});
			}
		}

		internal void UpdateActualItemVisualStyle()
		{
			UpdateItems(delegate(NavBarItem w)
			{
				w.UpdateActualVisualStyle();
			});
		}

		internal void ChangeGroupExpanded()
		{
			IsExpanded = !IsExpanded;
		}

		private void OnIsExpandedChanged()
		{
			if (NavBar != null && NavBar.View != null)
			{
				NavBar.View.RaiseEvent(new NavBarGroupExpandedChangedEventArgs(this, IsExpanded)
				{
					RoutedEvent = ExplorerBarView.GroupExpandedChangedEvent
				});
			}
		}

		private void OnCanExecuteChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateCanExecute();
		}

		private bool CoerceIsExpanded(bool newValue)
		{
			return CoerceIsExpandedCore(newValue, IsExpanded);
		}

		private bool CoerceIsExpandedCore(bool newValue, bool oldValue)
		{
			if (NavBar != null && NavBar.View != null && oldValue != newValue)
			{
				NavBarGroupExpandedChangingEventArgs e = new NavBarGroupExpandedChangingEventArgs(this, newValue);
				e.RoutedEvent = ExplorerBarView.GroupExpandedChangingEvent;
				NavBar.View.RaiseEvent(e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					return oldValue;
				}
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private void OnNavBarChanged(NavBarControl oldControl, NavBarControl newControl)
		{
			UpdateActualHeaderTemplateSelector();
			UpdateSelectors();
			UpdateActualNavPaneGroupButtonTemplateSelector();
			UpdateActualNavPaneOverflowGroupTemplateSelector();
			UpdateActualVisualStyle();
			UpdateActualGroupHeaderTemplate();
			UpdateActualItemVisualStyle();
			UpdateScrollMode();
			UpdateUseCustomIsVisible();
			UpdateActualAnimateGroupExpansion();
			UpdateActualItemDisplayMode();
			UpdateActualItemImageSettings();
			UpdateActualItemLayoutSettings();
			UpdateActualItemFontSettings();
			UpdateActualLayoutSettings();
			UpdateActualDisplayMode();
			UpdateActualImageSettings();
			UpdateActualFontSettings();
			oldControl?.RemoveChild(this);
			newControl?.AddChild(this);
		}

		private void OnScrollModeChanged()
		{
			ActualScrollMode = GroupScrollMode ?? ViewScrollMode;
		}

		private void UpdateCanExecute()
		{
			IsEnabled = CanExecute();
		}

		private bool CanExecute()
		{
			if (Command == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (Command is RoutedCommand)
			{
				if (CommandTarget == null)
				{
					CommandTarget = NavBar;
				}
				return ((RoutedCommand)Command).CanExecute(CommandParameter, CommandTarget);
			}
			return Command.CanExecute(CommandParameter);
		}

		internal void UpdateActualHeaderTemplateSelector()
		{
			ActualHeaderTemplateSelector = GetActualTemplateSelector(HeaderTemplateProperty, HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty, NavBarViewBase.HeaderTemplateProperty, NavBarViewBase.HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty);
		}

		internal void UpdateActualNavPaneGroupButtonTemplateSelector()
		{
			ActualNavPaneGroupButtonTemplateSelector = GetActualTemplateSelector(NavPaneGroupButtonTemplateProperty, NavPaneGroupButtonTemplateSelectorProperty, NavigationPaneView.GroupButtonTemplateProperty, NavigationPaneView.GroupButtonTemplateSelectorProperty);
		}

		internal void UpdateActualNavPaneOverflowGroupTemplateSelector()
		{
			ActualNavPaneOverflowGroupTemplateSelector = GetActualTemplateSelector(NavPaneOverflowGroupTemplateProperty, NavPaneOverflowGroupTemplateSelectorProperty, NavigationPaneView.OverflowGroupTemplateProperty, NavigationPaneView.OverflowGroupTemplateSelectorProperty);
		}

		internal void UpdateSelectors()
		{
			ConditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector conditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector = new ConditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector(delegate(DataTemplate t)
			{
				bool? flag = NavBarItemsAttachedBehaviorTemplateConsistencyChecker<NavBarItem>.CheckTemplate(t);
				return !flag.HasValue || flag.Value;
			});
			ConditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector conditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector2 = new ConditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector(delegate(DataTemplate t)
			{
				bool? flag = NavBarItemsAttachedBehaviorTemplateConsistencyChecker<NavBarItem>.CheckTemplate(t);
				return !flag.HasValue || !flag.Value;
			});
			DataTemplate template = NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.View).With((NavBarViewBase x) => x.ItemTemplate);
			DataTemplateSelector selector = NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.View).With((NavBarViewBase x) => x.ItemTemplateSelector);
			conditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector.Add(ItemTemplate, ItemTemplateSelector);
			conditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector.Add(template, selector);
			conditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector2.Add(ItemTemplate, ItemTemplateSelector);
			conditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector2.Add(template, selector);
			((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(AttachedItemTemplateSelectorProperty, (object)conditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector);
			((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ActualItemTemplateSelectorProperty, (object)conditionalPrioritizedDataTemplateSelector2);
		}

		internal void UpdateActualGroupHeaderTemplate()
		{
			ActualGroupHeaderTemplate = ((GroupHeaderTemplate == null && NavBar != null && NavBar.View != null) ? NavBar.View.GroupHeaderTemplate : GroupHeaderTemplate);
		}

		internal void UpdateActualAnimateGroupExpansion()
		{
			AnimateGroupExpansion = NavBar == null || NavBar.View == null || NavBar.View.AnimateGroupExpansion;
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualItemImageSettings()
		{
			UpdateItems(delegate(NavBarItem item)
			{
				item.UpdateActualImageSettings();
			});
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualItemLayoutSettings()

			UpdateItems(delegate(NavBarItem item)
			{
				item.UpdateActualLayoutSettings();
			});
		}

				item.UpdateActualDisplayMode();
			});
		}

				item.UpdateActualFontSettings();
			});
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualDisplayMode()
		{
			if (DisplayMode != DisplayMode.Default)
			{
				ActualDisplayMode = DisplayMode;
			}
			else if (NavBar == null || NavBar.View == null || NavBar.View.GroupDisplayMode == DisplayMode.Default)
			{
				ActualDisplayMode = DisplayMode.ImageAndText;
			}
			else
			{
				ActualDisplayMode = NavBar.View.GroupDisplayMode;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualLayoutSettings()
		{
			if (LayoutSettings != null)
			{
				ActualLayoutSettings = LayoutSettings;
			}
			else if (NavBar == null || NavBar.View == null || NavBar.View.GroupLayoutSettings == null)
			{
				ActualLayoutSettings = LayoutSettings.Default;
			}
			else
			{
				ActualLayoutSettings = NavBar.View.GroupLayoutSettings;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualImageSettings()
		{
			if (ImageSettings != null)
			{
				ActualImageSettings = ImageSettings;
			}
			else if (NavBar == null || NavBar.View == null || NavBar.View.GroupImageSettings == null)
			{
				ActualImageSettings = ImageSettings.GroupDefault;
			}
			else
			{
				ActualImageSettings = NavBar.View.GroupImageSettings;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualFontSettings()
		{
			if (FontSettings != null)
			{
				ActualFontSettings = FontSettings;
			}
			else if (NavBar == null || NavBar.View == null || NavBar.View.GroupFontSettings == null)
			{
				ActualFontSettings = FontSettings.Default;
			}
			else
			{
				ActualFontSettings = NavBar.View.GroupFontSettings;
			}
		}

		internal void RaiseActivateEvent()
		{
			if (this.Activate != null)
			{
				this.Activate(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		internal void RaiseClickEvent()
		{
			if (!((ContentElement)(object)this).IsEnabledCore)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (this.Click != null)
			{
				this.Click(this, new EventArgs());
			}
			if (((INavigatorClient)(object)NavBar).Return((INavigatorClient x) => !x.IsAttached, () => true) && Command != null)
			{
				if (Command is RoutedCommand)
				{
					((RoutedCommand)Command).Execute(CommandParameter, CommandTarget);
				}
				else
				{
					Command.Execute(CommandParameter);
				}
			}
		}

		private DataTemplateSelector GetActualTemplateSelector(DependencyProperty groupTemplateProperty, DependencyProperty groupTemplateSelectorProperty, DependencyProperty viewTemplateProperty, DependencyProperty viewTemplateSelectorProperty)
		{
			//IL_009d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a3: Expected O, but got Unknown
			DataTemplate dataTemplate = (DataTemplate)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(groupTemplateProperty);
			DataTemplateSelector dataTemplateSelector = (DataTemplateSelector)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(groupTemplateSelectorProperty);
			DataTemplate dataTemplate2 = ((NavBar != null && NavBar.View != null) ? ((DataTemplate)NavBar.View.GetValue(viewTemplateProperty)) : null);
			DataTemplateSelector dataTemplateSelector2 = ((NavBar != null && NavBar.View != null) ? ((DataTemplateSelector)NavBar.View.GetValue(viewTemplateSelectorProperty)) : null);
			bool flag = dataTemplate != null || dataTemplateSelector != null;
			DataTemplate dataTemplate3 = (flag ? dataTemplate : dataTemplate2);
			DataTemplateSelector dataTemplateSelector3 = (flag ? dataTemplateSelector : dataTemplateSelector2);
			return (DataTemplateSelector)new ActualTemplateSelectorWrapper(dataTemplateSelector3, dataTemplate3);
		}

		private void UpdateItems(Action<NavBarItem> action)
		{
			foreach (NavBarItem synchronizedItem in SynchronizedItems)
			{
				action(synchronizedItem);
			}
		}

		private void RaiseInternalIsActiveChanged(EventArgs args)
		{
			foreach (EventHandler item in handlersInternalIsActiveChanged)
			{
				item(this, args);
			}
		}

		protected virtual BarButtonItem CreateMenuGroupItem()
		{
			//IL_000e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0018: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0041: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			BarButtonItem bItem = new BarButtonItem();
			((BarItem)bItem).GlyphSize = (GlyphSize)1;
			BindingOperations.SetBinding((DependencyObject)(object)bItem, BarItem.ContentProperty, new Binding("Header")
			{
				Source = this,
				Converter = (IValueConverter)new ObjectToStringConverter()
			});
			BindingOperations.SetBinding((DependencyObject)(object)bItem, BarItem.IsVisibleProperty, new Binding("ActualIsVisible")
			{
				Source = this
			});
			DelegateCommand showCommand = DelegateCommandFactory.Create(delegate
			{
				NavBar.SelectionStrategy.SelectGroup(this);
			}, () => true, useCommandManager: false);
			((BarItem)bItem).Command = new DelegateCommand(delegate
			{
				showCommand.Execute(((BarItem)bItem).CommandParameter);
				if (Command != null)
				{
					Command.Execute(CommandParameter);
				}
			});
			return bItem;
		}

		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateMenuGroupItemGlyph()
		{
			((BarItem)MenuGroupItem).Glyph = ImageSourceInNavPaneMenu ?? ImageSource;
		}

		protected void RaisePropertyChanged(string propertyName)
		{

			{
				this.propertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}

		[SpecialName]
		object INavigationItem.get_DataContext()

		}
	}
	internal class NavBarItemsAttachedBehaviorTemplateConsistencyChecker<T> where T : class
	{
		private static Delegate getChildTypeFromChildIndexDelegate;

		private static Delegate GetChildTypeFromChildIndexDelegate
		{
			get
			{
				if ((object)getChildTypeFromChildIndexDelegate == null)
				{
					getChildTypeFromChildIndexDelegate = CreateGetter(typeof(DataTemplate), "ChildTypeFromChildIndex", BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
				}
				return getChildTypeFromChildIndexDelegate;
			}
		}

		private static Delegate CreateGetter(Type entityType, string propertyName, BindingFlags bindingFlags)
		{
			PropertyInfo property = entityType.GetProperty(propertyName, bindingFlags);
			ParameterExpression parameterExpression = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Parameter(property.DeclaringType);
			MemberExpression body = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Property(parameterExpression, propertyName);
			LambdaExpression lambdaExpression = System.Linq.Expressions.Expression.Lambda(body, parameterExpression);
			return lambdaExpression.Compile();
		}

		private static Dictionary<int, Type> GetChildTypeFromChildIndex(DataTemplate template)
		{
			return GetChildTypeFromChildIndexDelegate.DynamicInvoke(template) as Dictionary<int, Type>;
		}

		public static bool? CheckTemplate(DataTemplate template)
		{
			if (template == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			Dictionary<int, Type> childTypeFromChildIndex = GetChildTypeFromChildIndex(template);
			int count = childTypeFromChildIndex.Count;
			if (count < 1)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (typeof(T).IsAssignableFrom(childTypeFromChildIndex[1]))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (count < 2)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if ((typeof(ContentControl).IsAssignableFrom(childTypeFromChildIndex[1]) || typeof(ContentPresenter).IsAssignableFrom(childTypeFromChildIndex[1])) && typeof(T).IsAssignableFrom(childTypeFromChildIndex[2]))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}

		static NavBarItem()
		{
			SourceContentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("SourceContent", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				SetCurrentValueIfDefault(d, ContentProperty, e.NewValue);
			}));
			ContentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Content", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarItem), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItem)(object)d).OnContentPropertyChanged(e);
			}));
			IsVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarItem), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			TemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Template", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarItem), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			IsSelectedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsSelected", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItem)(object)d).If((NavBarItem x) => x.IsSelected).With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group).With((NavBarGroup x) => x.NavBar)
					.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy)
					.Do(delegate(SelectionStrategy x)
					{
						x.SelectItem(d);
					});
			}));
			ImageSourceProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ImageSource", typeof(ImageSource), typeof(NavBarItem), new PropertyMetadata(null, null, (DependencyObject d, object value) => CoerceImageSource(d, (ImageSource)value)));
			GroupPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("Group", typeof(NavBarGroup), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnGroupChanged));
			GroupProperty = GroupPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			CommandProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Command", typeof(ICommand), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnCommandPropertyChanged));
			CommandParameterProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CommandParameter", typeof(object), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItem)(object)d).UpdateCanExecute();
			}));
			CommandTargetProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CommandTarget", typeof(IInputElement), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItem)(object)d).UpdateCanExecute();
			}));
			VisualStyleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("VisualStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(NavBarItem), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnVisualStyleChanged));
			ActualVisualStylePropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ActualVisualStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItem)(object)d).OnActualVisualStyleChanged(d, e);
			}));
			ActualVisualStyleProperty = ActualVisualStylePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			DisplayModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DisplayMode", typeof(DisplayMode), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DisplayMode.Default, OnDisplayModePropertyChanged));
			LayoutSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("LayoutSettings", typeof(LayoutSettings), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnLayoutSettingsPropertyChanged));
			ImageSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ImageSettings", typeof(ImageSettings), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnImageSettingsPropertyChanged));
			ActualDisplayModePropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ActualDisplayMode", typeof(DisplayMode), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DisplayMode.Default));
			ActualLayoutSettingsPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ActualLayoutSettings", typeof(LayoutSettings), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ActualImageSettingsPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ActualImageSettings", typeof(ImageSettings), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			FontSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("FontSettings", typeof(FontSettings), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnFontSettingsPropertyChanged));
			ActualFontSettingsPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ActualFontSettings", typeof(FontSettings), typeof(NavBarItem), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ActualFontSettingsProperty = ActualFontSettingsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			ActualDisplayModeProperty = ActualDisplayModePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			ActualLayoutSettingsProperty = ActualLayoutSettingsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			ActualImageSettingsProperty = ActualImageSettingsPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
		}

		private static void SetCurrentValueIfDefault(DependencyObject dObj, DependencyProperty dp, object value)
		{
			if (DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(dObj, dp).BaseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Default)
			{
				dObj.SetCurrentValue(dp, value);
			}
		}

		private void OnContentPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OldValue != null)
			{
				((FrameworkContentElement)this).RemoveLogicalChild(e.OldValue);
			}
			if (e.NewValue != null)
			{
				((FrameworkContentElement)this).AddLogicalChild(e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnGroupChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			NavBarItem item = (NavBarItem)(object)d;
			item.UpdateActualVisualStyle();
			item.UpdateActualLayoutSettings();
			item.UpdateActualDisplayMode();
			item.UpdateActualImageSettings();
			item.UpdateActualFontSettings();
			NavBarGroup navBarGroup = e.OldValue as NavBarGroup;
			NavBarGroup navBarGroup2 = e.NewValue as NavBarGroup;
			navBarGroup?.RemoveChild(item);
			if (navBarGroup2 == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			navBarGroup2.AddChild(item);
			if (item.IsSelected)
			{
				navBarGroup2.NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy).Do(delegate(SelectionStrategy x)
				{
					x.SelectItem(item);
				});
			}
		}

		private static void OnCommandPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarItem)(object)d).OnCommandChanged((ICommand)e.OldValue, (ICommand)e.NewValue);
		}

		private static void OnVisualStyleChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarItem)(object)d).UpdateActualVisualStyle();
		}

		protected static void OnDisplayModePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarItem)(object)d).OnDisplayModeChanged((DisplayMode)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected static void OnLayoutSettingsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarItem)(object)d).OnLayoutSettingsChanged((LayoutSettings)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected static void OnImageSettingsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarItem)(object)d).OnImageSettingsChanged((ImageSettings)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected static void OnFontSettingsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarItem)(object)d).OnFontSettingsChanged((FontSettings)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnFontSettingsChanged(FontSettings oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualFontSettings();
		}

		protected virtual void OnImageSettingsChanged(ImageSettings oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualImageSettings();
		}

		protected virtual void OnLayoutSettingsChanged(LayoutSettings oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualLayoutSettings();
		}

		protected virtual void OnDisplayModeChanged(DisplayMode oldValue)
		{
			UpdateActualDisplayMode();
		}

		protected virtual void OnCommandChanged(ICommand oldCommand, ICommand newCommand)
		{
			if (oldCommand != null)
			{
				oldCommand.CanExecuteChanged -= onCanExecuteChangedEventHandler;
				onCanExecuteChangedEventHandler = null;
			}
			if (newCommand != null)
			{
				onCanExecuteChangedEventHandler = OnCanExecuteChanged;
				newCommand.CanExecuteChanged += onCanExecuteChangedEventHandler;
			}
			UpdateCanExecute();
		}

		public static ImageSource CoerceImageSource(DependencyObject dObj, ImageSource imageSource)
		{
			if (imageSource == null || imageSource.IsFrozen)
			{
				return imageSource;
			}
			if (imageSource is BitmapImage bitmapImage && imageSource is IUriContext uriContext && bitmapImage.UriSource != null && !bitmapImage.UriSource.IsAbsoluteUri && uriContext.BaseUri == null)
			{
				uriContext.BaseUri = BaseUriHelper.GetBaseUri(dObj);
			}
			if (imageSource.CanFreeze)
			{
				imageSource = imageSource.GetAsFrozen() as ImageSource;
			}
			return imageSource;
		}

		private void OnCanExecuteChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateCanExecute();
		}

		private void UpdateCanExecute()
		{
			if (Command != null)
			{
				IInputElement target = CommandTarget ?? ((Group == null) ? null : Group.NavBar);
				IsEnabled = ((Command is RoutedCommand) ? ((RoutedCommand)Command).CanExecute(CommandParameter, target) : Command.CanExecute(CommandParameter));
			}
		}

		private void OnActualVisualStyleChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		internal void UpdateActualVisualStyle()
		{
			if (VisualStyle != null)
			{
				ActualVisualStyle = VisualStyle;
			}
			else if (Group != null && Group.ItemVisualStyle != null)
			{
				ActualVisualStyle = Group.ItemVisualStyle;
			}
			else if (Group != null && Group.NavBar != null && Group.NavBar.View != null)
			{
				if (Group.NavBar.View is NavigationPaneView { IsPopupOpened: not false } navigationPaneView)
				{
					ActualVisualStyle = navigationPaneView.ItemVisualStyleInPopup;
				}
				else
				{
					ActualVisualStyle = Group.NavBar.View.ItemVisualStyle;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ActualVisualStyle = VisualStyle;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualDisplayMode()
		{
			if (DisplayMode != DisplayMode.Default)
			{
				ActualDisplayMode = DisplayMode;
			}
			else if (Group == null)
			{
				ActualDisplayMode = DisplayMode.ImageAndText;
			}
			else if (Group.ItemDisplayMode != DisplayMode.Default)
			{
				ActualDisplayMode = Group.ItemDisplayMode;
			}
			else if (Group.NavBar == null || Group.NavBar.View == null || Group.NavBar.View.ItemDisplayMode == DisplayMode.Default)
			{
				ActualDisplayMode = DisplayMode.ImageAndText;
			}
			else
			{
				ActualDisplayMode = Group.NavBar.View.ItemDisplayMode;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualLayoutSettings()
		{
			if (LayoutSettings != null)
			{
				ActualLayoutSettings = LayoutSettings;
			}
			else if (Group == null)
			{
				ActualLayoutSettings = LayoutSettings.Default;
			}
			else if (Group.ItemLayoutSettings != null)
			{
				ActualLayoutSettings = Group.ItemLayoutSettings;
			}
			else if (Group.NavBar == null || Group.NavBar.View == null || Group.NavBar.View.ItemLayoutSettings == null)
			{
				ActualLayoutSettings = LayoutSettings.Default;
			}
			else
			{
				ActualLayoutSettings = Group.NavBar.View.ItemLayoutSettings;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualImageSettings()
		{
			if (ImageSettings != null)
			{
				ActualImageSettings = ImageSettings;
			}
			else if (Group == null)
			{
				ActualImageSettings = ImageSettings.ItemDefault;
			}
			else if (Group.ItemImageSettings != null)
			{
				ActualImageSettings = Group.ItemImageSettings;
			}
			else if (Group.NavBar == null || Group.NavBar.View == null || Group.NavBar.View.ItemImageSettings == null)
			{
				ActualImageSettings = ImageSettings.ItemDefault;
			}
			else
			{
				ActualImageSettings = Group.NavBar.View.ItemImageSettings;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateActualFontSettings()
		{
			if (FontSettings != null)
			{
				ActualFontSettings = FontSettings;
			}
			else if (Group == null)
			{
				ActualFontSettings = FontSettings.Default;
			}
			else if (Group.ItemFontSettings != null)
			{
				ActualFontSettings = Group.ItemFontSettings;
			}
			else if (Group.NavBar == null || Group.NavBar.View == null || Group.NavBar.View.ItemFontSettings == null)
			{
				ActualFontSettings = FontSettings.Default;
			}
			else
			{
				ActualFontSettings = Group.NavBar.View.ItemFontSettings;
			}
		}

		internal void RaiseClickEvent()
		{
			if (Group != null && Group.NavBar != null && Group.NavBar.AllowSelectItem && (IsEnabled || Group.NavBar.AllowSelectDisabledItem))
			{
				Group.SelectedItem = this;
			}
			if (IsEnabled)
			{
				if (this.Click != null)
				{
					this.Click(this, new EventArgs());
				}
				ExecuteCommand();
			}
		}

		internal void RaiseSelectEvent()
		{
			if (this.Select != null)
			{
				this.Select(this, new EventArgs());
			}
		}

		private void ExecuteCommand()
		{
			if (Command != null)
			{
				if (Command is RoutedCommand routedCommand)
				{
					routedCommand.Execute(CommandParameter, CommandTarget);
				}
				else
				{
					Command.Execute(CommandParameter);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class ImageAndTextContentPresenter : XPFContentPresenter
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContextElementProperty;

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(OrientationProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public object ContextElement
		{
			get
			{
				return ((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ContextElementProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ContextElementProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public double ImageFallbackSize { get; set; }

		static ImageAndTextContentPresenter()
		{
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(ImageAndTextContentPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Orientation.Horizontal, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure));
			ContextElementProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ContextElement", typeof(object), typeof(ImageAndTextContentPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((FrameworkElement)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			SetLayoutSettingsBinding();
			SetImageSettingsBinding();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateTransform(Size size)
		{
			UIElement child = GetChild();
			if (child != null)
			{
				if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
				{
					((UIElement)this).RenderTransform = new RotateTransform();
					return;
				}
				TransformGroup transformGroup = new TransformGroup();
				transformGroup.Children.Add(new RotateTransform
				{
					Angle = -90.0
				});
				transformGroup.Children.Add(new TranslateTransform
				{
					Y = size.Width
				});
				((UIElement)this).RenderTransform = transformGroup;
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			UIElement child = GetChild();
			if (child == null)
			{
				return Size.Empty;
			}
			child.Measure(GetCorrectSize(availableSize));
			return GetCorrectSize(child.DesiredSize);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			UIElement child = GetChild();
			if (child == null)
			{
				return Size.Empty;
			}
			Size correctSize = GetCorrectSize(finalSize);
			child.Arrange(new Rect(0.0, 0.0, correctSize.Width, correctSize.Height));
			UpdateTransform(correctSize);
			return GetCorrectSize(correctSize);
		}

		private Size GetCorrectSize(Size size)
		{
			if (Orientation != Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				return new Size(size.Height, size.Width);
			}
			return size;
		}

		private UIElement GetChild()
		{
			if (VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount((DependencyObject)(object)this) == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return VisualTreeHelper.GetChild((DependencyObject)(object)this, 0) as UIElement;
		}

		private void SetImageSettingsBinding()
		{
		}

		private void SetLayoutSettingsBinding()
		{
		}
	}

		internal Image Image { get; set; }

		internal FrameworkElement Content { get; set; }

		static ImageAndTextDecorator()
		{
			DisplayModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DisplayMode", typeof(DisplayMode), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ImageAndTextDecorator)d).OnDisplayModeChanged();
			}));
			ImageDockingProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ImageDocking", typeof(Dock), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), new PropertyMetadata(Dock.Left, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ImageAndTextDecorator)d).OnImageDockingChanged();
			}));
			ContentSourceProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ContentSource", typeof(object), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			ContentStyleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ContentStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			ImageSourceProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ImageSource", typeof(ImageSource), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			ImageStyleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ImageStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			ImageFallbackSizeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ImageFallbackSize", typeof(double), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), new PropertyMetadata(0.0));
			DisplayModeTextMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DisplayModeTextMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DisplayModeImageMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DisplayModeImageMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DockingTopContentMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DockingTopContentMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DockingTopImageMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DockingTopImageMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DockingBottomContentMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DockingBottomContentMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DockingBottomImageMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DockingBottomImageMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DockingRightContentMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DockingRightContentMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DockingRightImageMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DockingRightImageMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DockingLeftContentMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DockingLeftContentMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DockingLeftImageMarginProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DockingLeftImageMargin", typeof(Thickness), typeof(ImageAndTextDecorator), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Thickness)));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<ImageAndTextDecorator>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			SetPropertiesBinding();
			Initialize();
			UpdateContentByDisplayMode();
			UpdateImageTransform();
		}

		private void UpdateImageTransform()
		{
			if (Image != null)
			{
				_ = Image.Visibility;
				_ = 2;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual void Initialize()
		{
			Grid = GetTemplateChild("grid") as Grid;
			Image = GetTemplateChild("PART_Image") as Image;
			Content = GetTemplateChild("PART_Content") as FrameworkElement;
			SetBindings();
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateContentByDisplayMode()
		{
			switch (DisplayMode)
			{
			case DisplayMode.ImageAndText:
				if (Image.Source != null)
				{
					Image.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				}
				Content.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				break;
			case DisplayMode.Image:
				if (Image.Source != null)
				{
					Image.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
					Grid.SetColumnSpan(Image, 2);
				}
				Content.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				Image.Margin = DisplayModeImageMargin;
				return;
			case DisplayMode.Text:
				Content.Visibility = Visibility.Visible;
				Image.Visibility = Visibility.Collapsed;
				Content.Margin = DisplayModeTextMargin;
				Grid.SetColumnSpan(Content, 2);
				return;
			}
			UpdateImageDocking();
		}

		protected internal virtual void UpdateImageDocking()
		{
			if (Grid == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Grid.ColumnDefinitions[0].Width = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Auto);
			Grid.ColumnDefinitions[1].Width = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Star);
			ClearValue();
			switch (ImageDocking)
			{
			case Dock.Bottom:
				if (Image != null)
				{
					Grid.SetRow(Image, 1);
					Grid.SetColumnSpan(Image, 2);
					Image.Margin = DockingBottomImageMargin;
				}
				if (Content != null)
				{
					Grid.SetColumn(Content, 0);
					Grid.SetRow(Content, 0);
					Grid.SetColumnSpan(Content, 2);
					Content.Margin = DockingBottomContentMargin;
				}
				break;
			case Dock.Left:
				if (Image != null)
				{
					Grid.SetColumn(Image, 0);
					Image.Margin = DockingLeftImageMargin;
				}
				if (Content != null)
				{
					Grid.SetColumn(Content, 1);
					Content.Margin = DockingLeftContentMargin;
				}
				Grid.ColumnDefinitions[0].Width = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Auto);
				Grid.ColumnDefinitions[1].Width = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Star);
				break;
			case Dock.Right:
				Grid.ColumnDefinitions[0].Width = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Star);
				Grid.ColumnDefinitions[1].Width = new GridLength(1.0, GridUnitType.Auto);
				if (Image != null)
				{
					Grid.SetColumn(Image, 1);
					Image.Margin = DockingRightImageMargin;
				}
				if (Content != null)
				{
					Grid.SetColumn(Content, 0);
					Content.Margin = DockingRightContentMargin;
				}
				break;
			case Dock.Top:
				if (Image != null)
				{
					Grid.SetRow(Image, 0);
					Grid.SetColumn(Image, 0);
					Grid.SetColumnSpan(Image, 2);
					Image.Margin = DockingTopImageMargin;
				}
				if (Content != null)
				{
					Grid.SetColumn(Content, 0);
					Grid.SetRow(Content, 1);
					Grid.SetColumnSpan(Content, 2);
					Content.Margin = DockingTopContentMargin;
				}
				break;
			}
		}

		private void ClearValue()
		{
			if (Image != null)
			{
				Image.ClearValue(Grid.RowProperty);
				Image.ClearValue(Grid.RowSpanProperty);
				Image.ClearValue(Grid.ColumnProperty);
				Image.ClearValue(Grid.ColumnSpanProperty);
			}
			if (Content != null)
			{
				Content.ClearValue(Grid.RowProperty);
				Content.ClearValue(Grid.RowSpanProperty);
				Content.ClearValue(Grid.ColumnProperty);
				Content.ClearValue(Grid.ColumnSpanProperty);
			}
		}

		private void OnDisplayModeChanged()
		{
			UpdateContentByDisplayMode();
		}

		private void OnImageDockingChanged()
		{
			UpdateImageDocking();
		}

		private void SetPropertiesBinding()
		{
			Image image = DependencyObjectExtensions.GetElementByName((DependencyObject)this, "PART_Image") as Image;
			Control content = DependencyObjectExtensions.GetElementByName((DependencyObject)this, "PART_Content") as Control;
			SettingsProvider.SetPropertiesBinding(image, ImageFallbackSize, content, FrameworkElementExtensions.GetTemplatedParent((FrameworkElement)this));
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			if (FrameworkElementExtensions.GetTemplatedParent((FrameworkElement)this) != null)
			{
				if (Image != null)
				{
					BindingOperations.SetBinding(Image, UIElement.VisibilityProperty, new Binding("Source")
					{
						Source = Image,
						Converter = new ImageSourceToVisibilityConverter(),
						ConverterParameter = DisplayMode
					});
				}
				MultiBinding multiBinding = new MultiBinding();
				multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new Binding("(0)")
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarViewBase.DisplayModeProperty),
					RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.TemplatedParent)
				});
				if (this is NavPaneImageAndTextDecorator)
				{
					multiBinding.Bindings.Add(new Binding
					{
						Path = new PropertyPath(NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider.IsCompleteCollapsedProperty)
					});
				}
				multiBinding.Converter = new CollapsedNavPaneDisplayModeConverter();
				multiBinding.ConverterParameter = this;
				SetBinding(DisplayModeProperty, multiBinding);
				SetBinding(NavBarViewBase.ImageSettingsProperty, new Binding("(0)")
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarViewBase.ImageSettingsProperty),
					RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.TemplatedParent)
				});
				SetBinding(NavBarViewBase.LayoutSettingsProperty, new Binding("(0)")
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarViewBase.LayoutSettingsProperty),
					RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.TemplatedParent)
				});
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneImageAndTextDecorator : ImageAndTextDecorator
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ContentOpacityProperty;

		public Thickness GroupButtonCollapsedImageMargin { get; set; }

		public double ContentOpacity
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(ContentOpacityProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ContentOpacityProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavPaneImageAndTextDecorator()
		{
			ContentOpacityProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ContentOpacity", typeof(double), typeof(NavPaneImageAndTextDecorator), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneImageAndTextDecorator)d).OnContentOpacityChanged();
			}));
		}

		public NavPaneImageAndTextDecorator()
		{
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
		}

		protected internal override void Initialize()
		{
			base.Initialize();
			if (DependencyObjectExtensions.GetElementByName((DependencyObject)this, "PART_Content") is ContentControl contentControl)
			{
				SetBinding(ContentOpacityProperty, new Binding("Opacity")
				{
					Source = contentControl
				});
				contentControl.SetBinding(UIElement.OpacityProperty, new Binding("(0)")
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarAnimationOptions.AnimationProgressProperty)
				});
			}
		}

		protected internal override void UpdateContentByDisplayMode()
		{
			if (ContentOpacity != 0.0)
			{
				base.UpdateContentByDisplayMode();
			}
		}

		protected internal override void UpdateImageDocking()
		{
			if (ContentOpacity != 0.0)
			{
				base.UpdateImageDocking();
			}
		}

		private void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateContentLayout();
		}

		private void OnContentOpacityChanged()
		{
			UpdateContentLayout();
		}

		private void UpdateContentLayout()
		{
			if (ContentOpacity == 0.0)
			{
				if (base.Image != null)
				{
					Grid.SetColumnSpan(base.Image, 2);
					Grid.SetColumn(base.Image, 0);
					base.Image.HorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Center;
					base.Image.Margin = GroupButtonCollapsedImageMargin;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				UpdateContentByDisplayMode();
			}
		}
	}
	public class ImageSourceToVisibilityConverter : IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (parameter != null && (DisplayMode)parameter == DisplayMode.Text)
			{
				return Visibility.Collapsed;
			}
			return (value == null) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	public class NavBarItemCollection : ObservableCollection<object>
	{
		private readonly NavBarGroup item;

		public object this[string name]
		{
			get
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < base.Count; i++)
				{
					if (base[i] is NavBarItem && ((FrameworkContentElement)(object)(NavBarItem)base[i]).Name == name)
					{
						return base[i];
					}
					if (base[i] is FrameworkElement && ((FrameworkElement)base[i]).Name == name)
					{
						return base[i];
					}
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public NavBarItemCollection(NavBarGroup item)
		{
			this.item = item;
		}
	}
	public class SynchronizedItemCollection : ObservableCollection<NavBarItem>
	{
		private readonly NavBarGroup group;

		private readonly NavBarItemCollection source;

		public SynchronizedItemCollection(NavBarGroup group, NavBarItemCollection source)
		{
			this.group = group;
			this.source = source;
			ConnectToSource();
		}

		private void ConnectToSource()
		{
			if (source == null)
			{
				Clear();
				return;
			}
			SyncCollectionHelper.PopulateCore(this, source, CreateNavBarItemIfNeeded);
			source.CollectionChanged += OnSourceCollectionChanged;
		}

		private void OnSourceCollectionChanged(object sender, NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			SyncCollectionHelper.SyncCollection(e, this, source, CreateNavBarItemIfNeeded);
		}

		private object CreateNavBarItemIfNeeded(object sourceObject)
		{
			if (sourceObject is NavBarItem)
			{
				return sourceObject;
			}
			NavBarItem navBarItem = new NavBarItem
			{
				SourceObject = sourceObject,
				Content = sourceObject
			};
			if (!(sourceObject is FrameworkElement))
			{
				((FrameworkContentElement)(object)navBarItem).DataContext = sourceObject;
			}
			return navBarItem;
		}

		protected override void OnCollectionChanged(NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnCollectionChanged(e);
			if (e.OldItems != null)
			{
				foreach (NavBarItem element in e.OldItems)
				{
					group.NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy).Do(delegate(SelectionStrategy x)
					{
						x.OnItemRemoved(group, element);
					});
					element.Group = null;
				}
			}
			if (e.OldItems == null && group.NavBar.With((NavBarControl x) => x.SelectionStrategy).With((SelectionStrategy x) => x.LastSelectedItem) != null && object.Equals(group.NavBar.SelectionStrategy.LastSelectedItem, e.NewItems[0]) && group.NavBar.SelectionStrategy.SelectedItem == null)
			{
				(e.NewItems[0] as NavBarItem).IsSelected = true;
				group.NavBar.SelectionStrategy.LastSelectedItem = null;
			}
			IEnumerable<NavBarItem> enumerable = ((e.NewItems == null) ? null : e.NewItems.OfType<NavBarItem>().AsEnumerable());
			if (e.Action == NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset && e.NewItems == null)
			{
				enumerable = this.AsEnumerable();
			}
			if (enumerable == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (NavBarItem item in enumerable)
			{
				if (item.Group != null && item.Group != group)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(NavBarLocalizer.GetString(NavBarStringId.ItemIsAlreadyAddedToAnotherGroupException));
				}
				item.Group = group;
			}
		}

		protected override void ClearItems()
		{
			while (base.Count != 0)
			{
				RemoveAt(0);
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Normal", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "MouseOverState", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "PressedState", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "EnabledState", GroupName = "DisabledStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "DisabledState", GroupName = "DisabledStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Unselected", GroupName = "SelectedStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Selected", GroupName = "SelectedStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Vertical", GroupName = "ViewOrientationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Horizontal", GroupName = "ViewOrientationStates")]
	public class NavBarItemControl : ButtonBase, INavBarContainer
	{
		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsEnabledCoreProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsSelectedCoreProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsMouseOverCoreProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsPanelOrientationProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty ViewOrientationProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DisplayModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty LayoutSettingsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ImageSettingsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty FontSettingsProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NavBarProperty;

		private PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChangedEventHandler;

		private readonly RequestContainersWeakEventHandler requestContainerHandler;

		internal new bool IsEnabledCore
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsEnabledCoreProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsEnabledCoreProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal bool IsMouseOverCore
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsMouseOverCoreProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsMouseOverCoreProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal bool IsSelectedCore
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsSelectedCoreProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsSelectedCoreProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Orientation ItemsPanelOrientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(ItemsPanelOrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsPanelOrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Orientation ViewOrientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(ViewOrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ViewOrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ImageSettings ImageSettings
		{
			get
			{
				return (ImageSettings)GetValue(ImageSettingsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ImageSettingsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public LayoutSettings LayoutSettings
		{
			get
			{
				return (LayoutSettings)GetValue(LayoutSettingsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(LayoutSettingsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public DisplayMode DisplayMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (DisplayMode)GetValue(DisplayModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(DisplayModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public FontSettings FontSettings
		{
			get
			{
				return (FontSettings)GetValue(FontSettingsProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(FontSettingsProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public NavBarControl NavBar
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarControl)GetValue(NavBarProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(NavBarProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected internal object Source { get; set; }

		protected ContentControl BorderControl { get; private set; }

		RequestContainersWeakEventHandler INavBarContainer.RequestContainerHandler => requestContainerHandler;

		public NavBarItemControl()
		{
			requestContainerHandler = new RequestContainersWeakEventHandler(this, delegate(INavBarContainer container, object sender, RequestContainersEventArgs args)
			{
				args.AddContainer(container);
			});
			base.IsEnabledChanged += OnIsEnabledChanged;
		}

		static NavBarItemControl()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavBarItemControl>();
			IsEnabledCoreProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsEnabledCore", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarItemControl), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItemControl)d).OnIsEnabledCorePropertyChanged();
			}));
			IsSelectedCoreProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsSelectedCore", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarItemControl), new PropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItemControl)d).OnIsSelectedCorePropertyChanged();
			}));
			IsMouseOverCoreProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsMouseOverCore", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarItemControl), new PropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItemControl)d).OnIsMouseOverCorePropertyChanged();
			}));
			ItemsPanelOrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsPanelOrientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavBarItemControl), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItemControl)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ViewOrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ViewOrientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavBarItemControl), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItemControl)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			DisplayModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DisplayMode", typeof(DisplayMode), typeof(NavBarItemControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DisplayMode.Default));
			LayoutSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("LayoutSettings", typeof(LayoutSettings), typeof(NavBarItemControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			ImageSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ImageSettings", typeof(ImageSettings), typeof(NavBarItemControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			FontSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("FontSettings", typeof(FontSettings), typeof(NavBarItemControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnFontSettingsPropertyChanged));
			NavBarProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavBar", typeof(NavBarControl), typeof(NavBarItemControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarItemControl)d).OnNavBarChanged((NavBarControl)e.OldValue);
			}));
			NavigationAutomationPeersCreator.Default.RegisterObject(typeof(NavBarItemControl), typeof(NavBarItemControlAutomationPeer), (CreateObjectMethod<AutomationPeer>)((object owner) => (AutomationPeer)(object)new NavBarItemControlAutomationPeer((NavBarItemControl)owner)));
			TextElementHelper<NavBarItemControl>.OverrideMetadata((NavBarItemControl iControl) => (DependencyObject)(object)(iControl.DataContext as NavBarItem));
		}

		protected static void OnFontSettingsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarItemControl)d).OnFontSettingsChanged((FontSettings)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnFontSettingsChanged(FontSettings oldValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != null && propertyChangedEventHandler != null)
			{
				oldValue.WeakPropertyChanged -= propertyChangedEventHandler;
			}
			if (FontSettings != null)
			{
				propertyChangedEventHandler = OnFontSettingsPropertyChanged;
				FontSettings.WeakPropertyChanged += propertyChangedEventHandler;
				FontSettings.CheckAllProperties(this);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnNavBarChanged(NavBarControl oldValue)
		{
			this.OnNavBarChanged(oldValue, NavBar);
		}

		private void OnFontSettingsPropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			FontSettings.SetValueIfNotDefaultOrClearIfUnset(this, e.PropertyName);
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			BorderControl = GetTemplateChild("PART_Border") as ContentControl;
			NavBarVisualStateHelper.UpdateStates(this, "DisabledStates");
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				BorderControl.Do(delegate(ContentControl x)
				{
					NavBarVisualStateHelper.UpdateStates(x, "DisabledStates");
				});
			});
			SetBindings();
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsEnabledChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		protected override void OnIsPressedChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnIsPressedChanged(e);
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnMouseLeftButtonDown(e);
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		protected override void OnContentChanged(object oldContent, object newContent)
		{
			FrameworkElement frameworkElement = newContent as FrameworkElement;
			FrameworkContentElement frameworkContentElement = newContent as FrameworkContentElement;
			if ((frameworkElement != null && frameworkElement.Parent != null) || (frameworkContentElement != null && frameworkContentElement.Parent != null))
			{
				RemoveLogicalChild(oldContent);
			}
			else
			{
				base.OnContentChanged(oldContent, newContent);
			}
		}

		private bool GoToState(string stateName)
		{
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				if (BorderControl != null)
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(BorderControl, stateName, useTransitions: false);
				}
			});
			return VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, stateName, useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void OnIsEnabledCorePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void OnIsMouseOverCorePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void OnIsSelectedCorePropertyChanged()
		{
			GoToState(IsSelectedCore ? "Selected" : "Unselected");
		}

		private void OnOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(IsEnabledCoreProperty, new Binding("IsEnabled"));
			SetBinding(IsSelectedCoreProperty, new Binding("IsSelected"));
			SetBinding(IsMouseOverCoreProperty, new Binding("IsMouseOver")
			{
				Source = this
			});
			SetBinding(ItemsPanelOrientationProperty, new Binding("Group.NavBar.View.ItemsPanelOrientation"));
			SetBinding(ViewOrientationProperty, new Binding("Group.NavBar.View.Orientation"));
		}

		private void UpdateOrientationStates()
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			empty += ((ViewOrientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? "ViewVertical" : "ViewHorizontal");
			empty += ((ItemsPanelOrientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? "AndItemsPanelVertical" : "AndItemsPanelHorizontal");
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, empty, useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateVisualState()
		{
			if (!(base.DataContext is NavBarItem navBarItem))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!IsEnabledCore)
			{
				GoToState("DisabledState");
				if (!navBarItem.Group.NavBar.AllowSelectDisabledItem)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				GoToState("EnabledState");
			}
			if (base.IsPressed && !IsSelectedCore)
			{
				GoToState("PressedState");
			}
			else if (base.IsMouseOver)
			{
				GoToState("MouseOverState");
			}
			else if (!base.IsMouseOver && IsSelectedCore)
			{
				GoToState("Normal");
				GoToState("Unselected");
				GoToState("Selected");
			}
			else
			{
				GoToState("Normal");
			}
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return NavigationAutomationPeersCreator.Default.Create((object)this);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarItemsControl : ItemsControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsSourceCoreProperty;

		private NavBarControl navBar;

		public INotifyCollectionChanged ItemsSourceCore
		{
			get
			{
				return (INotifyCollectionChanged)GetValue(ItemsSourceCoreProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsSourceCoreProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private NavBarControl NavBar
		{
			get
			{
				if (navBar == null)
				{
					navBar = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<NavBarControl>((DependencyObject)this);
				}
				return navBar;
			}
		}

		static NavBarItemsControl()
		{
			ItemsSourceCoreProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsSourceCore", typeof(INotifyCollectionChanged), typeof(NavBarItemsControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnItemsSourceCorePropertyChanged));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavBarItemsControl>();
		}

		protected static void OnItemsSourceCorePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavBarItemsControl)d).ItemsSource = ((NavBarItemsControl)d).ItemsSourceCore as IEnumerable;
		}

		protected override void PrepareContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			NavBarItemControl navBarItemControl = element as NavBarItemControl;
			base.PrepareContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			if (navBarItemControl != null)
			{
				navBarItemControl.NavBar = NavBar;
				navBarItemControl.Source = item;
			}
		}

		protected override void ClearContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.ClearContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			(element as NavBarItemControl).Do(delegate(NavBarItemControl x)
			{
				x.NavBar = null;
				x.Source = null;
			});
		}
	}
