				return (Orientation)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ViewOrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ViewOrientationProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		internal ScrollMode ScrollMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (ScrollMode)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ScrollModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ScrollModeProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		internal NavBarViewBase OwnerView
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarViewBase)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(OwnerViewProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(OwnerViewProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		ScrollControl IScrollMode.ScrollControl => (ScrollControl)((FrameworkElement)this).GetTemplateChild("scrollControl");

		public bool ShowBorder
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ShowBorderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ShowBorderProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		static GroupItemsContainer()
		{
			ShowBorderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnShowBorderChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			NavBarViewKindProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavBarViewKind", typeof(NavBarViewKind), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnNavBarViewKindChanged();
			}));
			DisplaySourceProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("DisplaySource", typeof(DisplaySource), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(DisplaySource.Items, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnDisplaySourceChanged();
			}));
			ItemsCountProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsCount", typeof(int), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(0, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnItemsCountChanged();
			}));
			NavBarGroupContentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavBarGroupContent", typeof(object), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnNavBarGroupContentChanged();
			}));
			GroupPositionProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupPosition", typeof(GroupPosition), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(GroupPosition.First, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnGroupPositionChanged();
			}));
			AnimationProgressProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("AnimationProgress", typeof(double), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(0.0, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnAnimationProgressChanged();
			}));
			IsOverflowPanelVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsOverflowPanelVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).IsOverflowPanelVisiblePropertyChanged();
			}));
			IsSplitterVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsSplitterVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnIsSplitterVisiblePropertyChanged();
			}));
			ItemsControlGroupCountProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsControlGroupCount", typeof(int), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(0, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnItemsControlGroupCountPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ItemsPanelOrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsPanelOrientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnItemsPanelOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ViewOrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ViewOrientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ScrollModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ScrollMode", typeof(ScrollMode), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(ScrollMode.Buttons, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((GroupItemsContainer)(object)d).OnScrollModePropertyChanged();
			}));
			OwnerViewProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("OwnerView", typeof(NavBarViewBase), typeof(GroupItemsContainer), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<GroupItemsContainer>();
		}

		public GroupItemsContainer()
		{
			((FrameworkElement)this).DataContextChanged += OnDataContextChanged;
		}

		private void OnDataContextChanged(object sender, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.OldValue == null)
			{
				SetBindings();
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((FrameworkElement)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			OwnerView = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<NavBarViewBase>((DependencyObject)(object)this);
			SetBindings();
			Initialize();
		}

		private void Initialize()
		{
			OnNavBarViewKindChanged();
			OnDisplaySourceChanged();
			OnItemsCountChanged();
			UpdateScrollModeStates();
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			if (OwnerView != null)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(NavBarViewKindProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("NavBarViewKind")
				{
					Source = OwnerView
				});
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(ShowBorderProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("ShowBorder")
				{
					Source = OwnerView
				});
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(ItemsPanelOrientationProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("ItemsPanelOrientation")
				{
					Source = OwnerView
				});
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(ViewOrientationProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("Orientation")
				{
					Source = OwnerView
				});
				if (OwnerView is NavigationPaneView)
				{
					((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(IsSplitterVisibleProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("IsSplitterVisible")
					{
						Source = OwnerView
					});
					((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(ItemsControlGroupCountProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("ItemsControlGroupCount")
					{
						Source = OwnerView
					});
					((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(IsOverflowPanelVisibleProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("IsOverflowPanelVisible")
					{
						Source = OwnerView
					});
				}
			}
			if (((FrameworkElement)this).DataContext != null)
			{
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(DisplaySourceProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("DisplaySource"));
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(ItemsCountProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("Items.Count"));
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(NavBarGroupContentProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("Content"));
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(ScrollModeProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("ActualScrollMode"));
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(ScrollingSettings.ScrollModeProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("ActualScrollMode"));
				((FrameworkElement)this).SetBinding(GroupPositionProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("(0)")
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarPositionPanel.GroupPositionProperty)
				});
			}
		}

		private void OnAnimationProgressChanged()
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void OnDisplaySourceChanged()
		{
			UpdateStateExplorerBarContentEmptyOrItemsCountZero();
		}

		private void OnGroupPositionChanged()
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowBorderChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void OnItemsCountChanged()
		{
			UpdateStateExplorerBarContentEmptyOrItemsCountZero();
		}

		private void OnItemsPanelOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void OnOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void OnNavBarGroupContentChanged()
		{
			UpdateStateExplorerBarContentEmptyOrItemsCountZero();
		}

		private void OnNavBarViewKindChanged()
		{
			UpdateStateNavBarViewKind();
			UpdateStateExplorerBarContentEmptyOrItemsCountZero();
			UpdateNavPaneBorders();
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void IsOverflowPanelVisiblePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateNavPaneBorders();
		}

		private void OnItemsControlGroupCountPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateNavPaneBorders();
		}

		private void OnIsSplitterVisiblePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateNavPaneBorders();
		}

		private void OnScrollModePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateVisualState();
		}

		private void UpdateStateNavBarViewKind()
		{
			switch (NavBarViewKind)
			{
			case NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar:
				VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, "NavBarViewKindExplorerBar", useTransitions: false);
				break;
			case NavBarViewKind.NavigationPane:
				VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, "NavBarViewKindNavigationPane", useTransitions: false);
				break;
			case NavBarViewKind.SideBar:
				VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, "NavBarViewKindSideBar", useTransitions: false);
				break;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateNavPaneBorders()
		{
			if (ShowBorder)
			{
				string stateName = ((NavBarViewKind == NavBarViewKind.NavigationPane && ItemsControlGroupCount == 0 && !IsSplitterVisible && !IsOverflowPanelVisible) ? "ShowBottomBorder" : "HideBottomBorder");
				VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, stateName, useTransitions: false);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateScrollModeStates()
		{
			ScrollingSettings.UpdateScrollModeStates((Control)(object)this);
		}

		private void UpdateStateExplorerBarContentEmptyOrItemsCountZero()
		{
			bool flag = NavBarViewKind == NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar && DisplaySource == DisplaySource.Content && NavBarGroupContent == null;
			bool flag2 = NavBarViewKind == NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar && DisplaySource == DisplaySource.Items && ItemsCount == 0;
			if (flag || flag2)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, "ExplorerBarItemsOrContentEmpty", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, "ExplorerBarItemsOrContentNotEmpty", useTransitions: false);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateVisualState()
		{
			string empty = string.Empty;
			empty = DefineStateByNavBarViewKind();
			if (ShowBorder)
			{
				empty += DefineStateByGroupPosition();
				if (AnimationProgress == 0.0)
				{
					empty += "0";
				}
			}
			else
			{
				empty += "NoBorder";
			}
			empty += DefineStateByScrollMode(empty);
			VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, empty, useTransitions: false);
		}

		private string DefineStateByGroupPosition()
		{
			return GroupPosition switch
			{
				GroupPosition.First => "First", 
				GroupPosition.Last => "Last", 
				GroupPosition.Middle => "Middle", 
				GroupPosition.Single => "Single", 
				_ => string.Empty, 
			};
		}

		private string DefineStateByNavBarViewKind()
		{
			return NavBarViewKind switch
			{
				NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar => "ExplorerBar", 
				NavBarViewKind.NavigationPane => "NavigationPane", 
				NavBarViewKind.SideBar => "SideBar", 
				_ => string.Empty, 
			};
		}

		private string DefineStateByScrollMode(string state)
		{
			if (state == "SideBarLast" && OwnerView != null && ScrollingSettings.GetScrollMode(OwnerView) == ScrollMode.ScrollBar)
			{
				return "ScrollModeNormal";
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}
	}
	public enum DisplayMode
	{
		ImageAndText,
		Image,
		Text,
		Default
	}
	public class ImageSettings : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private double width = 16.0;

		private double height = 16.0;

		private Stretch stretch = Stretch.Uniform;

		private StretchDirection stretchDirection = StretchDirection.Both;

		public static ImageSettings GroupDefault => new ImageSettings
		{
			Height = 24.0,
			Width = 24.0,
			Stretch = Stretch.Uniform,
			StretchDirection = StretchDirection.Both
		};

		public static ImageSettings ItemDefault => new ImageSettings
		{
			Height = 16.0,
			Width = 16.0,
			Stretch = Stretch.Uniform,
			StretchDirection = StretchDirection.Both
		};

		public StretchDirection StretchDirection
		{
			get
			{
				return stretchDirection;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != stretchDirection)
				{
					stretchDirection = value;
					RaisePropertyChange("StretchDirection");
				}
			}
		}

		public Stretch Stretch
		{
			get
			{
				return stretch;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != stretch)
				{
					stretch = value;
					RaisePropertyChange("Stretch");
				}
			}
		}

		public double Height
		{
			get
			{
				return height;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != height)
				{
					height = value;
					RaisePropertyChange("Height");
				}
			}
		}

		public double Width
		{
			get
			{
				return width;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != width)
				{
					width = value;
					RaisePropertyChange("Width");
				}
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		protected virtual void RaisePropertyChange(string propertyName)
		{
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName));
			}
		}
	}
	public class LayoutSettings : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private HorizontalAlignment imageHorizontalAlignment;

		private VerticalAlignment imageVerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;

		private HorizontalAlignment textHorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;

		private VerticalAlignment textVerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;

	}
	public class FontSettings : INotifyPropertyChanged
	{
		private FontWeight? fontWeight;

		private FontStyle? fontStyle;

		private FontStretch? fontStretch;

		private double fontSize = double.NaN;

		private FontFamily fontFamily;

		private WeakList<Control> ownerList = new WeakList<Control>();

		private WeakList<PropertyChangedEventHandler> propertyChangedEHList = new WeakList<PropertyChangedEventHandler>();

		public static FontSettings Default => new FontSettings();

		public FontFamily FontFamily
		{
			get
			{
				return fontFamily;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != fontFamily)
				{
					fontFamily = value;
					RaisePropertyChange("FontFamily");
				}
			}
		}

		public double FontSize
		{
			get
			{
				return fontSize;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != fontSize)
				{
					fontSize = value;
					RaisePropertyChange("FontSize");
				}
			}
		}

		public FontStretch? FontStretch
		{
			get
			{
				return fontStretch;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value == fontStretch))
				{
					fontStretch = value;
					RaisePropertyChange("FontStretch");
				}
			}
		}

		public FontStyle? FontStyle
		{
			get
			{
				return fontStyle;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value == fontStyle))
				{
					fontStyle = value;
					RaisePropertyChange("FontStyle");
				}
			}
		}

		public FontWeight? FontWeight
		{
			get
			{
				return fontWeight;
			}
			set
			{
				if (!(value == fontWeight))
				{
					fontWeight = value;
					RaisePropertyChange("FontWeight");
				}
			}
		}

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler PropertyChanged;

		public event PropertyChangedEventHandler WeakPropertyChanged
		{
			add
			{
				propertyChangedEHList.Add(value);
			}
			remove
			{
				propertyChangedEHList.Remove(value);
			}
		}

		public void AddOwner(Control control)
		{
			ownerList.Add(control);
			CheckAllProperties(control);
		}

		public void RemoveOwner(Control control)
		{
			ownerList.Remove(control);
		}

		private void RaisePropertyChange(string propertyName)
		{
			PropertyChangedEventArgs e = new PropertyChangedEventArgs(propertyName);
			if (this.PropertyChanged != null)
			{
				this.PropertyChanged(this, e);
			}
			foreach (PropertyChangedEventHandler propertyChangedEH in propertyChangedEHList)
			{
				propertyChangedEH(this, e);
			}
			foreach (Control owner in ownerList)
			{
				SetValueIfNotDefaultOrClearIfUnset(owner, propertyName);
			}
		}

		public void SetValueIfNotDefaultOrClearIfUnset(Control control, string propertyName)
		{
			switch (propertyName)
			{
			case "FontWeight":
				CheckFontWeight(control);
				break;
			case "FontStyle":
				CheckFontStyle(control);
				break;
			case "FontStretch":
				CheckFontStretch(control);
				break;
			case "FontSize":
				CheckFontSize(control);
				break;
			case "FontFamily":
				CheckFontFamily(control);
				break;
			}
		}

		public void CheckAllProperties(Control control)
		{
			CheckFontWeight(control);
			CheckFontStyle(control);
			CheckFontStretch(control);
			CheckFontSize(control);
			CheckFontFamily(control);
		}

		private void SetCurrentValueCore(Control control, DependencyProperty property, object value)
		{
			control.SetCurrentValue(property, value);
		}

		private void CheckFontFamily(Control control)
		{
			if (FontFamily == null)
			{
				control.ClearValue(Control.FontFamilyProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				SetCurrentValueCore(control, Control.FontFamilyProperty, FontFamily);
			}
		}

		private void CheckFontSize(Control control)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(FontSize))
			{
				control.ClearValue(Control.FontSizeProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				SetCurrentValueCore(control, Control.FontSizeProperty, FontSize);
			}
		}

		private void CheckFontStretch(Control control)
		{
			if (!FontStretch.HasValue)
			{
				control.ClearValue(Control.FontStretchProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				SetCurrentValueCore(control, Control.FontStretchProperty, FontStretch.Value);
			}
		}

		private void CheckFontStyle(Control control)
		{
			if (!FontStyle.HasValue)
			{
				control.ClearValue(Control.FontStyleProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				SetCurrentValueCore(control, Control.FontStyleProperty, FontStyle.Value);
			}
		}

		private void CheckFontWeight(Control control)
		{
			if (!FontWeight.HasValue)
			{
				control.ClearValue(Control.FontWeightProperty);
			}
			else
			{
				SetCurrentValueCore(control, Control.FontWeightProperty, FontWeight.Value);
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavBarImageSettingsExtension : MarkupExtension
	{
		[Description("")]
		public double Width { get; set; }

		[Description("")]
		public double Height { get; set; }

		[Description("")]
		public Stretch Stretch { get; set; }

		[Description("")]
		public StretchDirection StretchDirection { get; set; }

		public NavBarImageSettingsExtension()
		{
			Width = 16.0;
			Height = 16.0;
			Stretch = Stretch.Uniform;
			StretchDirection = StretchDirection.Both;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			ImageSettings imageSettings = new ImageSettings();
			imageSettings.Width = Width;
			imageSettings.Height = Height;
			imageSettings.Stretch = Stretch;
			imageSettings.StretchDirection = StretchDirection;
			return imageSettings;
		}
	}
	public class NavBarLayoutSettingsExtension : MarkupExtension
	{
		[Description("")]
		public VerticalAlignment ImageVerticalAlignment { get; set; }

		[Description("")]
		public HorizontalAlignment ImageHorizontalAlignment { get; set; }

		[Description("")]
		public VerticalAlignment TextVerticalAlignment { get; set; }

		[Description("")]
		public HorizontalAlignment TextHorizontalAlignment { get; set; }

		[Description("")]
		public Dock ImageDocking { get; set; }

		public NavBarLayoutSettingsExtension()
		{
			ImageVerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
			ImageHorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Left;
			TextVerticalAlignment = VerticalAlignment.Center;
			TextHorizontalAlignment = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch;
			ImageDocking = Dock.Left;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			LayoutSettings layoutSettings = new LayoutSettings();
			layoutSettings.ImageVerticalAlignment = ImageVerticalAlignment;
			layoutSettings.ImageHorizontalAlignment = ImageHorizontalAlignment;
			layoutSettings.TextVerticalAlignment = TextVerticalAlignment;
			layoutSettings.TextHorizontalAlignment = TextHorizontalAlignment;
			layoutSettings.ImageDocking = ImageDocking;
			return layoutSettings;
		}
	}
	public class NavBarFontSettingsExtension : MarkupExtension
	{
		public FontFamily FontFamily { get; set; }

		public double FontSize { get; set; }

		public FontStretch? FontStretch { get; set; }

		public FontStyle? FontStyle { get; set; }

		public FontWeight? FontWeight { get; set; }

		public NavBarFontSettingsExtension()
		{
			FontFamily = null;
			FontSize = double.NaN;
			FontStretch = null;
			FontStyle = null;
			FontWeight = null;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return new FontSettings
			{
				FontFamily = FontFamily,
				FontSize = FontSize,
				FontStretch = FontStretch,
				FontStyle = FontStyle,
				FontWeight = FontWeight
			};
		}
	}
	internal class TextElementHelper<T> where T : FrameworkElement
	{
		public static void OverrideMetadata(Func<T, DependencyObject> getTargetFunc)
		{
			TextElement.FontSizeProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(T), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(SystemFonts.MessageFontSize, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnFontSizeChanged((T)d, (double)e.NewValue, getTargetFunc);
			}));
			TextElement.FontWeightProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(T), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(SystemFonts.MessageFontWeight, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnFontWeightChanged((T)d, (FontWeight)e.NewValue, getTargetFunc);
			}));
			TextElement.FontFamilyProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(T), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(SystemFonts.MessageFontFamily, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnFontFamilyChanged((T)d, (FontFamily)e.NewValue, getTargetFunc);
			}));
			TextElement.FontStretchProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(T), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(FontStretches.Normal, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnFontStretchChanged((T)d, (FontStretch)e.NewValue, getTargetFunc);
			}));
			TextElement.FontStyleProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(T), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(SystemFonts.MessageFontStyle, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnFontStyleChanged((T)d, (FontStyle)e.NewValue, getTargetFunc);
			}));
		}

		private static bool CheckIsConnectedToVisualTree(T source)
		{
			return PresentationSource.FromVisual(source).With((PresentationSource x) => x.RootVisual).ReturnSuccess();
		}

		private static void OnFontStyleChanged(T source, FontStyle newValue, Func<T, DependencyObject> getTargetFunc)
		{
			if (!CheckIsConnectedToVisualTree(source))
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = getTargetFunc(source);
			if (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				if (DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(dependencyObject, TextElement.FontSizeProperty).BaseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Inherited)
				{
					FontStyle value = ((newValue == FontStyles.Italic) ? FontStyles.Normal : FontStyles.Italic);
					TextElement.SetFontStyle(dependencyObject, value);
				}
				TextElement.SetFontStyle(dependencyObject, newValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnFontStretchChanged(T source, FontStretch newValue, Func<T, DependencyObject> getTargetFunc)
		{
			if (!CheckIsConnectedToVisualTree(source))
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = getTargetFunc(source);
			if (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				if (DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(dependencyObject, TextElement.FontWeightProperty).BaseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Inherited)
				{
					TextElement.SetFontStretch(dependencyObject, FontStretch.FromOpenTypeStretch(Math.Max(newValue.ToOpenTypeStretch() + 1, 9)));
				}
				TextElement.SetFontStretch(dependencyObject, newValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnFontFamilyChanged(T source, FontFamily newValue, Func<T, DependencyObject> getTargetFunc)
		{
			if (!CheckIsConnectedToVisualTree(source))
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = getTargetFunc(source);
			if (dependencyObject == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(dependencyObject, TextElement.FontSizeProperty).BaseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Inherited)
			{
				FontFamily value = (string.Equals(newValue.With((FontFamily x) => x.ToString()), "Times New Roman") ? new FontFamily("Sergoe UI") : new FontFamily("Times New Roman"));
				TextElement.SetFontFamily(dependencyObject, value);
			}
			TextElement.SetFontFamily(dependencyObject, newValue);
		}

		private static void OnFontWeightChanged(T source, FontWeight newValue, Func<T, DependencyObject> getTargetFunc)
		{
			if (!CheckIsConnectedToVisualTree(source))
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = getTargetFunc(source);
			if (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				if (DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(dependencyObject, TextElement.FontWeightProperty).BaseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Inherited)
				{
					TextElement.SetFontWeight(dependencyObject, FontWeight.FromOpenTypeWeight(Math.Max(newValue.ToOpenTypeWeight() + 1, 999)));
				}
				TextElement.SetFontWeight(dependencyObject, newValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnFontSizeChanged(T source, double newValue, Func<T, DependencyObject> getTargetFunc)
		{
			if (!CheckIsConnectedToVisualTree(source))
			{
				return;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = getTargetFunc(source);
			if (dependencyObject != null)
			{
				if (DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(dependencyObject, TextElement.FontSizeProperty).BaseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Inherited)
				{
					TextElement.SetFontSize(dependencyObject, newValue + 1.0);
				}
				TextElement.SetFontSize(dependencyObject, newValue);
			}
		}
	}
	internal static class AttachedProperties
	{
		public static Dictionary<string, DependencyProperty> Properties { get; set; }

		static AttachedProperties()
		{
			Properties = new Dictionary<string, DependencyProperty>();
			Properties.Add("NavBarAnimationOptions.HorizontalExpand", NavBarAnimationOptions.HorizontalExpandProperty);
			Properties.Add("NavBarAnimationOptions.VerticalExpand", NavBarAnimationOptions.VerticalExpandProperty);
			Properties.Add("XPFDockPanel.Dock", DockPanel.DockProperty);
			Properties.Add("NavigationPaneView.Element", NavigationPaneView.ElementProperty);
			Properties.Add("CursorHelper.Cursor", CursorHelper.CursorProperty);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarTargetPropertyResolver : TargetPropertyUpdater
	{
		public NavBarTargetPropertyResolver(FrameworkElement owner)
			: base(owner)
		{
		}

		protected override DependencyProperty GetDependencyPropertyByPath(string path)
		{
			if (!AttachedProperties.Properties.ContainsKey(path))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return AttachedProperties.Properties[path];
		}
	}
	public class NavBarTargetPropertyResolverFactory : TargetPropertyUpdaterFactory
	{
		public override TargetPropertyUpdater CreateTargetPropertyResolver(FrameworkElement owner)
		{
			return (TargetPropertyUpdater)(object)new NavBarTargetPropertyResolver(owner);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarVisualStateHelper : VisualStateHelper
	{
		public static void UpdateStates(FrameworkElement owner, string groupName)
		{
			VisualStateHelper.UpdateStates(owner, groupName, AttachedProperties.Properties);
		}
	}
	public enum NavBarViewKind
	{
		ExplorerBar,
		NavigationPane,
		SideBar
	}
	public class NavBarItemSelectedEventArgs : RoutedEventArgs
	{
		public NavBarGroup Group { get; internal set; }

		public NavBarItem Item { get; internal set; }

		public NavBarItemSelectedEventArgs(NavBarGroup group, NavBarItem item)
		{
			Group = group;
			Item = item;
		}

		protected override void InvokeEventHandler(Delegate genericHandler, object genericTarget)
		{
			((NavBarItemSelectedEventHandler)genericHandler)(genericTarget, this);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarItemSelectingEventArgs : RoutedEventArgs
	{
		public bool Cancel { get; set; }

		public NavBarGroup PrevGroup { get; internal set; }

		public NavBarItem PrevItem { get; internal set; }

		public NavBarGroup NewGroup { get; internal set; }

		public NavBarItem NewItem { get; internal set; }

		public NavBarItemSelectingEventArgs(NavBarGroup prevGroup, NavBarItem prevItem, NavBarGroup newGroup, NavBarItem newItem)
		{
			PrevGroup = prevGroup;
			PrevItem = prevItem;
			NewGroup = newGroup;
			NewItem = newItem;
		}

		protected override void InvokeEventHandler(Delegate genericHandler, object genericTarget)
		{
			((NavBarItemSelectingEventHandler)genericHandler)(genericTarget, this);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarActiveGroupChangedEventArgs : RoutedEventArgs
	{
		public NavBarGroup Group { get; internal set; }

		public NavBarActiveGroupChangedEventArgs(NavBarGroup group)
		{
			Group = group;
		}

		protected override void InvokeEventHandler(Delegate genericHandler, object genericTarget)
		{
			((NavBarActiveGroupChangedEventHandler)genericHandler)(genericTarget, this);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarActiveGroupChangingEventArgs : RoutedEventArgs
	{
		public bool Cancel { get; set; }

		public NavBarGroup PrevGroup { get; internal set; }

		public NavBarGroup NewGroup { get; internal set; }

		public NavBarActiveGroupChangingEventArgs(NavBarGroup prevGroup, NavBarGroup newGroup)
		{
			PrevGroup = prevGroup;
			NewGroup = newGroup;
		}

		protected override void InvokeEventHandler(Delegate genericHandler, object genericTarget)
		{
			((NavBarActiveGroupChangingEventHandler)genericHandler)(genericTarget, this);
		}
	}

		static NavBarViewBase()
		{
			HeaderTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("HeaderTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnHeaderTemplateChanged));
			HeaderTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("HeaderTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnHeaderTemplateSelectorChanged));
			ItemTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnItemTemplateChanged));
			ItemControlTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemControlTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			ItemTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnItemTemplateSelectorChanged));
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarViewBase)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ItemsPanelTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsPanelTemplate", typeof(ItemsPanelTemplate), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			ItemsPanelOrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsPanelOrientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical));
			ItemSelectingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ItemSelectingEvent", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(NavBarItemSelectingEventHandler), typeof(NavBarViewBase));
			ItemSelectedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ItemSelectedEvent", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(NavBarItemSelectedEventHandler), typeof(NavBarViewBase));
			ActiveGroupChangingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ActiveGroupChangingEvent", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(NavBarActiveGroupChangingEventHandler), typeof(NavBarViewBase));
			ActiveGroupChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ActiveGroupChangedEvent", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(NavBarActiveGroupChangedEventHandler), typeof(NavBarViewBase));
			NavBarViewKindProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavBarViewKind", typeof(NavBarViewKind), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar));
			ImageSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("ImageSettings", typeof(ImageSettings), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			LayoutSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("LayoutSettings", typeof(LayoutSettings), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			DisplayModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("DisplayMode", typeof(DisplayMode), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DisplayMode.ImageAndText));
			GroupVisualStyleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupVisualStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnGroupVisualStyleChanged));
			ItemVisualStyleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemVisualStyle", typeof(Style), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnItemVisualStyleChanged));
			GroupHeaderTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupHeaderTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnGroupHeaderTemplateChanged));
			NavBarPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("NavBar", typeof(NavBarControl), typeof(NavBarViewBase), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnNavBarPropertyChanged));
			NavBarProperty = NavBarPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			AnimateGroupExpansionProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("AnimateGroupExpansion", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, OnAnimateGroupExpansionPropertyChanged));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(NavBarViewBase), new CommandBinding(NavBarCommands.ChangeGroupExpanded, OnChangeGroupExpanded));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(NavBarViewBase), new CommandBinding(NavBarCommands.SetActiveGroup, OnSetActiveGroup));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(NavBarViewBase), new CommandBinding(NavBarCommands.SelectItem, OnSelectItem));
			ClickEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("ClickEvent", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(RoutedEventHandler), typeof(NavBarViewBase));
			ScrollingSettings.IsManipulationEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarViewBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			NavigationAutomationPeersCreator.Default.RegisterObject(typeof(NavBarViewBase), typeof(NavBarViewAutomationPeer), (CreateObjectMethod<AutomationPeer>)((object owner) => (AutomationPeer)(object)new NavBarViewAutomationPeer((NavBarViewBase)owner)));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarViewBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
			GroupDisplayModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupDisplayMode", typeof(DisplayMode), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DisplayMode.Default, OnGroupDisplayModePropertyChanged));
			GroupLayoutSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupLayoutSettings", typeof(LayoutSettings), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnGroupLayoutSettingsPropertyChanged));
			GroupImageSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupImageSettings", typeof(ImageSettings), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnGroupImageSettingsPropertyChanged));
			ItemDisplayModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemDisplayMode", typeof(DisplayMode), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DisplayMode.Default, OnItemDisplayModePropertyChanged));
			ItemLayoutSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemLayoutSettings", typeof(LayoutSettings), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnItemLayoutSettingsPropertyChanged));
			ItemImageSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemImageSettings", typeof(ImageSettings), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnItemImageSettingsPropertyChanged));
			GroupFontSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupFontSettings", typeof(FontSettings), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnGroupFontSettingsPropertyChanged));
			ItemFontSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemFontSettings", typeof(FontSettings), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnItemFontSettingsPropertyChanged));
			FontSettingsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("FontSettings", typeof(FontSettings), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnFontSettingsPropertyChanged));
			ShowBorderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarViewBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			Control.ForegroundProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarViewBase), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				d.CoerceValue(Control.ForegroundProperty);
			}));

		}

		protected static void OnFontSettingsPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (d is Control)
			{
				FontSettings fontSettings = (FontSettings)e.OldValue;
				FontSettings fontSettings2 = (FontSettings)e.NewValue;
				fontSettings?.RemoveOwner((Control)d);
				fontSettings2?.AddOwner((Control)d);
			}
		}

		public NavBarViewBase()
		{
			AddHandler(ButtonBase.ClickEvent, new RoutedEventHandler(ButtonBaseClick));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			NavBarVisualStateHelper.UpdateStates(this, "OrientationStates");
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		public void ChangeGroupExpanded(NavBarGroup group)
		{
			group.ChangeGroupExpanded();
		}

		public void SetActiveGroup(NavBarGroup group)
		{
			NavBar.ActiveGroup = group;
		}

		public void SelectItem(NavBarItem item)
		{
			if (item != null && item.Group != null)
			{
				item.Group.SelectedItem = item;
			}
		}

		public NavBarGroup GetNavBarGroup(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			return GetNavBarGroupInternal(e, inHeaderOnly: false);
		}

		internal NavBarGroup GetNavBarGroupInternal(RoutedEventArgs e, bool inHeaderOnly)
		{
			object originalSource = e.OriginalSource;
			if (!(originalSource is DependencyObject))
			{
				return null;
			}
			DependencyObject dependencyObject = originalSource as DependencyObject;
			DependencyObject dependencyObject2 = ((!inHeaderOnly) ? dependencyObject : LayoutHelper.FindLayoutOrVisualParentObject(dependencyObject, (Predicate<DependencyObject>)((DependencyObject x) => NavBarGroupHelper.GetIsGroupHeader(x)), false, (DependencyObject)null));
			if (dependencyObject2 == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return LayoutHelper.FindLayoutOrVisualParentObject<NavBarGroupControl>(dependencyObject2, true, (DependencyObject)null)?.Group;
		}

		public NavBarItem GetNavBarItem(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			object originalSource = e.OriginalSource;
			NavBarItemControl navBarItemControl = ((originalSource is DependencyObject) ? LayoutHelper.FindLayoutOrVisualParentObject<NavBarItemControl>(originalSource as DependencyObject, true, (DependencyObject)null) : null);
			if (navBarItemControl != null)
			{
				return navBarItemControl.DataContext as NavBarItem;
			}
			return null;
		}

		internal T FindAncestor<T>(object obj) where T : class
		{
			return FindAncestorCore<T>(obj);
		}

		internal static T FindAncestorCore<T>(object obj) where T : class
		{
			for (FrameworkElement frameworkElement = obj as FrameworkElement; frameworkElement != null; frameworkElement = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(frameworkElement) as FrameworkElement)
			{
				if (frameworkElement.DataContext != null && frameworkElement.DataContext is T)
				{
					return (T)frameworkElement.DataContext;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		}

		protected internal virtual void RaiseGroupAdding(NavBarGroup group, object sourceObject)

				groupAdding(this, e);
			}
		}

		{

			if (itemAdding != null)
			{
				itemAdding(this, e);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateOrientationStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? "Vertical" : "Horizontal", useTransitions: false);
			UpdateLayout();
		}

		internal void ButtonBaseClick(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			NavBarGroup navBarGroup = GetNavBarGroup(e);
			NavBarGroup navBarGroupInternal = GetNavBarGroupInternal(e, inHeaderOnly: true);
			NavBarItem navBarItem = GetNavBarItem(e);
			if (navBarGroup != null && navBarGroup.NavBar == NavBar)
			{
				NavBar.SelectionStrategy.SelectGroup(navBarGroup);
			}
			RaiseEvent(new RoutedEventArgs(ClickEvent, e.OriginalSource));
			if (navBarGroup == null || navBarGroup.NavBar != NavBar)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (navBarItem == null)
			{
				if (navBarGroupInternal != null)
				{
					navBarGroup.RaiseClickEvent();
				}
			}
			else if (navBarItem.Group != null && navBarItem.Group.NavBar == NavBar)
			{
				navBarItem.RaiseClickEvent();
			}
		}

		protected override AutomationPeer OnCreateAutomationPeer()
		{
			return NavigationAutomationPeersCreator.Default.Create((object)this);
		}

		private object GetActualSource(RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			return e.OriginalSource;
		}
	}
	internal class NavBarValueSelectorHelper
	{
		public static object GetValue(DependencyObject[] objs, DependencyProperty[] properties, object defaultValue)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < objs.Count(); i++)
			{
				object obj = ((objs[i] != null) ? objs[i].GetValue(properties[i]) : null);
				if (obj != null && !obj.Equals(defaultValue))
				{
					return obj;
				}
			}
			return defaultValue;
		}
	}
	public class IsActiveToAnimationProgressConverter : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return ((bool)value) ? 1.0 : 0.0;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}
	public class NavBarViewPresenter : ContentControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ViewProperty;

		public NavBarViewBase View
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarViewBase)GetValue(ViewProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ViewProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavBarViewPresenter()
		{
			ViewProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("View", typeof(NavBarViewBase), typeof(NavBarViewPresenter), new PropertyMetadata(delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarViewPresenter)d).OnViewPropertyChanged();
			}));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavBarViewPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		protected virtual void OnViewPropertyChanged()
		{
			if (View != null)
			{
				FrameworkElementExtensions.RemoveFromVisualTree((FrameworkElement)View);
			}
			base.Content = View;
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Expanded", GroupName = "ExpandStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Collapsed", GroupName = "ExpandStates")]
	public abstract class ExpandButtonBase : Button
	{
		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsExpandedProperty;

		internal bool IsExpanded
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsExpandedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsExpandedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static ExpandButtonBase()
		{
			IsExpandedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsExpanded", typeof(bool), typeof(ExpandButtonBase), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ExpandButtonBase)d).OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged();
			}));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			SetBindings();
			UpdateExpandStates();
		}

		protected internal abstract void SetBindings();

		protected internal virtual void OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateExpandStates();
		}

		private void UpdateExpandStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, IsExpanded ? "Expanded" : "Collapsed", useTransitions: false);
		}
	}
	public class NavigationPaneCommands
	{
		private static readonly RoutedCommand changeNavPaneExpanded = new RoutedCommand("ChangeNavPaneExpanded", typeof(NavigationPaneCommands));

		private static readonly RoutedCommand showMoreGroups = new RoutedCommand("ShowMoreGroups", typeof(NavigationPaneCommands));

		private static readonly RoutedCommand showFewerGroups = new RoutedCommand("ShowFewerGroups", typeof(NavigationPaneCommands));

		[Description("Toggles the Navigation Pane‘s expanded state.")]
		public static RoutedCommand ChangeNavPaneExpanded => changeNavPaneExpanded;

		[Description("Increments (by one) the number of visible group headers displayed within the Navigation Pane’s group button panel.")]
		public static RoutedCommand ShowMoreGroups => showMoreGroups;

		[Description("Decreases the number of visible group headers displayed within the Navigation Pane’s group button panel.")]
		public static RoutedCommand ShowFewerGroups => showFewerGroups;
	}
	public enum LayoutOptions
	{
		Fixed,
		Fill,
		CalcSize
	}
	public class NavigationPanePanelBase : Panel
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavigationPanePanelBase), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, OnOrientationChanged));

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected NavigationPaneView NavigationPaneView { get; set; }

		private static void OnOrientationChanged(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (o is NavigationPanePanel navigationPanePanel)
			{
				navigationPanePanel.OnOrientationChanged((Orientation)e.OldValue, (Orientation)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnOrientationChanged(Orientation oldValue, Orientation newValue)
		{
			InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateNavigationPaneView()
		{
			if (NavigationPaneView == null)
			{
				NavigationPaneView = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<NavigationPaneView>((DependencyObject)this);
				if (NavigationPaneView != null)
				{
					OnNavigationPaneViewChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnNavigationPaneViewChanged()
		{
			UpdateOrientationBinding();
		}

		protected void UpdateOrientationBinding()
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding("Orientation");
			binding.Source = NavigationPaneView;
			SetBinding(OrientationProperty, binding);
		}

		protected Size GetSize(Size size)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				return size;
			}
			return new Size(size.Height, size.Width);
		}

		protected Size CreateSize(double width, double height)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				return new Size(width, height);
			}
			return new Size(height, width);
		}

		protected double GetHeight(Size size)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				return size.Height;
			}
			return size.Width;
		}

		protected double GetWidth(Size size)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				return size.Width;
			}
			return size.Height;
		}

		protected Rect CreateRect(double x, double y, double width, double height)
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				return new Rect(x, y, width, height);
			}
			return new Rect(y, x, height, width);
		}
	}
	public class NavigationPanePanel : NavigationPanePanelBase
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty HeaderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Header", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(NavigationPanePanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnHeaderChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActiveGroupProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActiveGroup", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(NavigationPanePanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnActiveGroupChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty SplitterProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Splitter", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(NavigationPanePanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnSplitterChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupsControlProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupsControl", typeof(NavPaneGroupButtonPanelControl), typeof(NavigationPanePanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnGroupsControlChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OverflowPanelProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("OverflowPanel", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(NavigationPanePanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnOverflowPanelChanged));

		public FrameworkElement Header
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)GetValue(HeaderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(HeaderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public FrameworkElement ActiveGroup
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)GetValue(ActiveGroupProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ActiveGroupProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public FrameworkElement Splitter
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)GetValue(SplitterProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(SplitterProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public NavPaneGroupButtonPanelControl GroupsControl
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavPaneGroupButtonPanelControl)GetValue(GroupsControlProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GroupsControlProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public FrameworkElement OverflowPanel
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)GetValue(OverflowPanelProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OverflowPanelProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private static void OnHeaderChanged(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (o is NavigationPanePanel navigationPanePanel)
			{
				navigationPanePanel.OnHeaderChanged((FrameworkElement)e.OldValue, (FrameworkElement)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnActiveGroupChanged(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (o is NavigationPanePanel navigationPanePanel)
			{
				navigationPanePanel.OnActiveGroupChanged((FrameworkElement)e.OldValue, (FrameworkElement)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnSplitterChanged(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (o is NavigationPanePanel navigationPanePanel)
			{
				navigationPanePanel.OnSplitterChanged((FrameworkElement)e.OldValue, (FrameworkElement)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnGroupsControlChanged(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (o is NavigationPanePanel navigationPanePanel)
			{
				navigationPanePanel.OnGroupsControlChanged((NavPaneGroupButtonPanelControl)e.OldValue, (NavPaneGroupButtonPanelControl)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		private static void OnOverflowPanelChanged(DependencyObject o, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (o is NavigationPanePanel navigationPanePanel)
			{
				navigationPanePanel.OnOverflowPanelChanged((FrameworkElement)e.OldValue, (FrameworkElement)e.NewValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnHeaderChanged(FrameworkElement oldValue, FrameworkElement newValue)
		{
			base.Children.Remove(oldValue);
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				base.Children.Add(newValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnActiveGroupChanged(FrameworkElement oldValue, FrameworkElement newValue)
		{
			base.Children.Remove(oldValue);
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				base.Children.Add(newValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSplitterChanged(FrameworkElement oldValue, FrameworkElement newValue)
		{
			base.Children.Remove(oldValue);
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				base.Children.Add(newValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnGroupsControlChanged(FrameworkElement oldValue, FrameworkElement newValue)
		{
			base.Children.Remove(oldValue);
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				base.Children.Add(newValue);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnOverflowPanelChanged(FrameworkElement oldValue, FrameworkElement newValue)
		{
			base.Children.Remove(oldValue);
			if (newValue != null)
			{
				base.Children.Add(newValue);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnNavigationPaneViewChanged()
		{
			base.OnNavigationPaneViewChanged();
			if (base.NavigationPaneView != null)
			{
				base.NavigationPaneView.Panel = this;
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			UpdateNavigationPaneView();
			double num = GetHeight(availableSize);
			double width = GetWidth(availableSize);
			double num2 = 0.0;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			if (Header != null)
			{
				Header.Measure(CreateSize(width, double.PositiveInfinity));
				num -= GetHeight(Header.DesiredSize);
				num = Math.Max(num, 0.0);
				num2 += GetHeight(Header.DesiredSize);
				num3 = GetWidth(Header.DesiredSize);
			}
			if (Splitter != null)
			{
				Splitter.Measure(CreateSize(width, double.PositiveInfinity));
				num -= GetHeight(Splitter.DesiredSize);
				num = Math.Max(num, 0.0);
				num2 += GetHeight(Splitter.DesiredSize);
				num3 = Math.Max(num3, GetWidth(Splitter.DesiredSize));
			}
			if (OverflowPanel != null)
			{
				OverflowPanel.Measure(CreateSize(width, double.PositiveInfinity));
				num -= GetHeight(OverflowPanel.DesiredSize);
				num = Math.Max(num, 0.0);
				num2 += GetHeight(OverflowPanel.DesiredSize);
				num3 = Math.Max(num3, GetWidth(OverflowPanel.DesiredSize));
			}
			if (GroupsControl != null)
			{
				double num4 = ((ActiveGroup != null) ? GetHeight(new Size(ActiveGroup.MinWidth, ActiveGroup.MinHeight)) : 0.0);
				GroupsControl.Measure(CreateSize(width, Math.Max(0.0, num - num4)));
				num -= GetHeight(GroupsControl.DesiredSize);
				num = Math.Max(num, 0.0);
				num2 += GetHeight(GroupsControl.DesiredSize);
				num3 = Math.Max(num3, GetWidth(GroupsControl.DesiredSize));
			}
			if (ActiveGroup != null)
			{
				ActiveGroup.Measure(CreateSize(width, num));
				num -= GetHeight(ActiveGroup.DesiredSize);
				num2 += GetHeight(ActiveGroup.DesiredSize);
				num3 = Math.Max(num3, GetWidth(ActiveGroup.DesiredSize));
			}
			return CreateSize(num3, num2);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			double num = GetHeight(finalSize);
			double num2 = num;
			double width = GetWidth(finalSize);
			double num3 = 0.0;
			if (Header != null)
			{
				num3 = GetHeight(Header.DesiredSize);
				Header.Arrange(CreateRect(0.0, 0.0, width, num3));
				num -= num3;
			}
			double num4 = 0.0;
			if (OverflowPanel != null)
			{
				num4 = GetHeight(OverflowPanel.DesiredSize);
				OverflowPanel.Arrange(CreateRect(0.0, num2 - num4, width, num4));
				num -= num4;
			}
			double num5 = 0.0;
			if (GroupsControl != null)
			{
				num5 = GetHeight(GroupsControl.DesiredSize);
				GroupsControl.Arrange(CreateRect(0.0, num2 - num4 - num5, width, num5));
				num -= num5;
			}
			double num6 = 0.0;
			if (Splitter != null)
			{
				num6 = GetHeight(Splitter.DesiredSize);
				Splitter.Arrange(CreateRect(0.0, num2 - num4 - num5 - num6, width, num6));
				num -= num6;
			}
			if (ActiveGroup != null)
			{
				ActiveGroup.Arrange(CreateRect(0.0, num3, width, num));
			}
			return finalSize;
		}
	}

	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "WithSplitter", GroupName = "SplitterBehaviorStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "WithoutSplitter", GroupName = "SplitterBehaviorStates")]
	public class NavPaneOverflowPanel : ContentControl
	{
		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsSplitterVisibleProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsControlGroupCountProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowBorderProperty;

		internal bool IsSplitterVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsSplitterVisibleProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsSplitterVisibleProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal int ItemsControlGroupCount
		{
			get
			{
				return (int)GetValue(ItemsControlGroupCountProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsControlGroupCountProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShowBorder
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShowBorderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowBorderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavPaneOverflowPanel()
		{
			ShowBorderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneOverflowPanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneOverflowPanel)d).OnShowBorderChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			IsSplitterVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsSplitterVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneOverflowPanel), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneOverflowPanel)d).OnIsSplitterVisiblePropertyChanged();
			}));
			ItemsControlGroupCountProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsControlGroupCount", typeof(int), typeof(NavPaneOverflowPanel), new PropertyMetadata(0, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneOverflowPanel)d).OnItemsControlGroupCountPropertyChanged();
			}));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavPaneOverflowPanel>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			SetBindings();
		}

		private void OnItemsControlGroupCountPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateSplitterBehaviorStates();
		}

		private void OnIsSplitterVisiblePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateSplitterBehaviorStates();
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowBorderChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			UpdateShowBorderStates();
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(IsSplitterVisibleProperty, new Binding("View.IsSplitterVisible"));
			SetBinding(ItemsControlGroupCountProperty, new Binding("View.ItemsControlGroupCount"));
			SetBinding(ShowBorderProperty, new Binding("View.ShowBorder"));
		}

		private void UpdateSplitterBehaviorStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, (!IsSplitterVisible && ItemsControlGroupCount == 0 && ShowBorder) ? "WithoutSplitter" : "WithSplitter", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateShowBorderStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, ShowBorder ? "WithBorder" : "WithoutBorder", useTransitions: false);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarPopupMenu : PopupMenu
	{
		protected override void UpdatePlacement(UIElement control)
		{
			((BarPopupExpandable)this).UpdatePlacement(control);
			if (((FrameworkElement)this).DataContext is NavBarControl && ((NavBarControl)((FrameworkElement)this).DataContext).View.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				((UIElement)this).RenderTransform = new RotateTransform
				{
					Angle = 90.0
				};
				((Popup)this).VerticalOffset -= ((FrameworkElement)control).ActualWidth;
			}
		}
	}

			//IL_002c: Expected O, but got Unknown

			BarCheckItem val = new BarCheckItem();
			((FrameworkContentElement)val).Name = "GroupItem" + ItemCount;
			BarCheckItem val2 = val;
			((FrameworkContentElement)(object)val2).Tag = true;
			if (group.MenuItemDisplayBinding != null)
			{
				((FrameworkContentElement)(object)val2).SetBinding(BarItem.ContentProperty, (BindingBase)(createBindingClone(group.MenuItemDisplayBinding, BindingMode.OneWay) as Binding).Do(delegate(Binding x)
				{
					x.Source = group.Header;
				}));
			}
			else if ((group.Header as DependencyObject).With(LogicalTreeHelper.GetParent) == null)
			{

			((BarItem)val2).ContentTemplate = group.HeaderTemplate;

		}

		protected override void OnTouchDown(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTouchDown(e);
			point = e.GetTouchPoint(this);
			emulateClick = true;
		}

		protected override void OnTouchMove(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTouchMove(e);
			if (point == null || !object.Equals(e.GetTouchPoint(this).Position, point.Position))
			{
				emulateClick = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnTouchUp(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTouchUp(e);
			if (emulateClick)
			{
				point = null;
				emulateClick = false;
				if (!e.Handled)
				{
					OnClick();
				}
			}
		}

	public class NavPaneItemsControl : ItemsControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty VisibleElementCountProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		public NavPaneItemsControl()

		protected virtual void CreateBindings()
		{

			{

			};

		protected override DependencyObject GetContainerForItemOverride()
		{
			NavBarGroupControl navBarGroupControl = new NavBarGroupControl();
			navBarGroupControl.SetValue(NavigationPaneView.ElementProperty, GetValue(NavigationPaneView.ElementProperty));
			if (base.DataContext is NavBarControl)
			{
				navBarGroupControl.SetBinding(ScrollingSettings.ScrollModeProperty, new Binding("(0)")
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath(ScrollingSettings.ScrollModeProperty),
					Source = ((NavBarControl)base.DataContext).View
				});
			}
			return navBarGroupControl;
		}

		protected override void PrepareContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.PrepareContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			if (element is NavBarGroupControl navBarGroupControl)
			{
				navBarGroupControl.SetBinding(NavBarGroupControl.NavBarProperty, new Binding("NavBar")
				{
					Source = this
				});
			}
		}

		protected override void ClearContainerForItemOverride(DependencyObject element, object item)
		{
			base.ClearContainerForItemOverride(element, item);
			if (element is NavBarGroupControl navBarGroupControl)
			{
				navBarGroupControl.ClearValue(NavBarGroupControl.NavBarProperty);
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		protected virtual void OnNavBarChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void VisibleElementCountChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneGroupButtonPanelControl : NavPaneItemsControl
	{
		public Size GetChildrenDesiredSizeByInfinity()
		{
			return MeasureOverride(SizeHelper.Infinite);
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneGroupButtonPanel : NavigationPanePanelBase
	{
		protected NavigationPaneView View { get; set; }

		protected int GetMaxVisibleGroupCount()
		{
			if (View == null)
			{
				View = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<NavigationPaneView>((DependencyObject)this);
			}
			if (View == null)
			{
				return base.Children.Count;
			}
			return View.ActualMaxVisibleGroupCount;
		}

		private void UpdateGroupPosition(int count)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < count; i++)
			{
				SetGroupPosition(base.Children[i], count);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void SetGroupPosition(UIElement elem, int count)
		{
			if (count == 1)
			{
				NavBarPositionPanel.SetGroupPosition(elem, GroupPosition.Single);
			}
			else if (base.Children.IndexOf(elem) == 0)
			{
				NavBarPositionPanel.SetGroupPosition(elem, GroupPosition.First);
			}
			else if (base.Children.IndexOf(elem) == count - 1)
			{
				NavBarPositionPanel.SetGroupPosition(elem, GroupPosition.Last);
			}
			else
			{
				NavBarPositionPanel.SetGroupPosition(elem, GroupPosition.Middle);
			}
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			UpdateNavigationPaneView();
			double num = 0.0;
			double val = 0.0;
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				child.Measure(CreateSize(GetWidth(availableSize), double.PositiveInfinity));
				val = Math.Max(val, GetWidth(child.DesiredSize));
			}
			int i;
			for (i = 0; i < Math.Min(base.Children.Count, GetMaxVisibleGroupCount()); i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement2 = base.Children[i];
				uIElement2.IsHitTestVisible = true;
				if (num + GetHeight(uIElement2.DesiredSize) > GetHeight(availableSize))
				{
					break;
				}
				num += GetHeight(uIElement2.DesiredSize);
			}
			if (View != null)
			{
				View.ActualVisibleGroupCount = i;
			}
			UpdateGroupPosition(i);
			for (; i < base.Children.Count; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement3 = base.Children[i];
				uIElement3.IsHitTestVisible = false;
			}
			return CreateSize(Math.Min(val, GetWidth(availableSize)), num);
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size finalSize)
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				child.Arrange(CreateRect(0.0, num, GetWidth(finalSize), GetHeight(child.DesiredSize)));
				num += GetHeight(child.DesiredSize);
			}
			return finalSize;
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Normal", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "IsMouseOver", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "IsPressed", GroupName = "CommonStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "IsActiveTrue", GroupName = "IsActiveStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "IsActiveFalse", GroupName = "IsActiveStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Expanded", GroupName = "ExpandStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Collapsed", GroupName = "ExpandStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Vertical", GroupName = "OrientationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Horizontal", GroupName = "OrientationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "WithSplitter", GroupName = "SplitterVisibilityStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "WithoutSplitter", GroupName = "SplitterVisibilityStates")]
	public class NavPaneGroupButton : NavPaneXPFButton
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupPositionProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsActiveProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsExpandedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsSplitterVisibleProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsPressedCoreProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowBorderProperty;

		public GroupPosition GroupPosition
		{

				return (GroupPosition)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(GroupPositionProperty);

			set

		}

		public bool IsActive
		{
			get

				return (bool)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(IsActiveProperty);

			set

			}
		}

		public bool IsExpanded
		{

		public bool IsSplitterVisible

				return (bool)((DependencyObject)(object)this).GetValue(IsPressedCoreProperty);
