		static NavPaneGroupButton()
		{
			ShowBorderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneGroupButton), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButton)(object)d).OnShowBorderChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			GroupPositionProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupPosition", typeof(GroupPosition), typeof(NavPaneGroupButton), new PropertyMetadata(GroupPosition.First, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButton)(object)d).OnGroupPositionPropertyChanged();
			}));
			IsActiveProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsActive", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneGroupButton), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButton)(object)d).OnIsActivePropertyChanged();
			}));
			IsExpandedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsExpanded", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneGroupButton), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButton)(object)d).OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged();
			}));
			IsSplitterVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsSplitterVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneGroupButton), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButton)(object)d).OnIsSplitterVisiblePropertyChanged();
			}));
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavPaneGroupButton), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButton)(object)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			IsMouseOverCoreProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsMouseOverCore", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneGroupButton), new PropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButton)(object)d).OnIsMouseOverPropertyChanged();
			}));
			IsPressedCoreProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsPressedCore", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneGroupButton), new PropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButton)(object)d).OnIsPressedPropertyChanged();
			}));
		}

		public NavPaneGroupButton()
		{
			SetBindings();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).OnApplyTemplate();
			UpdateVisualStates();
		}

		private void OnGroupPositionPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateSplitterVisibilityStates();
		}

		private void OnIsActivePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateActiveState();
		}

		private void OnIsExpandedPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateStateIsExpanded();
		}

		private void OnIsMouseOverPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateCommonStates();
		}

		private void OnIsPressedPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateCommonStates();
		}

		private void OnIsSplitterVisiblePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateSplitterVisibilityStates();
		}

		private void OnOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateStateOrientation();
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowBorderChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			UpdateShowBorderStates();
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(GroupPositionProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("(0)")
			{
				Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarPositionPanel.GroupPositionProperty)
			});
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(IsActiveProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("IsActive"));
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(IsMouseOverCoreProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("IsMouseOver")
			{
				RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.Self)
			});
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(IsPressedCoreProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("IsPressed")
			{
				RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.Self)
			});
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(IsExpandedProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("NavBar.View.IsExpanded"));
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(IsSplitterVisibleProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("NavBar.View.IsSplitterVisible"));
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(OrientationProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("NavBar.View.Orientation"));
			((FrameworkElement)(object)this).SetBinding(ShowBorderProperty, (BindingBase)new Binding("NavBar.View.ShowBorder"));
		}

		private void UpdateActiveState()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, IsActive ? "IsActiveTrue" : "IsActiveFalse", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateCommonStates()
		{
			string stateName = "Normal";
			if (IsPressedCore && !IsActive)
			{
				stateName = "IsPressed";
			}
			else if (IsMouseOverCore && !IsActive)
			{
				stateName = "IsMouseOver";
			}
			VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, stateName, useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateSplitterVisibilityStates()
		{
			string stateName = ((!IsSplitterVisible && (GroupPosition == GroupPosition.First || GroupPosition == GroupPosition.Single)) ? "WithoutSplitter" : "WithSplitter");
			VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, stateName, useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateStateIsExpanded()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, IsExpanded ? "Expanded" : "Collapsed", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateStateOrientation()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? "Horizontal" : "Vertical", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateShowBorderStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState((FrameworkElement)(object)this, ShowBorder ? "WithBorder" : "WithoutBorder", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateVisualStates()
		{
			UpdateActiveState();
			UpdateShowBorderStates();
			UpdateSplitterVisibilityStates();
			UpdateStateIsExpanded();
			UpdateStateOrientation();
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneXPFButton : XPFButton
	{
		private bool emulateClick;

		private TouchPoint point;

		static NavPaneXPFButton()
		{
			UIElement.IsManipulationEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavPaneXPFButton), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
		}

		public NavPaneXPFButton()
		{
			NavBarGroupHelper.SetIsGroupHeader((DependencyObject)(object)this, value: true);
		}

		protected override void OnTouchDown(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			((UIElement)this).OnTouchDown(e);
			point = e.GetTouchPoint((IInputElement)this);
			emulateClick = true;
		}

		protected override void OnTouchMove(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			((UIElement)this).OnTouchMove(e);
			if (point == null || !object.Equals(e.GetTouchPoint((IInputElement)this).Position, point.Position))
			{
				emulateClick = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnTouchUp(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			((UIElement)this).OnTouchUp(e);
			if (!emulateClick)
			{
				return;
			}
			point = null;
			emulateClick = false;
			if (!e.Handled)
			{
				NavBarGroup group = ((FrameworkElement)this).DataContext as NavBarGroup;
				group.With((NavBarGroup x) => x.NavBar).Do(delegate(NavBarControl x)
				{
					x.SelectionStrategy.SelectGroup(group);
				});
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneGroupButtonPanelContentPresenter : ContentPresenter
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty WithoutSplitterThicknessProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty WithSplitterThicknessProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty IsSplitterVisibleProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsControlGroupCountProperty;

		public Thickness WithSplitterThickness
		{
			get
			{
				return (Thickness)GetValue(WithSplitterThicknessProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(WithSplitterThicknessProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Thickness WithoutSplitterThickness
		{
			get
			{
				return (Thickness)GetValue(WithoutSplitterThicknessProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(WithoutSplitterThicknessProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal bool IsSplitterVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsSplitterVisibleProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsSplitterVisibleProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal int ItemsControlGroupCount
		{
			get
			{
				return (int)GetValue(ItemsControlGroupCountProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsControlGroupCountProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavPaneGroupButtonPanelContentPresenter()
		{
			IsSplitterVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsSplitterVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneGroupButtonPanelContentPresenter), new PropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButtonPanelContentPresenter)d).OnIsSplitterVisiblePropertyChanged();
			}));
			ItemsControlGroupCountProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsControlGroupCount", typeof(int), typeof(NavPaneGroupButtonPanelContentPresenter), new PropertyMetadata(0, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneGroupButtonPanelContentPresenter)d).OnItemsControlGroupCountPropertyChanged();
			}));
			WithoutSplitterThicknessProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("WithoutSplitterThickness", typeof(Thickness), typeof(NavPaneGroupButtonPanelContentPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new Thickness(0.0)));
			WithSplitterThicknessProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("WithSplitterThickness", typeof(Thickness), typeof(NavPaneGroupButtonPanelContentPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(new Thickness(0.0)));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			SetBindings();
		}

		private void OnItemsControlGroupCountPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateSplitterBehaviorStates();
		}

		private void OnIsSplitterVisiblePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateSplitterBehaviorStates();
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(IsSplitterVisibleProperty, new Binding("View.IsSplitterVisible"));
			SetBinding(ItemsControlGroupCountProperty, new Binding("View.ItemsControlGroupCount"));
		}

		private void UpdateSplitterBehaviorStates()
		{
			if (ItemsControlGroupCount == 0)
			{
				base.Margin = new Thickness(0.0);
			}
			else
			{
				base.Margin = (IsSplitterVisible ? WithSplitterThickness : WithoutSplitterThickness);
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavBarStackPanel : StackPanel
	{
		private const double DesignTimeDefaultSecondarySize = 100.0;

		private SizeHelperBase SizeHelper => SizeHelperBase.GetDefineSizeHelper(base.Orientation);

		protected double CalcSecondarySize()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (FrameworkElement child in base.Children)
			{
				num = Math.Max(num, SizeHelper.GetSecondarySize(child.DesiredSize));
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected double CalcDesiredSize()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (FrameworkElement child in base.Children)
			{
				num += SizeHelper.GetDefineSize(child.DesiredSize);
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneItemsControlPanel : NavBarPositionPanel
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty VisibleElementCountProperty;

		public int VisibleElementCount
		{
			get
			{
				return (int)GetValue(VisibleElementCountProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(VisibleElementCountProperty, value);
			}
		}

		protected NavPaneItemsControl Owner { get; private set; }

		static NavPaneItemsControlPanel()
		{
			VisibleElementCountProperty = NavPaneItemsControl.VisibleElementCountProperty.AddOwner(typeof(NavPaneItemsControlPanel), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0, OnVisibleElementCountChanged));
		}

		protected static void OnVisibleElementCountChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavPaneItemsControlPanel)d).OnVisibleElementCountChanged(e);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureChildrenOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			SizeHelperBase defineSizeHelper = SizeHelperBase.GetDefineSizeHelper(base.Orientation);
			double num = 0.0;
			int num2 = 0;
			foreach (FrameworkElement child in base.Children)
			{
				NavBarGroup navBarGroup = child.DataContext as NavBarGroup;
				child.Measure(defineSizeHelper.CreateSize(double.PositiveInfinity, defineSizeHelper.GetSecondarySize(availableSize)));
				if (num + defineSizeHelper.GetDefineSize(child.DesiredSize) < defineSizeHelper.GetDefineSize(availableSize))
				{
					num += defineSizeHelper.GetDefineSize(child.DesiredSize);
					num2++;
					if (navBarGroup != null)
					{
						navBarGroup.IsVisibleInOverflowPanel = true;
					}
				}
				else if (navBarGroup != null)
				{
					navBarGroup.IsVisibleInOverflowPanel = false;
				}
			}
			VisibleElementCount = num2;
			return defineSizeHelper.CreateSize(num, CalcSecondarySize());
		}

		protected virtual void OnVisibleElementCountChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (Owner == null)
			{
				Owner = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<NavPaneItemsControl>((DependencyObject)this);
			}
			if (Owner != null)
			{
				Owner.VisibleElementCount = VisibleElementCount;
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Vertical", GroupName = "OrientationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Horizontal", GroupName = "OrientationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Dragging", GroupName = "DraggingStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "NotDragging", GroupName = "DraggingStates")]
	public class NavPaneSplitter : Thumb
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ShowBorderProperty;

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool ShowBorder
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(ShowBorderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ShowBorderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavPaneSplitter()
		{
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavPaneSplitter), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneSplitter)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ShowBorderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ShowBorder", typeof(bool), typeof(NavPaneSplitter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneSplitter)d).OnShowBorderChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}));
			UIElement.IsManipulationEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavPaneSplitter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavPaneSplitter>();
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			UpdateAnimations();
			SetBindings();
			UpdateDraggingStates();
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		protected virtual void OnShowBorderChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(OrientationProperty, new Binding("View.Orientation"));
			SetBinding(ShowBorderProperty, new Binding("View.ShowBorder"));
		}

		private void UpdateAnimations()
		{
			NavBarVisualStateHelper.UpdateStates(this, "OrientationStates");
		}

		private void UpdateDraggingStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, base.IsDragging ? "Dragging" : "NotDragging", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateOrientationStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? "Horizontal" : "Vertical", useTransitions: false);
			string text = ((Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? "Horizontal" : "Vertical");
			text += (ShowBorder ? "WithBorder" : "WithoutBorder");
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, text, useTransitions: false);
		}

		protected override void OnPropertyChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnPropertyChanged(e);
			if (e.Property == Thumb.IsDraggingProperty)
			{
				UpdateDraggingStates();
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavigationPaneViewContent : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ViewProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NavBarProperty;

		public NavigationPaneView View
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavigationPaneView)GetValue(ViewProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ViewProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public NavBarControl NavBar
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarControl)GetValue(NavBarProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(NavBarProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavigationPaneViewContent()
		{
			ViewProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("View", typeof(NavigationPaneView), typeof(NavigationPaneViewContent), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			NavBarProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavBar", typeof(NavBarControl), typeof(NavigationPaneViewContent), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneExpandedChangedEventArgs : RoutedEventArgs
	{
		public bool IsExpanded { get; internal set; }

		public NavPaneExpandedChangedEventArgs(bool isExpanded)
		{
			IsExpanded = isExpanded;
		}

		protected override void InvokeEventHandler(Delegate genericHandler, object genericTarget)
		{
			((NavPaneExpandedChangedEventHandler)genericHandler)(genericTarget, this);
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneExpandedChangingEventArgs : NavPaneExpandedChangedEventArgs
	{
		public bool Cancel { get; set; }

		public NavPaneExpandedChangingEventArgs(bool isExpanded)
			: base(isExpanded)
		{
		}

		protected override void InvokeEventHandler(Delegate genericHandler, object genericTarget)
		{
			((NavPaneExpandedChangingEventHandler)genericHandler)(genericTarget, this);
		}
	}
	public delegate void NavPaneExpandedChangedEventHandler(object sender, NavPaneExpandedChangedEventArgs e);
	public delegate void NavPaneExpandedChangingEventHandler(object sender, NavPaneExpandedChangingEventArgs e);

		Normal,

		Both = 3
	}

		private ActionsList Actions { get; set; }

		protected ContentPresenter NavPaneContentPresenter { get; private set; }

		public DXExpander Expander
		{
			get
			{
				return expander;
			}
			protected set
			{
				if (Expander != value)
				{
					UnSubscribeExpanderEvents();
					expander = value;
					SubscribeExpanderEvents();
				}
			}
		}

		public event NavPaneExpandedChangingEventHandler NavPaneExpandedChanging
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(NavPaneExpandedChangingEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(NavPaneExpandedChangingEvent, value);
			}
		}

		public event NavPaneExpandedChangedEventHandler NavPaneExpandedChanged
		{
			add
			{
				AddHandler(NavPaneExpandedChangedEvent, value);
			}
			remove
			{
				RemoveHandler(NavPaneExpandedChangedEvent, value);
			}
		}

		static NavigationPaneView()
		{
			defaultItemsControlGroupCount = 3;
			MaxVisibleGroupCountProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("MaxVisibleGroupCount", typeof(int), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(defaultItemsControlGroupCount, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure, OnMaxVisibleGroupCountPropertyChanged), (ValidateValueCallback)ValidateMaxVisibleGroupCount);
			ActiveGroupMinHeightProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActiveGroupMinHeight", typeof(double), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(150.0, OnActiveGroupMinHeightChanged), (ValidateValueCallback)ValidateActiveGroupMinHeight);
			IsExpandedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsExpanded", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(true, OnIsExpandedChanged, CoerceIsExpanded));
			GroupButtonTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupButtonTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnGroupButtonTemplateChanged));
			GroupButtonTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupButtonTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnGroupButtonTemplateSelectorChanged));
			GroupButtonControlTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupButtonControlTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			OverflowGroupTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("OverflowGroupTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnOverflowGroupTemplateChanged));
			OverflowGroupTemplateSelectorProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("OverflowGroupTemplateSelector", typeof(DataTemplateSelector), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnOverflowGroupTemplateSelectorChanged));
			OverflowGroupControlTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("OverflowGroupControlTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			IsExpandButtonVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsExpandButtonVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(true));
			IsOverflowPanelVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsOverflowPanelVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(true, OnIsOverFlowPanelVisibleChanged));
			IsSplitterVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsSplitterVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(true, OnIsSplitterVisibleChanged));
			ExpandButtonModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ExpandButtonMode", typeof(ExpandButtonMode), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(ExpandButtonMode.Normal));
			ExpandedTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ExpandedTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			CollapsedTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CollapsedTemplate", typeof(DataTemplate), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			CollapsedActiveGroupControlTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("CollapsedActiveGroupControlTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null));
			ExpandedWidthProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ExpandedWidth", typeof(double), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(double.NaN));
			ItemsControlGroupsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsControlGroups", typeof(ReadOnlyObservableCollection<NavBarGroup>), typeof(NavigationPaneView));
			OverflowPanelGroupsProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("OverflowPanelGroups", typeof(ReadOnlyObservableCollection<NavBarGroup>), typeof(NavigationPaneView));
			ItemsControlGroupCountProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsControlGroupCount", typeof(int), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(defaultItemsControlGroupCount, ItemsControlGroupCountPropertyChanged));
			ElementProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("Element", typeof(Element), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Element.ActiveGroup));
			ContentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("Content", typeof(NavigationPaneViewContent), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null));
			NavPaneExpandedChangingEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("NavPaneExpandedChangingEvent", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(NavPaneExpandedChangingEventHandler), typeof(NavigationPaneView));
			NavPaneExpandedChangedEvent = EventManager.RegisterRoutedEvent("NavPaneExpandedChangedEvent", RoutingStrategy.Direct, typeof(NavPaneExpandedChangedEventHandler), typeof(NavigationPaneView));
			ItemVisualStyleInPopupProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemVisualStyleInPopup", typeof(Style), typeof(NavigationPaneView), new PropertyMetadata(null, OnItemVisualStyleInPopupPropertyChanged));
			MaxPopupWidthProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("MaxPopupWidth", typeof(double), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(double.PositiveInfinity));
			ActualIsOverflowPanelVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActualIsOverflowPanelVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			ActualIsSplitterVisibleProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActualIsSplitterVisible", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			IsPopupOpenProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsPopupOpen", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavigationPaneView)d).OnIsPopupOpenChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
			}, CoerceOnPopupOpen));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(NavigationPaneView), new CommandBinding(NavigationPaneCommands.ChangeNavPaneExpanded, OnChangeNavPaneExpanded));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(NavigationPaneView), new CommandBinding(NavigationPaneCommands.ShowMoreGroups, OnShowMoreGroups, delegate(object d, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavigationPaneView)d).CanShowMoreGroups(e);
			}));
			CommandManager.RegisterClassCommandBinding(typeof(NavigationPaneView), new CommandBinding(NavigationPaneCommands.ShowFewerGroups, OnShowFewerGroups, delegate(object d, CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavigationPaneView)d).CanShowFewerGroups(e);
			}));
			NavigationAutomationPeersCreator.Default.RegisterObject(typeof(NavigationPaneView), typeof(NavigationPaneViewAutomationPeer), (CreateObjectMethod<AutomationPeer>)((object owner) => (AutomationPeer)(object)new NavigationPaneViewAutomationPeer((NavigationPaneView)owner)));
			ActualMaxVisibleGroupCountPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ActualMaxVisibleGroupCount", typeof(int), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(defaultItemsControlGroupCount, OnActualMaxVisibleGroupCountChanged));
			ActualMaxVisibleGroupCountProperty = ActualMaxVisibleGroupCountPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			IsCompactPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("IsCompact", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnIsCompactChanged));
			IsCompactProperty = IsCompactPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			EachCollapsedGroupHasSelectedItemProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("EachCollapsedGroupHasSelectedItem", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
			ActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItemProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem", typeof(object), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.BindsTwoWayByDefault, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavigationPaneView)d).OnActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItemChanged(e);
			}));
			PeekFormShowModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("PeekFormShowMode", typeof(PeekFormShowMode), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(PeekFormShowMode.Both));
			PeekFormShowDelayProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("PeekFormShowDelay", typeof(int), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(700));
			PeekFormHideDelayProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("PeekFormHideDelay", typeof(int), typeof(NavigationPaneView), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(400));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavigationPaneView>();
		}

		private static object CoerceOnPopupOpen(DependencyObject d, object baseValue)
		{
			NavBarControl navBar = ((NavigationPaneView)d).NavBar;
			if (navBar.ActiveGroup != null && navBar.ActiveGroup.NavPaneShowMode != ShowMode.Items)
			{
				return !((NavigationPaneView)d).IsExpanded && (bool)baseValue;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static void OnIsSplitterVisibleChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneView)d).UpdateActualIsSplitterVisible();
		}

		private static void OnIsOverFlowPanelVisibleChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneView)d).UpdateActualIsOverflowPanelVisible();
		}

		private static void OnIsCompactChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if ((bool)e.NewValue)
			{
				((NavigationPaneView)d).ActualMaxVisibleGroupCount = 0;
			}
			else
			{
				((NavigationPaneView)d).ActualMaxVisibleGroupCount = ((NavigationPaneView)d).MaxVisibleGroupCount;
			}
		}

		private void OnActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItemChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (CollapsedNavPaneSelectionStrategy.IsValidItem(ActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem))
			{
				CollapsedNavPaneSelectionStrategy.SelectItem(ActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem);
			}
			else
			{
				CollapsedNavPaneSelectionStrategy.UnselectItem(base.NavBar.ActiveGroup);
			}
			UpdateActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem();
		}

		private static void ItemsControlGroupCountPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			NavigationPaneView navigationPaneView = (NavigationPaneView)d;
			navigationPaneView.UpdateView();
		}

		private static void InvalidateMeasureVisalTree(FrameworkElement elem, FrameworkElement root)
		{
			while (elem != root && elem != null)
			{
				elem.InvalidateMeasure();
				elem = VisualTreeHelper.GetParent(elem) as FrameworkElement;
			}
			root.InvalidateMeasure();
		}

		private static void OnMaxVisibleGroupCountPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			_ = ((NavigationPaneView)d).IsInternalSetMaxVisibleGroupCount;
			if (!((NavigationPaneView)d).IsCompact)
			{
				((NavigationPaneView)d).ActualMaxVisibleGroupCount = (int)e.NewValue;
			}
		}

		private static void OnActualMaxVisibleGroupCountChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneView)d).ItemsControlGroupCount = (int)e.NewValue;
			((NavigationPaneView)d).UpdateView();
		}

		private static bool ValidateMaxVisibleGroupCount(object d)
		{
			return (int)d >= 0;
		}

		private static void OnActiveGroupMinHeightChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneView)d).UpdateView();
		}

		private static bool ValidateActiveGroupMinHeight(object d)
		{
			return (double)d >= 0.0;
		}

		private static void OnIsExpandedChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneView)d).OnIsExpandedChanged();
		}

		private static object CoerceIsExpanded(DependencyObject d, object value)
		{
			return ((NavigationPaneView)d).CoerceIsExpanded((bool)value);
		}

		private static void OnGroupButtonTemplateChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneView)d).UpdateActualGroupButtonTemplateSelector();
		}

		private static void OnChangeNavPaneExpanded(object sender, ExecutedRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneView)sender).ChangeNavPaneExpanded();
		}

		private static void OnGroupButtonTemplateSelectorChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)

			((NavigationPaneView)d).UpdateActualGroupButtonTemplateSelector();
		}

		private static void OnOverflowGroupTemplateChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneView)d).UpdateActualOverflowGroupTemplateSelector();
		}

		}

			((NavigationPaneView)d).OnItemVisualStyleInPopupChanged();
		}

			((NavigationPaneView)sender).ShowMoreGroups(e.Parameter);
		}

			((NavigationPaneView)sender).ShowFewerGroups(e.Parameter);
		}

		public static void SetElement(DependencyObject d, Element element)
		{
			d.SetValue(ElementProperty, element);
		}

		public static Element GetElement(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (Element)d.GetValue(ElementProperty);
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsPopupOpenedChanged()
		{
			UpdateActualItemVisualStyle();
		}

		private void CreateBindings()
		{
			Binding binding = new Binding("IsExpanded")
			{
				Source = this,
				Converter = new IsActiveToAnimationProgressConverter()
			};
			BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, NavBarAnimationOptions.AnimationProgressProperty, binding);
			if (Window.GetWindow(this) != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, MaxPopupWidthProperty, new Binding("ActualWidth")
				{
					RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.FindAncestor, typeof(Window), 1),
					Converter = new DoubleDivisionConverter(),
					ConverterParameter = 2.0
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnActualVisibleGroupCountChanged()
		{
			UpdateOverflowControlGroupsCollection();
		}

		private void UpdateActualIsSplitterVisible()
		{
			ActualIsSplitterVisible = IsSplitterVisible && IsSplitterVisibleInternal;
		}

		private void UpdateActualIsOverflowPanelVisible()
		{
			ActualIsOverflowPanelVisible = IsOverflowPanelVisibleInternal && IsOverflowPanelVisible;
		}

		private double GetExpandedWidthCore()
		{
			if (!double.IsNaN(ExpandedWidth))
			{
				return ExpandedWidth;
			}
			if (double.IsNaN(expandedWidthCore))
			{
				return double.NaN;
			}
			if (Panel == null || ExpandInfoProvider.IsExpanding || ExpandInfoProvider.IsJustExpanded)
			{
				return expandedWidthCore;
			}
			if (!(Panel.ActiveGroup is NavPaneActiveGroupControl navPaneActiveGroupControl))
			{
				return expandedWidthCore;
			}
			return Math.Max(GetSizeHelper().GetSecondarySize(navPaneActiveGroupControl.desiredInfiniteSize), expandedWidthCore);
		}

		public NavigationPaneView()
		{
			UpdateActualGroupButtonTemplateSelector();
			UpdateActualOverflowGroupTemplateSelector();
			Actions = new ActionsList();
			Actions.Add(new UpdateItemsControlGroupsAction(this));
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
			base.Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
			base.ActiveGroupChanged += OnActiveGroupChanged;
			base.NavBarViewKind = NavBarViewKind.NavigationPane;
			ExpandInfoProvider = new NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider(this);
			onMenuManagerBeforeCheckCloseAllPopups = OnMenuManagerBeforeCheckCloseAllPopups;
			CreateBindings();
			UpdateActualIsSplitterVisible();
			UpdateActualIsOverflowPanelVisible();
		}

		private void OnActiveGroupChanged(object sender, NavBarActiveGroupChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem();
		}

		private void UpdateActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem()
		{
			if (base.NavBar != null && base.NavBar.ActiveGroup != null)
			{
				ActiveGroupCollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem = base.NavBar.ActiveGroup.CollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem;
			}
		}

		protected internal virtual SizeHelperBase GetSizeHelper()
		{
			return SizeHelperBase.GetDefineSizeHelper(base.Orientation);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (IsExpanded && !ExpandInfoProvider.IsJustExpanded && Expander.AnimationProgress == 1.0 && !IsInfiniteWidth(availableSize))
			{
				ExpandedWidthCore = ((!double.IsNaN(ExpandedWidth)) ? ExpandedWidth : ((base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? availableSize.Width : availableSize.Height));
			}
			measureByInfinityChanged = IsExpanded && !ExpandInfoProvider.IsExpanding && !ExpandInfoProvider.IsCollapsing && !ExpandInfoProvider.IsJustCollapsed && !ExpandInfoProvider.IsJustExpanded && double.IsNaN(ExpandedWidth);
			if (measureByInfinityChanged)
			{
				measureByInfinityChanged &= lastMeasureSize.IsEmpty || ((base.Orientation != Orientation.Vertical) ? (double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Height) && !double.IsInfinity(lastMeasureSize.Height)) : (double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Width) && !double.IsInfinity(lastMeasureSize.Width)));
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					measureByInfinityChanged = false;
				});
			}
			bool flag = !double.IsNaN(ExpandedWidthCore) && !measureByInfinityChanged;
			double width = availableSize.Width;
			double height = availableSize.Height;
			if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				width = (flag ? Math.Min(ExpandedWidthCore, availableSize.Width) : availableSize.Width);
			}
			else
			{
				height = (flag ? Math.Min(ExpandedWidthCore, availableSize.Height) : availableSize.Height);
			}
			return base.MeasureOverride(new Size(width, height));
		}

		private bool IsInfiniteWidth(Size availableSize)
		{
			if (base.Orientation != Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				return double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Height);
			}
			return double.IsInfinity(availableSize.Width);
		}

		protected override void OnNavBarChanged(DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnNavBarChanged(e);
			Content = new NavigationPaneViewContent
			{
				View = this,
				NavBar = base.NavBar
			};
		}

		private void CanShowMoreGroups(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = ActualVisibleGroupCount < ItemsControlGroups.Count;
		}

		private void CanShowFewerGroups(CanExecuteRoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			e.CanExecute = ActualVisibleGroupCount > 0;
		}

		private void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.NavBar != null)
			{
				base.LayoutUpdated += NavigationPaneView_LayoutUpdated;
				UpdateState();
			}
		}

		private void OnUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.LayoutUpdated -= NavigationPaneView_LayoutUpdated;
			base.ActiveGroupChanged -= OnActiveGroupChanged;
		}

		private void NavigationPaneView_LayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			Actions.Execute(this);
		}

		internal override void SetNavBar(NavBarControl navBar)
		{
			base.SetNavBar(navBar);
			UpdateActualGroupButtonTemplateSelector();
			UpdateActualOverflowGroupTemplateSelector();
		}

		public void ChangeNavPaneExpanded()
		{
			IsExpanded = !IsExpanded;
		}

		public void ShowMoreGroups(object parameter)
		{
			int num = ((parameter == null) ? 1 : ((int)parameter));
			InternalSetMaxVisibleGroupCount(Math.Min(ItemsControlGroups.Count, MaxVisibleGroupCount + num));
		}

		public void ShowFewerGroups(object parameter)
		{
			int num = ((parameter == null) ? 1 : ((int)parameter));
			InternalSetMaxVisibleGroupCount(Math.Max(0, MaxVisibleGroupCount - num));
		}

		internal void InternalSetMaxVisibleGroupCount(int value)
		{
			IsInternalSetMaxVisibleGroupCount = true;
			MaxVisibleGroupCount = value;
			IsInternalSetMaxVisibleGroupCount = false;
			UpdateView();
		}

		private bool ContainsItems(IList list)
		{
			if (list != null)
			{
				return list.Count > 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void UpdateItemsControlGroupsCollection()
		{
			Actions.Add(new UpdateItemsControlGroupsAction(this));
		}

		private void UpdateOverflowControlGroupsCollection()
		{
			Actions.Add(new UpdateOverflowGroupsAction(this));
		}

		private void OnIsExpandedChanged()
		{
			RaiseEvent(new NavPaneExpandedChangedEventArgs(IsExpanded)
			{
				RoutedEvent = NavPaneExpandedChangedEvent
			});
			if (base.NavBar != null)
			{
				base.NavBar.RaiseIsExpandedChanged(!IsExpanded, IsExpanded);
			}
			UpdateExpandedWidthCore();
			UpdateAnimationMinHeightWidth();
			ResetWidthProperty();
			UpdatePresenterTemplateByEnabledState();
			UpdateVisualStateByExpandState();
		}

		internal void UpdateGroupsDisplayMode(DisplayMode mode)
		{
			if (base.NavBar == null || base.NavBar.Groups == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (NavBarGroup group in base.NavBar.Groups)
			{
				if (group.ImageSource != null)
				{
					if (mode == DisplayMode.Text)
					{
						NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider.SetIsCompleteCollapsed((DependencyObject)(object)group, value: false);
					}
					else
					{
						NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider.SetIsCompleteCollapsed((DependencyObject)(object)group, value: true);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateAnimationMinHeightWidth()
		{
			if (IsExpanded)
			{
				return;
			}
			double num = 24.0;
			if (Panel != null && Panel.GroupsControl != null)
			{
				foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)Panel.GroupsControl.Items)
				{
					NavBarGroup navBarGroup = (item as NavBarGroup) ?? (new FrameworkElementInfoSLCompatibilityConverterExtension
					{
						ConvertToInfo = false
					}.Convert(item) as NavBarGroup);
					if (navBarGroup != null)
					{
						num = Math.Max((base.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? navBarGroup.ActualImageSettings.Height : navBarGroup.ActualImageSettings.Width, num);
					}
				}
			}
			NavBarAnimationOptions.SetMinHeight(this, num + 12.0);
			NavBarAnimationOptions.SetMinWidth(this, num + 12.0);
		}

		private void ResetWidthProperty()
		{
			if (base.NavBar == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				base.NavBar.Width = double.NaN;
			}
			else
			{
				base.NavBar.Height = double.NaN;
			}
			if (IsExpanded && storedMinWidth != null)
			{
				if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
				{
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.NavBar, FrameworkElement.MinWidthProperty, storedMinWidth);
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.NavBar, FrameworkElement.MaxWidthProperty, storedMaxWidth);
				}
				else
				{
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.NavBar, FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty, storedMinWidth);
					DependencyObjectExtensions.RestorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.NavBar, FrameworkElement.MaxHeightProperty, storedMaxWidth);
				}
				storedMinWidth = null;
				storedMaxWidth = null;
			}
		}

		private bool CoerceIsExpanded(bool newValue)
		{
			return CoerceIsExpandedCore(newValue, IsExpanded);
		}

		private void SubscribeExpanderEvents()
		{
			//IL_0026: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0030: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ExpandInfoProvider.Expander = Expander;
			if (Expander != null)
			{
				Expander.GetExpandCollapseInfo += new ExpandCollapseInfoEventHandler(OnGetExpandCollapseInfo);
			}
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size arrangeBounds)
		{
			if (IsExpanded)
			{
				arrangeBounds = ((base.Orientation != Orientation.Vertical) ? new Size(arrangeBounds.Width, double.IsNaN(ExpandedWidth) ? arrangeBounds.Height : Math.Min(ExpandedWidth, arrangeBounds.Height)) : new Size(double.IsNaN(ExpandedWidth) ? arrangeBounds.Width : Math.Min(ExpandedWidth, arrangeBounds.Width), arrangeBounds.Height));
			}
			Size result = base.ArrangeOverride(arrangeBounds);
			if (Expander == null)
			{
				return result;
			}
			if (IsExpanded && Expander.AnimationProgress == 1.0)
			{
				ExpandedWidthCore = ((!double.IsNaN(ExpandedWidth)) ? ExpandedWidth : ((base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? arrangeBounds.Width : arrangeBounds.Height));
			}
			if (!IsExpanded && Expander.AnimationProgress == 0.0 && storedMinWidth == null)
			{
				if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
				{
					storedMinWidth = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.NavBar, FrameworkElement.MinWidthProperty);
					storedMaxWidth = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.NavBar, FrameworkElement.MaxWidthProperty);
					base.NavBar.MaxWidth = arrangeBounds.Width + base.Margin.Left + base.Margin.Right;
					base.NavBar.MinWidth = arrangeBounds.Width + base.Margin.Left + base.Margin.Right;
				}
				else
				{
					storedMinWidth = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.NavBar, FrameworkElement.MinHeightProperty);
					storedMaxWidth = DependencyObjectExtensions.StorePropertyValue((DependencyObject)base.NavBar, FrameworkElement.MaxHeightProperty);
					base.NavBar.MaxHeight = arrangeBounds.Height + base.Margin.Top + base.Margin.Bottom;
					base.NavBar.MinHeight = arrangeBounds.Height + base.Margin.Top + base.Margin.Bottom;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void OnGetExpandCollapseInfo(object sender, ExpandCollapseInfoEventArgs e)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(ExpandedWidthCore) || measureByInfinityChanged)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				if (double.IsInfinity(e.Size.Width))
				{
					e.Size = new Size(ExpandedWidthCore, e.Size.Height);
				}
			}
			else if (double.IsInfinity(e.Size.Height))
			{
				e.Size = new Size(e.Size.Width, ExpandedWidthCore);
			}
		}

		private void UnSubscribeExpanderEvents()
		{
			//IL_0015: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_001f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (Expander != null)
			{
				Expander.GetExpandCollapseInfo -= new ExpandCollapseInfoEventHandler(OnGetExpandCollapseInfo);
			}
		}

		private void UpdateExpandedWidthCore()
		{
			if (!IsExpanded && !ExpandInfoProvider.IsExpanding)
			{
				double num = ((base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? base.ActualWidth : base.ActualHeight);
				ExpandedWidthCore = ((!double.IsNaN(ExpandedWidth)) ? ExpandedWidth : ((num == 0.0) ? double.NaN : num));
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			DependencyObject templateChild = GetTemplateChild("PART_DXExpander");
			Expander = (DXExpander)(object)((templateChild is DXExpander) ? templateChild : null);
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			NavPaneContentPresenter = GetTemplateChild("PART_NavPaneContentPresenter") as ContentPresenter;
			UpdatePresenterTemplateByEnabledState();
			bool menuManagerFound = false;
			bool popupFound = false;
			bool collapsedGoupFound = false;
			base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
			{
				LayoutHelper.FindElement((FrameworkElement)this, (Predicate<FrameworkElement>)delegate(FrameworkElement fe)
				{
					if (fe is NavPanePopup)
					{
						navPanePopup = fe as NavPanePopup;
					}
					if (fe is CollapsedActiveGroup)
					{
						collapsedActiveGroup = fe as CollapsedActiveGroup;
					}
					if (fe is CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement)
					{
						collapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement = fe as CollapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement;
					}
					return menuManagerFound && popupFound && collapsedGoupFound;
				});
			});
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnMenuManagerBeforeCheckCloseAllPopups(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (collapsedActiveGroup != null && navPanePopup != null && navPanePopup.IsOpen)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				flag = navPanePopup.IsMouseOver;
				if (!(collapsedActiveGroup.IsMouseOver || (collapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement != null && collapsedActiveGroupDefaultElement.IsMouseOver) || flag))
				{
					PopupControlService.Instance.UpdateState((Popup)navPanePopup, false);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdatePresenterTemplateByEnabledState()
		{
			if (NavPaneContentPresenter != null)
			{
				Binding binding = new Binding(IsExpanded ? "ExpandedTemplate" : "CollapsedTemplate");
				binding.Source = this;
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(NavPaneContentPresenter, ContentPresenter.ContentTemplateProperty, binding);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void UpdateVisualStateByExpandState()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, IsExpanded ? "Expanded" : "Collapsed", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private bool CoerceIsExpandedCore(bool newValue, bool oldValue)
		{
			if (oldValue != newValue)
			{
				NavPaneExpandedChangingEventArgs e = new NavPaneExpandedChangingEventArgs(newValue);
				e.RoutedEvent = NavPaneExpandedChangingEvent;
				RaiseEvent(e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					return oldValue;
				}
			}
			return newValue;
		}

		private void UpdateActualGroupButtonTemplateSelector()
		{
			if (base.NavBar != null)
			{
				base.NavBar.UpdateGroups(delegate(NavBarGroup w)
				{
					w.UpdateActualNavPaneGroupButtonTemplateSelector();
				});
			}
		}

		private void UpdateActualOverflowGroupTemplateSelector()
		{
			if (base.NavBar != null)
			{
				base.NavBar.UpdateGroups(delegate(NavBarGroup w)
				{
					w.UpdateActualNavPaneOverflowGroupTemplateSelector();
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemVisualStyleInPopupChanged()
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsPopupOpenChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
		}

		private void UpdateState()
		{
			if (base.NavBar != null)
			{
				UpdateViewForce();
				UpdatePresenterTemplateByEnabledState();
				UpdateVisualStateByExpandState();
			}
		}

		protected internal override void UpdateView()
		{
			base.UpdateView();
			UpdateItemsControlGroupsCollection();
			UpdateOverflowControlGroupsCollection();
		}

		protected internal override void UpdateViewForce()
		{
			base.UpdateViewForce();
			UpdateView();
			Actions.Execute(this);
		}
	}
	internal interface IAction
	{
		bool IsSimilar(IAction action);

		void Execute(FrameworkElement panel);
	}
	internal class ActionsList : IAction
	{
		private List<IAction> actions = new List<IAction>();

		public int Count => actions.Count;

		public void Execute(FrameworkElement panel)
		{
			foreach (IAction action in actions)
			{
				action.Execute(panel);
			}
			actions.Clear();
		}

		public void Add(IAction action)
		{
			for (int num = actions.Count - 1; num >= 0; num--)
			{
				IAction action2 = actions[num];
				if (action2.IsSimilar(action))
				{
					actions.RemoveAt(num);
				}
			}
			actions.Add(action);
		}

		public bool IsSimilar(IAction action)
		{
			return false;
		}
	}
	internal abstract class ChangeCollectionAction : IAction
	{
		protected NavigationPaneView View { get; set; }

		public ChangeCollectionAction(NavigationPaneView view)
		{
			View = view;
		}

		public abstract void Execute(FrameworkElement panel);

		public bool IsSimilar(IAction action)
		{
			return action.GetType().Equals(GetType());
		}
	}
	internal class UpdateItemsControlGroupsAction : ChangeCollectionAction
	{
		public UpdateItemsControlGroupsAction(NavigationPaneView view)
			: base(view)
		{
		}

		public override void Execute(FrameworkElement panel)
		{
			ObservableCollection<NavBarGroup> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<NavBarGroup>();
			for (int i = 0; i < base.View.NavBar.Groups.Count; i++)
			{
				NavBarGroup navBarGroup = base.View.NavBar.Groups[i];
				if (navBarGroup.IsNavPaneGroup)
				{
					observableCollection.Add(navBarGroup);
				}
			}
			base.View.ItemsControlGroups = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<NavBarGroup>(observableCollection);
		}
	}
	internal class UpdateOverflowGroupsAction : ChangeCollectionAction
	{
		public UpdateOverflowGroupsAction(NavigationPaneView view)
			: base(view)
		{
		}

		public override void Execute(FrameworkElement panel)
		{
			List<NavBarGroup> list = new List<NavBarGroup>(base.View.NavBar.Groups.Where((NavBarGroup group) => group.IsNavPaneGroup));
			ObservableCollection<NavBarGroup> observableCollection = new ObservableCollection<NavBarGroup>();
			for (int num = base.View.ActualVisibleGroupCount; num < list.Count; num++)
			{
				NavBarGroup navBarGroup = list[num];
				if (navBarGroup.IsNavPaneGroup)
				{
					observableCollection.Add(navBarGroup);
				}
			}
			base.View.OverflowPanelGroups = new ReadOnlyObservableCollection<NavBarGroup>(observableCollection);
			foreach (NavBarGroup group in base.View.NavBar.Groups)
			{
				group.IsOverflowGroup = base.View.OverflowPanelGroups.Contains(group);
				group.UpdateMenuGroupItem();
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsCompleteCollapsedProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("IsCompleteCollapsed", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider), new PropertyMetadata(false));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsJustCollapsedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsJustCollapsed", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsCollapsingProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsCollapsing", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnIsCollapsingPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsJustExpandedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsJustExpanded", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsExpandedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsExpanded", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsExpandingProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("IsExpanding", typeof(bool), typeof(NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, OnIsExpandingPropertyChanged));

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ExpanderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Expander", typeof(DXExpander), typeof(NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnExpanderPropertyChanged));

		public DXExpander Expander
		{
			get
			{
				//IL_000b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
				//IL_0011: Expected O, but got Unknown
				return (DXExpander)GetValue(ExpanderProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ExpanderProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsExpanding
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsExpandingProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsExpandingProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsCollapsing
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsCollapsingProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsCollapsingProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsJustCollapsed
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsJustCollapsedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsJustCollapsedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsJustExpanded
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsJustExpandedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsJustExpandedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsExpanded
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(IsExpandedProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(IsExpandedProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public NavigationPaneView View { get; set; }

		public static bool GetIsCompleteCollapsed(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return (bool)obj.GetValue(IsCompleteCollapsedProperty);
		}

		public static void SetIsCompleteCollapsed(DependencyObject obj, bool value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(IsCompleteCollapsedProperty, value);
		}

		public NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider(NavigationPaneView view)
		{
			View = view;
		}

		protected static void OnIsCollapsingPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider)d).OnIsCollapsingChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected static void OnIsExpandingPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider)d).OnIsExpandingChanged((bool)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected static void OnExpanderPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			//IL_000d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0017: Expected O, but got Unknown
			((NavigationPaneExpandInfoProvider)d).OnExpanderChanged((DXExpander)e.OldValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnExpanderChanged(DXExpander oldValue)
		{
			ClearValue(IsExpandedProperty);
			ClearValue(IsExpandingProperty);
			ClearValue(IsCollapsingProperty);
			if (Expander != null)
			{
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, IsExpandedProperty, new Binding("IsExpanded")
				{
					Source = Expander
				});
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, IsExpandingProperty, new Binding("Expanding")
				{
					Source = Expander
				});
				BindingOperations.SetBinding(this, IsCollapsingProperty, new Binding("Collapsing")
				{
					Source = Expander
				});
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsExpandingChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			if (!IsExpanding && oldValue)
			{
				IsJustExpanded = true;
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					IsJustExpanded = false;
				});
			}
			if (IsExpanding)
			{
				View.UpdateGroupsDisplayMode(DisplayMode.Text);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnIsCollapsingChanged(bool oldValue)
		{
			if (!IsCollapsing && oldValue)
			{
				IsJustCollapsed = true;
				base.Dispatcher.BeginInvoke((Action)delegate
				{
					IsJustCollapsed = false;
				});
				View.UpdateGroupsDisplayMode(DisplayMode.ImageAndText);
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavPanePopup : Popup
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActualParentProperty;

		private bool? menuDropAlignment;

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public FrameworkElement ActualParent
		{
			get
			{
				return (FrameworkElement)GetValue(ActualParentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ActualParentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavPanePopup()
		{
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavPanePopup), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPanePopup)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ActualParentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActualParent", typeof(FrameworkElement), typeof(NavPanePopup), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPanePopup)d).OnActualParentPropertyChanged();
			}));
		}

		public NavPanePopup()
		{
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
			base.Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
		}

		public void ClosePopup()
		{
		}

		private void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseLeftButtonDown), handledEventsToo: true);
		}

		private void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			CollapsedActiveGroup.AllowCollapsed = false;
		}

		private void OnOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateHeightProperty();
		}

		private void OnActualParentPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateHeightProperty();
		}

		private void OnUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseLeftButtonDown));
		}

		protected override void OnOpened(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnOpened(e);
			UpdateHorizontalOffset();
		}

		internal void UpdateHorizontalOffset()
		{
			if (!menuDropAlignment.HasValue || (menuDropAlignment.HasValue && menuDropAlignment.Value != SystemParameters.MenuDropAlignment))
			{
				if (SystemParameters.MenuDropAlignment)
				{
					base.HorizontalOffset = (base.PlacementTarget as FrameworkElement).ActualWidth + (base.Child as FrameworkElement).RenderSize.Width;
				}
				else
				{
					base.HorizontalOffset = 1.0;
				}
				menuDropAlignment = SystemParameters.MenuDropAlignment;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateHeightProperty()
		{
			if (ActualParent != null)
			{
				string path = ((Orientation == Orientation.Vertical) ? "ActualHeight" : "ActualWidth");
				SetBinding(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, new Binding(path)
				{
					Source = ActualParent
				});
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Vertical", GroupName = "OrientationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Horizontal", GroupName = "OrientationStates")]
	public class CollapsedActiveGroup : ToggleButton
	{
		public static bool AllowCollapsed;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty ElementProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		internal Element Element
		{
			get
			{
				return (Element)GetValue(ElementProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ElementProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private UIElement Root { get; set; }

		public CollapsedActiveGroup()
		{
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
			base.Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
		}

		static CollapsedActiveGroup()
		{
			AllowCollapsed = true;
			ElementProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Element", typeof(Element), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroup), new PropertyMetadata(Element.ActiveGroup, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollapsedActiveGroup)d).OnElementPropertyChanged();
			}));
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(CollapsedActiveGroup), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((CollapsedActiveGroup)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			SetBindings();
			UpdateForegroundStates();
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		private UIElement GetRootElement()
		{
			return LayoutHelper.GetRoot((FrameworkElement)this);
		}

		private void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Subscribe();
		}

		private void OnMouseLeftButtonDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			AllowCollapsed = false;
		}

		private void OnMouseLeftButtonDownOnRootVisual(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			if (AllowCollapsed && base.IsChecked.HasValue && base.IsChecked.Value)
			{
				base.IsChecked = false;
			}
		}

		private void OnMouseLeftButtonUpOnRootVisual(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			AllowCollapsed = true;
		}

		private void ClosePopupOnWindowAction(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			base.IsChecked = false;
			AllowCollapsed = true;
		}

		private void OnElementPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateForegroundStates();
		}

		private void OnOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		private void OnUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			Unsubscribe();
		}

		private void Subscribe()
		{
			Unsubscribe();
			UIElement uIElement = (Root = GetRootElement());
			if (uIElement != null)
			{
				if (uIElement is Window window)
				{
					window.Activated += ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
					window.Deactivated += ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
					window.StateChanged += ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
					window.SizeChanged += ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
					window.LocationChanged += ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
				}
				uIElement.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseLeftButtonDownOnRootVisual), handledEventsToo: true);
				uIElement.AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonUpEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseLeftButtonUpOnRootVisual), handledEventsToo: true);
				AddHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseLeftButtonDown), handledEventsToo: true);
			}
		}

		private void Unsubscribe()
		{
			UIElement root = Root;
			Root = null;
			if (root != null)
			{
				if (root is Window window)
				{
					window.Activated -= ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
					window.Deactivated -= ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
					window.StateChanged -= ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
					window.SizeChanged -= ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
					window.LocationChanged -= ClosePopupOnWindowAction;
				}
				root.RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseLeftButtonDownOnRootVisual));
				root.RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonUpEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseLeftButtonUpOnRootVisual));
				RemoveHandler(UIElement.MouseLeftButtonDownEvent, new MouseButtonEventHandler(OnMouseLeftButtonDown));
			}
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(ElementProperty, new Binding("(0)")
			{
				Path = new PropertyPath(NavigationPaneView.ElementProperty),
				RelativeSource = new RelativeSource(RelativeSourceMode.Self)
			});
			SetBinding(OrientationProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.Orientation"));
			SetBinding(UIElement.OpacityProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.Expander.AnimationProgress")
			{
				Converter = new DoubleInvertConverter
				{
					MaxValue = 1.0
				}
			});
		}

		protected internal void UpdateForegroundStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, (Element == Element.CollapsedActiveGroup) ? "CustomForeground" : "NormalForeground", useTransitions: false);
		}

		private void UpdateOrientationStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal) ? "Horizontal" : "Vertical", useTransitions: false);
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "NormalMode", GroupName = "ExpandModeStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Invert", GroupName = "ExpandModeStates")]
	public class NavPaneExpandButton : ExpandButtonBase
	{
		internal static readonly DependencyProperty ExpandButtonModeProperty;

		private bool emulateClick;

		private TouchPoint point;

		internal ExpandButtonMode ExpandButtonMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (ExpandButtonMode)GetValue(ExpandButtonModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ExpandButtonModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavPaneExpandButton()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavPaneExpandButton>();
			ExpandButtonModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ExpandButtonMode", typeof(ExpandButtonMode), typeof(NavPaneExpandButton), new PropertyMetadata(ExpandButtonMode.Normal, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavPaneExpandButton)d).OnExpandButtonModePropertyChanged();
			}));
			UIElement.IsManipulationEnabledProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavPaneExpandButton), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			NavBarControl navBarControl = NavBarViewBase.FindAncestorCore<NavBarControl>(this);
			if (navBarControl != null)
			{
				SetBinding(ExpandButtonModeProperty, new Binding("View.ExpandButtonMode")
				{
					Source = navBarControl
				});
			}
			UpdateExpandModeStates();
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnExpandButtonModePropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateExpandModeStates();
		}

		private void UpdateExpandModeStates()
		{
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, (ExpandButtonMode == ExpandButtonMode.Normal) ? "NormalMode" : "Invert", useTransitions: false);
		}

		protected internal override void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(ExpandButtonBase.IsExpandedProperty, new Binding("NavBar.View.IsExpanded"));
		}

		protected override void OnTouchDown(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTouchDown(e);
			point = e.GetTouchPoint(this);
			emulateClick = true;
		}

		protected override void OnTouchMove(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTouchMove(e);
			if (point == null || !object.Equals(e.GetTouchPoint(this).Position, point.Position))
			{
				emulateClick = false;
			}
		}

		protected override void OnTouchUp(TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnTouchUp(e);
			if (emulateClick)
			{
				point = null;
				emulateClick = false;
				if (!e.Handled)
				{
					OnClick();
					e.Handled = true;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneContentPresenter : DXContentPresenter
	{
		private const string DefaultTemplateXAML = "<ControlTemplate TargetType='local:DXContentPresenter' xmlns='http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation' xmlns:x='http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml' xmlns:local='clr-namespace:DevExpress.Xpf.Core;assembly=DevExpress.Xpf.Core.v23.2'><ContentPresenter Content='{TemplateBinding Content}' ContentTemplate='{TemplateBinding ContentTemplate}' DataContext='{x:Null}' FlowDirection='{TemplateBinding FlowDirection}'/></ControlTemplate>";

		private static ControlTemplate _DefaultTemplate;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActualContentProperty;

		private static ControlTemplate DefaultTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				if (_DefaultTemplate == null)
				{
					_DefaultTemplate = (ControlTemplate)XamlReader.Parse("<ControlTemplate TargetType='local:DXContentPresenter' xmlns='http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml/presentation' xmlns:x='http://schemas.microsoft.com/winfx/2006/xaml' xmlns:local='clr-namespace:DevExpress.Xpf.Core;assembly=DevExpress.Xpf.Core.v23.2'><ContentPresenter Content='{TemplateBinding Content}' ContentTemplate='{TemplateBinding ContentTemplate}' DataContext='{x:Null}' FlowDirection='{TemplateBinding FlowDirection}'/></ControlTemplate>");
				}
				return _DefaultTemplate;
			}
		}

		public object ActualContent
		{
			get
			{
				return ((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ActualContentProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ActualContentProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavPaneContentPresenter()
		{
			ActualContentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActualContent", typeof(object), typeof(NavPaneContentPresenter), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, OnActualContentPropertyChanged));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavPaneContentPresenter>();
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(NavPaneContentPresenter), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		public NavPaneContentPresenter()
		{
			((Control)this).Template = DefaultTemplate;
		}

		protected static void OnActualContentPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			((NavPaneContentPresenter)(object)d).OnActualContentChanged(e.OldValue);
		}

		protected virtual void OnActualContentChanged(object oldValue)
		{
			SetContent();
		}

		private void ClearContent()
		{
			((DXContentPresenter)this).Content = null;
		}

		private void SetContent()
		{
			ClearContent();
			((DXContentPresenter)this).Content = ActualContent;
			if (((DXContentPresenter)this).Content is UIElement uIElement)
			{
				BaseValueSource baseValueSource = DependencyPropertyHelper.GetValueSource(uIElement, Control.ForegroundProperty).BaseValueSource;
				if ((baseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Default || baseValueSource == BaseValueSource.Inherited) && ((FrameworkElement)this).DataContext is NavBarGroup element)
				{
					uIElement.SetCurrentValue(Control.ForegroundProperty, TextElement.GetForeground((DependencyObject)(object)element));
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavPaneFlyoutControl : FlyoutControl
	{
		protected internal Popup Popup => ((FlyoutBase)this).Popup;

		public NavPaneFlyoutControl()

	public class NavPanePopupSizeGrip : Thumb
	{
		private FrameworkElement parent;

		private FrameworkElement root;

		static NavPanePopupSizeGrip()
		{
		}

		public NavPanePopupSizeGrip()
		{
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
			base.Unloaded += OnUnloaded;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			parent = (FrameworkElement)FrameworkElementExtensions.GetTemplatedParent((FrameworkElement)this);
			root = GetRootElement() as FrameworkElement;
		}

		protected virtual void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DragDelta += OnDragDelta;
		}

		protected virtual void OnUnloaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			base.DragDelta -= OnDragDelta;
		}

		private double GetMaxWidth()
		{
			if (!(base.DataContext is NavBarGroup { NavBar: var navBar }))
			{
				return parent.MaxWidth;
			}
			if (navBar == null)
			{
				return parent.MaxWidth;
			}
			if (!(navBar.View is NavigationPaneView navigationPaneView))
			{
				return parent.MaxWidth;
			}
			return navigationPaneView.MaxPopupWidth;
		}

		private UIElement GetRootElement()
		{
			return LayoutHelper.GetRoot((FrameworkElement)this);
		}

		private void OnDragDelta(object sender, DragDeltaEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateParentWidth(e.HorizontalChange);
		}

		private void UpdateParentWidth(double horizontalChange)
		{
			double num = parent.ActualWidth + horizontalChange;
			double maxWidth = GetMaxWidth();
			if (num <= parent.MinWidth)
			{
				parent.Width = parent.MinWidth;
			}
			else if (maxWidth <= num)
			{
				parent.Width = maxWidth;
			}
			else
			{
				parent.Width = num;
			}
		}
	}
	public enum GroupPosition
	{
		First,
		Last,
		Middle,
		Single
	}
	public class NavBarPositionPanel : NavBarStackPanel
	{
		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey GroupPositionPropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty GroupPositionProperty;

		static NavBarPositionPanel()
		{
			GroupPositionPropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttachedReadOnly("GroupPosition", typeof(GroupPosition), typeof(NavBarPositionPanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(GroupPosition.Middle));
			GroupPositionProperty = GroupPositionPropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
		}

		internal static void SetGroupPosition(DependencyObject d, GroupPosition value)
		{
			if (((FrameworkElement)d).DataContext is DependencyObject)
			{
				((DependencyObject)((FrameworkElement)d).DataContext).SetValue(GroupPositionPropertyKey, value);
			}
			d.SetValue(GroupPositionPropertyKey, value);
		}

		public static GroupPosition GetGroupPosition(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (GroupPosition)d.GetValue(GroupPositionProperty);
		}

		private Size OnMeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			UpdateChildren();
			return MeasureChildrenOverride(availableSize);
		}

		protected virtual Size MeasureChildrenOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			return base.MeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		protected sealed override Size MeasureOverride(Size availableSize)
		{
			return OnMeasureOverride(availableSize);
		}

		private void UpdateChildren()
		{
			List<UIElement> list = new List<UIElement>();
			foreach (UIElement child in base.Children)
			{
				if (UIElementHelper.IsVisible(child))
				{
					list.Add(child);
				}
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < list.Count; i++)
			{
				SetGroupPosition(list[i], GetGroupPosition(i, list.Count));
			}
		}

		private GroupPosition GetGroupPosition(int index, int count)
		{
			if (count == 1)
			{
				return GroupPosition.Single;
			}
			if (index == 0)
			{
				return GroupPosition.First;
			}
			if (index == count - 1)
			{
				return GroupPosition.Last;
			}
			return GroupPosition.Middle;
		}
	}
	public class NavPanePopupWindowContent : ContentControl, IScrollMode
	{
		private bool sizeValid;

		ScrollControl IScrollMode.ScrollControl => (ScrollControl)GetTemplateChild("scrollControl");

		public NavPanePopupWindowContent()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.Set<NavPanePopupWindowContent>(this);
			base.Loaded += OnLoaded;
		}

		private void OnLoaded(object sender, RoutedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.DataContext is NavBarGroup)
			{
				NavBarGroup navBarGroup = (NavBarGroup)base.DataContext;
				NavBarControl navBar = navBarGroup.NavBar;
				if (navBar != null && navBar.View is NavigationPaneView navigationPaneView && !double.IsNaN(base.Width) && base.Width > navigationPaneView.MaxPopupWidth)
				{
					base.Width = navigationPaneView.MaxPopupWidth;
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnRenderSizeChanged(SizeChangedInfo sizeInfo)
		{
			if (SystemParameters.MenuDropAlignment && base.Parent is NavPanePopup navPanePopup)
			{
				if (sizeInfo.PreviousSize.Width != 0.0)
				{
					sizeValid = true;
				}
				navPanePopup.UpdateHorizontalOffset();
				if (sizeValid)
				{
					navPanePopup.HorizontalOffset += sizeInfo.NewSize.Width - sizeInfo.PreviousSize.Width;
				}
			}
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			SetBinding(ScrollingSettings.ScrollModeProperty, new Binding("ActualScrollMode"));
			UpdateScrollModeStates();
		}

		private void UpdateScrollModeStates()
		{
			ScrollingSettings.UpdateScrollModeStates(this);
		}
	}
	public class NavPanePopupWindowFrame : ContentControl
	{
		public NavPanePopupWindowFrame()
		{
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.Set<NavPanePopupWindowFrame>(this);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarStringIdExtension : MarkupExtension

			StringId = stringId;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
