		}
	}
	public class CollapsedNavPaneDisplayModeConverter : IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (parameter is NavPaneImageAndTextDecorator && values[1] is bool && (bool)values[1])
			{
				return DisplayMode.ImageAndText;
			}
			return values[0];
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}
	}
	internal static class SettingsProvider
	{
		public static void SetPropertiesBinding(Image image, object imageFallbackSize, Control content, object settingsSource)
		{
			SetImagePropertiesBinding(image, imageFallbackSize, settingsSource);
			SetTextPropertiesBinding(content, settingsSource);
		}

		private static void SetImagePropertiesBinding(Image image, object imageFallbackSize, object settingsSource)
		{
			if (image != null)
			{
				image.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.HeightProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).Height", NavBarViewBase.ImageSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = imageFallbackSize
				});
				image.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.WidthProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).Width", NavBarViewBase.ImageSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = imageFallbackSize
				});
				image.SetBinding(Image.StretchProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).Stretch", NavBarViewBase.ImageSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = Stretch.Uniform
				});
				image.SetBinding(Image.StretchDirectionProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).StretchDirection", NavBarViewBase.ImageSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = StretchDirection.Both
				});
				image.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).ImageHorizontalAlignment", NavBarViewBase.LayoutSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = HorizontalAlignment.Left
				});
				image.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).ImageVerticalAlignment", NavBarViewBase.LayoutSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = VerticalAlignment.Center
				});
			}
		}

		private static void SetTextPropertiesBinding(Control content, object settingsSource)
		{
			if (content != null)
			{
				content.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.VerticalAlignmentProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).TextVerticalAlignment", NavBarViewBase.LayoutSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = VerticalAlignment.Center
				});
				content.SetBinding(FrameworkElement.HorizontalAlignmentProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).TextHorizontalAlignment", NavBarViewBase.LayoutSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
				});
				content.SetBinding(Control.VerticalContentAlignmentProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).TextVerticalAlignment", NavBarViewBase.LayoutSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = VerticalAlignment.Center
				});
				content.SetBinding(Control.HorizontalContentAlignmentProperty, new Binding
				{
					Path = new PropertyPath("(0).TextHorizontalAlignment", NavBarViewBase.LayoutSettingsProperty),
					Source = settingsSource,
					FallbackValue = HorizontalAlignment.Stretch
				});
			}
		}
	}
	public class BooleanToHiddenConverter : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return (!(bool)value) ? Visibility.Hidden : Visibility.Visible;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}
	public class IntToBooleanConverter : MarkupExtension, IValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return (int)value > 0;
		}

		public object ConvertBack(object value, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}

		}

		protected internal override void OnScrollModeChanged()

			{
				UpdateScrollingTemplate();
			}
		}

		protected internal override void SetBindings()

	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Vertical", GroupName = "OrientationStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "Horizontal", GroupName = "OrientationStates")]
	public class ScrollableContentControl : ContentControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty AllowScrollingProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ButtonUpTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ButtonDownTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NavBarViewKindProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NotAllowScrollingTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ScrollModeProperty;

		public bool AllowScrolling
		{
			get
			{
				return (bool)GetValue(AllowScrollingProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(AllowScrollingProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ControlTemplate ButtonDownTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (ControlTemplate)GetValue(ButtonDownTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ButtonDownTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ControlTemplate ButtonUpTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (ControlTemplate)GetValue(ButtonUpTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ButtonUpTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public NavBarViewKind NavBarViewKind
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarViewKind)GetValue(NavBarViewKindProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(NavBarViewKindProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ControlTemplate NotAllowScrollingTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (ControlTemplate)GetValue(NotAllowScrollingTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(NotAllowScrollingTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ScrollMode ScrollMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (ScrollMode)GetValue(ScrollModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ScrollModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static ScrollableContentControl()
		{
			AllowScrollingProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("AllowScrolling", typeof(bool), typeof(ScrollableContentControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(true, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ScrollableContentControl)d).OnAllowScrollingChanged();
			}));
			ButtonUpTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ButtonUpTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(ScrollableContentControl));
			ButtonDownTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ButtonDownTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(ScrollableContentControl));
			NavBarViewKindProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavBarViewKind", typeof(NavBarViewKind), typeof(ScrollableContentControl), new PropertyMetadata(NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ScrollableContentControl)d).OnNavBarViewKindChanged();
			}));
			NotAllowScrollingTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NotAllowScrollingTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(ScrollableContentControl));
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(ScrollableContentControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ScrollableContentControl)d).OnOrientationChanged();
			}));
			ScrollModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ScrollMode", typeof(ScrollMode), typeof(ScrollableContentControl), new PropertyMetadata(ScrollMode.Buttons, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ScrollableContentControl)d).OnScrollModeChanged();
			}));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ScrollableContentControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			UpdateAnimations();
			SetBindings();
			UpdateOrientationStates();
			OnNavBarViewKindChanged();
		}

		private void OnAllowScrollingChanged()
		{
			UpdateScrollingTemplate();
		}

		private void OnNavBarViewKindChanged()
		{
			if (NavBarViewKind == NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "ExplorerBar", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "NavBarViewKindNormal", useTransitions: false);
			}
		}

		private void OnOrientationChanged()
		{
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnScrollModeChanged()
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void SetBindings()
		{
			string text = ((base.DataContext is NavBarGroup) ? "NavBar.View." : "View.");
			SetBinding(OrientationProperty, new Binding(text + "Orientation"));
			SetBinding(NavBarViewKindProperty, new Binding(text + "NavBarViewKind"));
		}

		private void UpdateAnimations()
		{
			NavBarVisualStateHelper.UpdateStates(this, "OrientationStates");
		}

		protected internal void UpdateScrollingTemplate()
		{
			UpdateScrollingTemplate(AllowScrolling && ScrollMode != ScrollMode.None);
		}

		protected internal void UpdateScrollingTemplate(bool allowScrolling)
		{
			if (allowScrolling)
			{
				ClearValue(Control.TemplateProperty);
				return;
			}
			SetBindings();
			Binding binding = new Binding("NotAllowScrollingTemplate");
			binding.Source = this;
			SetBinding(Control.TemplateProperty, binding);
		}

		private void UpdateOrientationStates()
		{
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Horizontal", useTransitions: false);
			}
			else
			{
				VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, "Vertical", useTransitions: false);
			}
		}
	}
	public class NavBarRepeatButton : RepeatButton
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ItemsPanelOrientationProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty GroupPositionProperty;

		internal static readonly DependencyProperty NavBarViewKindProperty;

		private DispatcherTimer timer;

		private int delayCore;

		public Orientation Orientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(OrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public Orientation ItemsPanelOrientation
		{
			get
			{
				return (Orientation)GetValue(ItemsPanelOrientationProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ItemsPanelOrientationProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal GroupPosition GroupPosition
		{
			get
			{
				return (GroupPosition)GetValue(GroupPositionProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(GroupPositionProperty, value);
			}
		}

		internal NavBarViewKind NavBarViewKind
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarViewKind)GetValue(NavBarViewKindProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(NavBarViewKindProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static NavBarRepeatButton()
		{
			GroupPositionProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("GroupPosition", typeof(GroupPosition), typeof(NavBarRepeatButton), new PropertyMetadata(GroupPosition.Middle, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarRepeatButton)d).OnGroupPositionPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ItemsPanelOrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ItemsPanelOrientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavBarRepeatButton), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarRepeatButton)d).OnItemsPanelOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			NavBarViewKindProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NavBarViewKind", typeof(NavBarViewKind), typeof(NavBarRepeatButton), new PropertyMetadata(NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarRepeatButton)d).OnNavBarViewKindPropertyChanged();
			}));
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(NavBarRepeatButton), new PropertyMetadata(Orientation.Vertical, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarRepeatButton)d).OnOrientationPropertyChanged();
			}));
			DefaultStyleKeyRegistrator.OverrideMetadata<NavBarRepeatButton>();
		}

		public NavBarRepeatButton()
		{
			base.Delay = 200;
		}

		public override void OnApplyTemplate()
		{
			base.OnApplyTemplate();
			SetBindings();
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnGroupPositionPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateGroupPositionStates();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnItemsPanelOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnNavBarViewKindPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		protected internal virtual void OnOrientationPropertyChanged()
		{
			UpdateOrientationStates();
		}

		protected override void OnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.ClickMode != ClickMode.Hover)
			{
				base.OnMouseEnter(e);
			}
			else
			{
				SetDelayOnMouseEnter(e);
			}
		}

		private void OnTimeout(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			base.Delay = 0;
			timer.Stop();
			base.OnMouseEnter(e);
		}

		protected override void OnMouseLeave(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.ClickMode == ClickMode.Hover)
			{
				base.Delay = delayCore;
				if (timer != null)
				{
					timer.Stop();
				}
			}
			base.OnMouseLeave(e);
		}

		private void SetBindings()
		{
			SetBinding(ItemsPanelOrientationProperty, new Binding(((base.DataContext is NavBarGroup) ? "NavBar." : "") + "View.ItemsPanelOrientation"));
			SetBinding(NavBarViewKindProperty, new Binding(((base.DataContext is NavBarGroup) ? "NavBar." : "") + "View.NavBarViewKind"));
			SetBinding(GroupPositionProperty, new Binding("(0)")
			{
				Path = new PropertyPath(NavBarPositionPanel.GroupPositionProperty)
			});
		}

		private void SetDelayOnMouseEnter(MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			delayCore = base.Delay;
			if (timer == null)
			{
				timer = new DispatcherTimer();
				timer.Interval = new TimeSpan(0, 0, 0, 0, base.Delay);
				timer.Tick += delegate
				{
					OnTimeout(e);
				};
			}
			timer.Start();
		}

		private string UpdateGroupPositionStates()
		{
			if (NavBarViewKind == NavBarViewKind.SideBar && (GroupPosition == GroupPosition.Last || GroupPosition == GroupPosition.Single))
			{
				return "Last";
			}
			return string.Empty;
		}

		private void UpdateOrientationStates()
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			switch (NavBarViewKind)
			{
			case NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar:
				text = "ExplorerBar";
				break;
			case NavBarViewKind.NavigationPane:
				text = "NavigationPane";
				break;
			case NavBarViewKind.SideBar:
				text = "SideBar";
				break;
			}
			if (Orientation == Orientation.Vertical && ItemsPanelOrientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				text += "ViewVerticalAndItemsPanelHorizontal";
			}
			else if (Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal && ItemsPanelOrientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				text += "ViewHorizontalAndItemsPanelVertical";
			}
			text += UpdateGroupPositionStates();
			VisualStateManager.GoToState(this, text, useTransitions: false);
		}
	}
	public class NavBarSmoothScroller : SmoothScroller
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ButtonDownProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ButtonUpProperty;

		public NavBarRepeatButton ButtonDown
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarRepeatButton)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ButtonDownProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ButtonDownProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		public NavBarRepeatButton ButtonUp
		{
			get
			{
				return (NavBarRepeatButton)((DependencyObject)this).GetValue(ButtonUpProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				((DependencyObject)this).SetValue(ButtonUpProperty, (object)value);
			}
		}

		static NavBarSmoothScroller()
		{
			ButtonUpProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ButtonUp", typeof(NavBarRepeatButton), typeof(NavBarSmoothScroller), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarSmoothScroller)(object)d).OnButtonUpPropertyChanged();
			}));
			ButtonDownProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ButtonDown", typeof(NavBarRepeatButton), typeof(NavBarSmoothScroller), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((NavBarSmoothScroller)(object)d).OnButtonDownPropertyChanged();
			}));
		}

		private void OnButtonDownPropertyChanged()
		{
			((SmoothScroller)this).UpdateAllowScrolling();
		}

		private void OnButtonUpPropertyChanged()
		{
			((SmoothScroller)this).UpdateAllowScrolling();
		}

		protected override Vector GetMinScrollDeltaOverride()
		{
			double? minScrollValue = ScrollingSettings.GetMinScrollValue((DependencyObject)(object)this);
			if (!minScrollValue.HasValue)
			{
				return new Vector(3.0, 3.0);
			}
			return new Vector(minScrollValue.Value, minScrollValue.Value);
		}

		protected override void UpdateAllowScrolling()
		{
			((SmoothScroller)this).UpdateAllowScrolling();
			if (ButtonDown != null && ButtonUp != null)
			{
				ButtonDown.Visibility = ((!((SmoothScroller)this).AllowScrollDown && !((SmoothScroller)this).AllowScrollUp) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
				ButtonUp.Visibility = ((!((SmoothScroller)this).AllowScrollDown && !((SmoothScroller)this).AllowScrollUp) ? Visibility.Collapsed : Visibility.Visible);
			}
		}
	}
	internal interface IScrollMode
	{
		ScrollControl ScrollControl { get; }
	}
	public enum ScrollMode
	{
		Buttons,
		ScrollBar,
		None
	}
	public class ScrollControl : ContentControl
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ScrollButtonsControlTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ScrollBarControlTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty NoScrollControlTemplateProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActualScrollModeProperty;

		public ControlTemplate ScrollButtonsControlTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (ControlTemplate)GetValue(ScrollButtonsControlTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ScrollButtonsControlTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ControlTemplate ScrollBarControlTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (ControlTemplate)GetValue(ScrollBarControlTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ScrollBarControlTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ControlTemplate NoScrollControlTemplate
		{
			get
			{
				return (ControlTemplate)GetValue(NoScrollControlTemplateProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(NoScrollControlTemplateProperty, value);
			}
		}

		public ScrollMode ActualScrollMode
		{
			get
			{
				return (ScrollMode)GetValue(ActualScrollModeProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ActualScrollModeProperty, value);
			}
		}

		static ScrollControl()
		{
			ScrollButtonsControlTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ScrollButtonsControlTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(ScrollControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ScrollControl)d).RefreshScrollMode();
			}));
			ScrollBarControlTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ScrollBarControlTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(ScrollControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ScrollControl)d).RefreshScrollMode();
			}));
			NoScrollControlTemplateProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("NoScrollControlTemplate", typeof(ControlTemplate), typeof(ScrollControl), new PropertyMetadata(null, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ScrollControl)d).RefreshScrollMode();
			}));
			ActualScrollModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActualScrollMode", typeof(ScrollMode), typeof(ScrollControl), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ScrollMode.Buttons, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				((ScrollControl)d).RefreshScrollMode();
			}));
			UIElement.FocusableProperty.OverrideMetadata(typeof(ScrollControl), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));
		}

		protected internal virtual void RefreshScrollMode()
		{
			switch (ActualScrollMode)
			{
			case ScrollMode.ScrollBar:
				base.Template = ScrollBarControlTemplate;
				break;
			case ScrollMode.Buttons:
				base.Template = ScrollButtonsControlTemplate;
				break;
			case ScrollMode.None:
				base.Template = ((NoScrollControlTemplate != null) ? NoScrollControlTemplate : ScrollButtonsControlTemplate);
				break;
			}
		}
	}
	public class ScrollingSettings : DependencyObject
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty ScrollSpeedProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty TopBottomIndentProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ClickModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ScrollModeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty AccelerationRatioProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DecelerationRatioProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty MinScrollValueProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsManipulationEnabledProperty;

		static ScrollingSettings()
		{
			ScrollSpeedProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("ScrollSpeed", typeof(double), typeof(ScrollingSettings), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(200.0));
			AccelerationRatioProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("AccelerationRatio", typeof(double), typeof(ScrollingSettings), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(200.0));
			DecelerationRatioProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("DecelerationRatio", typeof(double), typeof(ScrollingSettings), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(200.0));
			TopBottomIndentProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("TopBottomIndent", typeof(double), typeof(ScrollingSettings), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(0.0));
			ClickModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("ClickMode", typeof(ClickMode), typeof(ScrollingSettings), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ClickMode.Press));
			ScrollModeProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("ScrollMode", typeof(ScrollMode), typeof(ScrollingSettings), new PropertyMetadata(ScrollMode.Buttons, delegate(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
			{
				OnScrollModePropertyChanged(d, e);
			}));
			MinScrollValueProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("MinScrollValue", typeof(double?), typeof(ScrollingSettings), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(null, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits));
			IsManipulationEnabledProperty = DependencyProperty.RegisterAttached("IsManipulationEnabled", typeof(bool), typeof(ScrollingSettings), new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.Inherits));
		}

		private static void OnScrollModePropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			UpdateScrollModeStates(d as Control, (ScrollMode)e.NewValue);
		}

		public static void SetClickMode(DependencyObject d, ClickMode value)
		{
			d.SetValue(ClickModeProperty, value);
		}

		public static ClickMode GetClickMode(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (ClickMode)d.GetValue(ClickModeProperty);
		}

		public static void SetScrollSpeed(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(ScrollSpeedProperty, value);
		}

		public static double GetScrollSpeed(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(ScrollSpeedProperty);
		}

		public static double? GetMinScrollValue(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return (double?)obj.GetValue(MinScrollValueProperty);
		}

		public static void SetMinScrollValue(DependencyObject obj, double? value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(MinScrollValueProperty, value);
		}

		public static void SetAccelerationRatio(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(AccelerationRatioProperty, value);
		}

		public static double GetAccelerationRatio(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(AccelerationRatioProperty);
		}

		public static void SetDecelerationRatio(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(DecelerationRatioProperty, value);
		}

		public static double GetDecelerationRatio(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(DecelerationRatioProperty);
		}

		public static bool GetIsManipulationEnabled(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return (bool)obj.GetValue(IsManipulationEnabledProperty);
		}

		public static void SetIsManipulationEnabled(DependencyObject obj, bool value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(IsManipulationEnabledProperty, value);
		}

		public static void SetTopBottomIndent(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(TopBottomIndentProperty, value);
		}

		public static double GetTopBottomIndent(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(TopBottomIndentProperty);
		}

		public static void SetScrollMode(DependencyObject obj, ScrollMode value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(ScrollModeProperty, value);
		}

		public static ScrollMode GetScrollMode(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return (ScrollMode)obj.GetValue(ScrollModeProperty);
		}

		internal static void UpdateScrollModeStates(Control source)
		{
			ScrollMode scrollMode = GetScrollMode(source);
			UpdateScrollModeStates(source, scrollMode);
		}

		private static void UpdateScrollModeStates(Control source, ScrollMode scrollMode)
		{
			if (source is IScrollMode && ((IScrollMode)source).ScrollControl != null)
			{
				((IScrollMode)source).ScrollControl.ActualScrollMode = scrollMode;
			}
		}
	}
	public class SideBarPanel : NavBarPositionPanel
	{
		private static readonly DependencyPropertyKey ResidualSizePropertyKey;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ResidualSizeProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ActiveGroupMinHeightProperty;

		public Size ResidualSize
		{
			get
			{
				return (Size)GetValue(ResidualSizeProperty);
			}
			internal set
			{
				SetValue(ResidualSizePropertyKey, value);
			}
		}

		public double ActiveGroupMinHeight
		{
			get
			{
				return (double)GetValue(ActiveGroupMinHeightProperty);
			}
			set
			{
				SetValue(ActiveGroupMinHeightProperty, value);
			}
		}

		private SizeHelperBase SizeHelper => SizeHelperBase.GetDefineSizeHelper(base.Orientation);

		static SideBarPanel()
		{
			ResidualSizePropertyKey = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterReadOnly("ResidualSize", typeof(Size), typeof(SideBarPanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(default(Size)));
			ResidualSizeProperty = ResidualSizePropertyKey.DependencyProperty;
			ActiveGroupMinHeightProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.Register("ActiveGroupMinHeight", typeof(double), typeof(SideBarPanel), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(150.0, FrameworkPropertyMetadataOptions.AffectsMeasure));
		}

		public SideBarPanel()
		{
			base.LayoutUpdated += OnLayoutUpdated;
		}

		private bool IsActiveGroup(NavBarGroupControl child)
		{
			if (!(child.DataContext is NavBarGroup navBarGroup))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return navBarGroup.IsActive;
		}

		private NavBarGroupControl GetActiveGroup()
		{
			foreach (NavBarGroupControl child in base.Children)
			{
				if (IsActiveGroup(child))
				{
					return child;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private double GetDesiredWidthWithoutActiveGroup()
		{
			double num = 0.0;
			foreach (NavBarGroupControl child in base.Children)
			{
				if (!IsActiveGroup(child))
				{
					num += SizeHelper.GetDefineSize(child.DesiredSize);
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		protected double GetActiveGroupRestSize(Size size)
		{
			NavBarGroupControl activeGroup = GetActiveGroup();
			double num = 0.0;
			double val = 0.0;
			foreach (NavBarGroupControl child in base.Children)
			{
				val = Math.Max(val, SizeHelper.GetSecondarySize(child.DesiredSize));
				num += ((child.Visibility == Visibility.Visible) ? child.MinDesiredSize.Height : 0.0);
			}
			double num2 = 0.0;
			if (activeGroup != null)
			{
				num2 = SizeHelper.GetDefineSize(size) - num;
				if (num2 < ActiveGroupMinHeight)
				{
					num2 = ActiveGroupMinHeight;
				}
			}
			return num2;
		}

		protected override Size ArrangeOverride(Size arrangeSize)
		{
			double activeGroupRestSize = GetActiveGroupRestSize(arrangeSize);
			if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Horizontal)
			{
				ResidualSize = new Size(activeGroupRestSize, double.PositiveInfinity);
			}
			else
			{
				ResidualSize = new Size(double.PositiveInfinity, activeGroupRestSize);
			}
			Rect finalRect = new Rect(0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0);
			foreach (NavBarGroupControl child in base.Children)
			{
				if (child.Visibility != Visibility.Visible)
				{
					continue;
				}
				finalRect = new Rect(new Point(finalRect.X, finalRect.Y), child.MinDesiredSize);
				if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
				{
					double num = child.MinDesiredSize.Height;
					if (child.Expander != null)
					{
						num += activeGroupRestSize * child.Expander.AnimationProgress;
					}
					finalRect.Height = num;
					finalRect.Width = arrangeSize.Width;
				}
				else
				{
					double num2 = child.MinDesiredSize.Height;
					if (child.Expander != null)
					{
						num2 += activeGroupRestSize * child.Expander.AnimationProgress;
					}
					finalRect.Width = num2;
					finalRect.Height = arrangeSize.Height;
				}
				child.Arrange(finalRect);
				if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
				{
					finalRect.Y += finalRect.Height;
				}
				else
				{
					finalRect.X += finalRect.Width;
				}
			}
			if (base.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				finalRect.Height = 0.0;
			}
			else
			{
				finalRect.Width = 0.0;
			}
			return new Size(finalRect.Right, finalRect.Bottom);
		}

		protected override Size MeasureChildrenOverride(Size constraint)
		{
			NavBarGroupControl activeGroup = GetActiveGroup();
			double num = 0.0;
			double num2 = 0.0;
			foreach (NavBarGroupControl child in base.Children)
			{
				child.Measure(SizeHelper.CreateSize(double.PositiveInfinity, SizeHelper.GetSecondarySize(constraint)));
				num2 = Math.Max(num2, SizeHelper.GetSecondarySize(child.DesiredSize));
				num += child.MinDesiredSize.Height;
			}
			double num3 = GetActiveGroupRestSize(constraint);
			if (double.IsInfinity(num3))
			{
				num3 = ActiveGroupMinHeight;
			}
			if (!double.IsInfinity(num3))
			{
				num += num3;
			}
			if (!double.IsPositiveInfinity(SizeHelper.GetDefineSize(constraint)))
			{
				num = SizeHelper.GetDefineSize(constraint);
			}
			if (!double.IsPositiveInfinity(SizeHelper.GetSecondarySize(constraint)))
			{
				num2 = SizeHelper.GetSecondarySize(constraint);
			}
			if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(num))
			{
				num = 0.0;
			}
			if (double.IsPositiveInfinity(num2))
			{
				num2 = 0.0;
			}
			return SizeHelper.CreateSize(num, num2);
		}

		private void OnLayoutUpdated(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (base.DataContext is NavBarControl && ((NavBarControl)base.DataContext).View is SideBarView)
			{
				base.LayoutUpdated -= OnLayoutUpdated;
				((SideBarView)((NavBarControl)base.DataContext).View).SideBarPanel = this;
			}
			else if (DependencyObjectExtensions.IsInDesignTool((DependencyObject)this))
			{
				SideBarView sideBarView = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<SideBarView>((DependencyObject)this);
				if (sideBarView != null)
				{
					base.LayoutUpdated -= OnLayoutUpdated;
					sideBarView.SideBarPanel = this;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "SideBarFirst", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "SideBarFirst0", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]
	[TemplateVisualState(Name = "SideBarMiddle", GroupName = "ViewAnimationProgressGroupPositionStates")]

	{
	}

			((UIElement)this).InvalidateMeasure();

	public class ThumbDragManager : FrameworkContentElement
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty UpDragDeltaProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty DownDragDeltaProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ThumbProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty ThumbDragInfoProperty;

		public static readonly DependencyProperty OrientationProperty;

		static ThumbDragManager()
		{
			UpDragDeltaProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("UpDragDelta", typeof(double), typeof(ThumbDragManager), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(UpDragDeltaPropertyChanged));
			DownDragDeltaProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("DownDragDelta", typeof(double), typeof(ThumbDragManager), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(DownDragDeltaPropertyChanged));
			ThumbProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("Thumb", typeof(NavPaneSplitter), typeof(ThumbDragManager), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(ThumbPropertyChanged));
			ThumbDragInfoProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("ThumbDragInfo", typeof(ThumbDragInfo), typeof(ThumbDragManager));
			OrientationProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("Orientation", typeof(Orientation), typeof(ThumbDragManager), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(OrientationPropertyChanged));
		}

		private static void OrientationPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ThumbDragInfo thumbDragInfo = GetThumbDragInfo(d);
			thumbDragInfo.Orientation = (Orientation)e.NewValue;
		}

		private static void UpDragDeltaPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ThumbDragInfo thumbDragInfo = GetThumbDragInfo(d);
		}

		private static void DownDragDeltaPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ThumbDragInfo thumbDragInfo = GetThumbDragInfo(d);
		}

		private static void ThumbPropertyChanged(DependencyObject d, DependencyPropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			ThumbDragInfo thumbDragInfo = GetThumbDragInfo(d);
			thumbDragInfo.Thumb = (NavPaneSplitter)e.NewValue;
		}

		public static ThumbDragInfo GetThumbDragInfo(DependencyObject d)
		{
			ThumbDragInfo thumbDragInfo = (ThumbDragInfo)d.GetValue(ThumbDragInfoProperty);
			if (thumbDragInfo == null)
			{
				thumbDragInfo = new ThumbDragInfo();
				SetThumbDragInfo(d, thumbDragInfo);
			}
			return thumbDragInfo;
		}

		public static void SetThumbDragInfo(DependencyObject d, ThumbDragInfo info)
		{
			d.SetValue(ThumbDragInfoProperty, info);
		}

		public static double GetUpDragDelta(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(UpDragDeltaProperty);
		}

		public static void SetUpDragDelta(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(UpDragDeltaProperty, value);
		}

		public static double GetDownDragDelta(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (double)d.GetValue(DownDragDeltaProperty);
		}

		public static void SetDownDragDelta(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(DownDragDeltaProperty, value);
		}

		public static NavPaneSplitter GetThumb(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (NavPaneSplitter)d.GetValue(ThumbProperty);
		}

		public static void SetThumb(DependencyObject d, double value)
		{
			d.SetValue(ThumbProperty, value);
		}

		public static Orientation GetOrientation(DependencyObject d)
		{
			return (Orientation)d.GetValue(OrientationProperty);
		}

		public static void SetOrientation(DependencyObject d, Orientation value)
		{
			d.SetValue(OrientationProperty, value);
		}
	}
	public class ThumbDragInfo
	{
		private NavBarControl navbar;

		private NavPaneSplitter thumb;

		private Point cursorPosition;

		private bool performingTouchDrag;

		public Orientation Orientation { get; set; }

		public NavPaneSplitter Thumb
		{
			get
			{
				return thumb;
			}
			set
			{
				SetThumb(value);
			}
		}

		private double ThumbDelta { get; set; }

		private void SetThumb(NavPaneSplitter thumb)
		{
			if (this.thumb != null)
			{
				this.thumb.MouseLeftButtonDown -= OnMouseDown;
				this.thumb.MouseMove -= OnMouseMove;
				this.thumb.DragStarted -= OnDragStarted;
				this.thumb.DragCompleted -= OnDragCompleted;
				this.thumb.DragDelta -= OnDragDelta;
				this.thumb.TouchDown -= OnTouchDown;
				this.thumb.TouchUp -= OnTouchUp;
				this.thumb.TouchMove -= OnTouchMove;
			}
			this.thumb = thumb;
			if (thumb != null)
			{
				thumb.MouseLeftButtonDown += OnMouseDown;
				thumb.MouseMove += OnMouseMove;
				thumb.DragStarted += OnDragStarted;
				thumb.DragCompleted += OnDragCompleted;
				thumb.DragDelta += OnDragDelta;
				thumb.TouchDown += OnTouchDown;
				thumb.TouchUp += OnTouchUp;
				thumb.TouchMove += OnTouchMove;
				navbar = LayoutHelper.FindParentObject<NavBarControl>((DependencyObject)thumb);
			}
		}

		private void OnTouchMove(object sender, TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			cursorPosition = e.GetTouchPoint(navbar).Position;
			if (performingTouchDrag)
			{
				OnDragDelta(sender, null);
			}
		}

		private void OnTouchUp(object sender, TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			performingTouchDrag = false;
		}

		private void OnTouchDown(object sender, TouchEventArgs e)
		{
			cursorPosition = e.GetTouchPoint(navbar).Position;
			OnMouseDown(sender, null);
			performingTouchDrag = true;
		}

		private void OnMouseDown(object sender, MouseButtonEventArgs e)
		{
			NavigationPaneView navigationPaneView = (NavigationPaneView)navbar.View;
			SizeHelperBase defineSizeHelper = SizeHelperBase.GetDefineSizeHelper(Orientation);
			navigationPaneView.MaxVisibleGroupCount = navigationPaneView.ActualVisibleGroupCount;
			ThumbDelta = defineSizeHelper.GetDefinePoint(navbar.TranslatePoint(cursorPosition, navigationPaneView.Panel.Splitter));
		}

		private void OnMouseMove(object sender, MouseEventArgs e)
		{
			cursorPosition = e.GetPosition(navbar);
		}

		private void OnDragStarted(object sender, DragStartedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private void OnDragCompleted(object sender, DragCompletedEventArgs e)
		{
		}

		private double GetHeight(Size size)
		{
			if (navbar.View.Orientation == Orientation.Vertical)
			{
				return size.Height;
			}
			return size.Width;
		}

		private void OnDragDelta(object sender, DragDeltaEventArgs e)
		{
			Rect relativeElementRect = LayoutHelper.GetRelativeElementRect((UIElement)thumb, (UIElement)navbar);
			int num = 0;
			if (!performingTouchDrag)
			{
				cursorPosition = Mouse.GetPosition(navbar);
			}
			foreach (NavBarGroup group in navbar.Groups)
			{
				if (group.IsNavPaneGroup)
				{
					num++;
				}
			}
			NavigationPaneView navigationPaneView = (NavigationPaneView)navbar.View;
			SizeHelperBase defineSizeHelper = SizeHelperBase.GetDefineSizeHelper(Orientation);
			double num2 = defineSizeHelper.GetDefinePoint(navigationPaneView.Panel.GroupsControl.TranslatePoint(new Point(0.0, navigationPaneView.Panel.GroupsControl.ActualHeight), navbar)) - defineSizeHelper.GetDefinePoint(cursorPosition) - ThumbDelta;
			double num3 = 0.0;
			int num4 = 0;
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				UIElement uIElement = (UIElement)navigationPaneView.Panel.GroupsControl.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromIndex(i);
				if (num3 + defineSizeHelper.GetDefineSize(uIElement.DesiredSize) > num2)
				{
					break;
				}
				num3 += defineSizeHelper.GetDefineSize(uIElement.DesiredSize);
				num4++;
			}
			navigationPaneView.InternalSetMaxVisibleGroupCount(num4);
		}
	}
	public class MultiSourceConverter : MarkupExtension, IMultiValueConverter
	{
		public object Convert(object[] values, Type targetType, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < values.Count(); i++)
			{
				if (values[i] != null)
				{
					return values[i];
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public object[] ConvertBack(object value, Type[] targetTypes, object parameter, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public override object ProvideValue(IServiceProvider serviceProvider)
		{
			return this;
		}
	}
	public class StateInfo
	{
		public object Value { get; set; }

		public string StateName { get; set; }

		public StateInfo(object value, string stateInfo)
		{
			Value = value;
			StateName = stateInfo;
		}
	}
	public class StateSwitcher
	{
		public static void Switch(FrameworkElement target, object value, StateInfo[] stateInfo)
		{
			foreach (StateInfo stateInfo2 in stateInfo)
			{
				if (stateInfo2.Value.Equals(value))
				{
					VisualStateManager.GoToState(target, stateInfo2.StateName, useTransitions: true);
					break;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	internal class ItemSourceAdapter : ObservableCollectionConverter<object, object>
	{
		public string GroupDescription { get; set; }

		protected override void InsertItem(int index, object item)
		{
			((ObservableCollection<object>)(object)this).InsertItem(index, item);
			Subscribe(item);
		}

		protected override void RemoveItem(int index)
		{
			Unsubscribe(((Collection<object>)(object)this)[index]);
			((ObservableCollection<object>)(object)this).RemoveItem(index);
		}

		private void Subscribe(object item)
		{
			if (item is INotifyPropertyChanged)
			{
				((INotifyPropertyChanged)item).PropertyChanged += OnItemPropertyChanged;
			}
		}

		private void Unsubscribe(object item)
		{
			if (item is INotifyPropertyChanged)
			{
				((INotifyPropertyChanged)item).PropertyChanged -= OnItemPropertyChanged;
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnItemPropertyChanged(object sender, PropertyChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(e.PropertyName) || e.PropertyName == GroupDescription)
			{
				((ObservableCollection<object>)(object)this).OnCollectionChanged(new NotifyCollectionChangedEventArgs(NotifyCollectionChangedAction.Reset));
			}
		}
	}
}

namespace DevExpress.Xpf.NavBar.Internal
{
	public class NavBarGroupHelper
	{
		public static readonly DependencyProperty IsGroupHeaderProperty = DependencyPropertyManager.RegisterAttached("IsGroupHeader", typeof(bool), typeof(NavBarGroupHelper), (PropertyMetadata)new FrameworkPropertyMetadata(false));

		public static bool GetIsGroupHeader(DependencyObject obj)
		{
			return (bool)obj.GetValue(IsGroupHeaderProperty);
		}

		public static void SetIsGroupHeader(DependencyObject obj, bool value)
		{
			obj.SetValue(IsGroupHeaderProperty, value);
		}
	}
	public class SelectionStrategy
	{
		private readonly NavBarControl owner;

		private readonly Locker publicLocker;

		private readonly Locker multipleSelectionLocker;

		private readonly Locker commonLocker;

		private readonly Locker indexSelectionLocker;

		private readonly Locker singleSelectionLocker;

		private readonly Locker changingEventLocker;

		private readonly Locker groupChangingEventLocker;

		private bool allowSelectMultiple;

		private bool allowSelectDisabled;

		private bool allowSelectItems;

		private NavBarGroup selectedGroup;

		private NavBarItem selectedItem;

		private List<NavBarItem> selectedItems;

		private List<NavBarItem> pendingSelectedItems;

		protected NavBarControl Owner => owner;

		protected NavBarViewBase View => Owner.View;

		protected bool IsLocked
		{
			get
			{
				if (!publicLocker.IsLocked)
				{
					return commonLocker.IsLocked;
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public bool AllowGroupChangingEvent => !groupChangingEventLocker.IsLocked;

		public bool AllowSelectItems
		{
			get
			{
				return allowSelectItems;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != allowSelectItems)
				{
					allowSelectItems = value;
					OnAllowSelectItemsChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public bool AllowSelectDisabled
		{
			get
			{
				return allowSelectDisabled;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != allowSelectDisabled)
				{
					allowSelectDisabled = value;
					OnAllowSelectDisabledChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public bool AllowSelectMultiple
		{
			get
			{
				return allowSelectMultiple;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value != allowSelectMultiple)
				{
					allowSelectMultiple = value;
					OnAllowSelectMultipleChanged();
				}
			}
		}

		public List<NavBarItem> SelectedItems
		{
			get
			{
				if (!multipleSelectionLocker.IsLocked)
				{
					return selectedItems;
				}
				return pendingSelectedItems;
			}
			set
			{
				if (SelectedItems != value)
				{
					List<NavBarItem> oldSelectedItems = SelectedItems;
					if (multipleSelectionLocker.IsLocked)
					{
						pendingSelectedItems = value;
						return;
					}
					selectedItems = value;
					UpdateOwnerSelectedItems(oldSelectedItems);
				}
			}
		}

		public NavBarItem SelectedItem
		{
			get
			{
				return selectedItem;
			}
			set
			{
				if (selectedItem != value)
				{
					NavBarItem oldSelectedItem = selectedItem;
					selectedItem = value;
					UpdateOwnerSelectedItem(oldSelectedItem);
				}
			}
		}

		public NavBarGroup SelectedGroup
		{
			get
			{
				return selectedGroup;
			}
			set
			{
				if (selectedGroup != value)
				{
					NavBarGroup oldValue = selectedGroup;
					selectedGroup = value;
					UpdateOwnerSelectedGroup(oldValue);
				}
			}
		}

		protected internal NavBarItem LastSelectedItem { get; set; }

		private void UpdateOwnerSelectedGroup(NavBarGroup oldValue)
		{
			oldValue.Do(delegate(NavBarGroup x)
			{
				x.IsActive = false;
			});
			Owner.SelectedGroup = SelectedGroup.With((NavBarGroup x) => GetGroupSourceObject(x)) ?? SelectedGroup;
			Owner.GetBindingExpression(NavBarControl.SelectedGroupProperty).Do(delegate(BindingExpression x)
			{
				x.UpdateSource();
			});
			Owner.ActiveGroup = SelectedGroup;
			if (SelectedGroup != null)
			{
				SelectedGroup.IsActive = true;
				SelectedGroup.UpdateScrollMode();
				SelectedGroup.RaiseActivateEvent();
			}
			if (View != null)
			{
				View.RaiseEvent(new NavBarActiveGroupChangedEventArgs(SelectedGroup)
				{
					RoutedEvent = NavBarViewBase.ActiveGroupChangedEvent
				});
			}
			if (SelectedGroup != null)
			{
				if (SelectedGroup.SelectedItem != null && (AllowSelectMultiple ? (!SelectedItems.Contains(SelectedGroup.SelectedItem)) : (SelectedGroup.SelectedItem != SelectedItem)))
				{
					SelectItem(SelectedGroup.SelectedItem, SelectedGroup);
				}
				if (SelectedGroup.SelectedItem == null && SelectedGroup.SelectedItemIndex != -1)
				{
					SelectItem(SelectedGroup, SelectedGroup.SelectedItemIndex);
				}
			}
		}

		private void UpdateOwnerSelectedItem(NavBarItem oldSelectedItem)
		{
			Owner.SelectedItem = SelectedItem.With((NavBarItem x) => x.SourceObject) ?? SelectedItem;
			if (AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				return;
			}
			SelectedItems = new List<NavBarItem>();
			if (oldSelectedItem.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group) != SelectedItem.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group))
			{
				ClearItemSelection(oldSelectedItem);
			}
			else
			{
				changingEventLocker.Lock();
				oldSelectedItem.Do(delegate(NavBarItem x)
				{
					x.IsSelected = false;
				});
				changingEventLocker.Unlock();
			}
			SelectedItem.Do(delegate(NavBarItem x)
			{
				x.IsSelected = true;
			});
			SelectedItem.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group).Do(delegate(NavBarGroup x)
			{
				if (x.SelectedItem != SelectedItem)
				{
					x.SelectedItem = SelectedItem;
				}
			});
			RaiseItemSelected(SelectedItem, SelectedGroup);
		}

		private void RaiseItemSelected(NavBarItem item, NavBarGroup group)
		{
			if (View != null)
			{
				View.RaiseEvent(new NavBarItemSelectedEventArgs(item.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group) ?? group, item)
				{
					RoutedEvent = NavBarViewBase.ItemSelectedEvent
				});
			}
		}

		private void ClearItemSelection(NavBarItem oldSelectedItem)
		{
			changingEventLocker.Lock();
			oldSelectedItem.Do(delegate(NavBarItem x)
			{
				x.IsSelected = false;
			});
			oldSelectedItem.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group).Do(delegate(NavBarGroup x)
			{
				x.SelectedItem = null;
			});
			changingEventLocker.Unlock();
		}

		private void UpdateOwnerSelectedItems(List<NavBarItem> oldSelectedItems)
		{
			commonLocker.Lock();
			if (AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				SelectedItem = null;
			}
			IEnumerable<NavBarItem> enumerable = oldSelectedItems.Except(SelectedItems);
			IEnumerable<NavBarItem> enumerable2 = SelectedItems.Except(oldSelectedItems);
			List<NavBarItem> list = new List<NavBarItem>();
			foreach (NavBarItem removedItem in enumerable)
			{
				NavBarItem newItem = enumerable2.FirstOrDefault((NavBarItem x) => x.Group == removedItem.Group);
				if (newItem != null && (AllowSelectMultiple || SelectedItem != newItem))
				{
					list.Add(removedItem);
					removedItem.IsSelected = false;
					newItem.IsSelected = true;
					newItem.Group.Do(delegate(NavBarGroup x)
					{
						x.SelectedItem = newItem;
					});
				}
			}
			enumerable = enumerable.Except(list);
			enumerable2 = enumerable2.Except(list);
			foreach (NavBarItem item in enumerable)
			{
				if (AllowSelectMultiple || SelectedItem != item)
				{
					item.IsSelected = false;
					item.Group.Do(delegate(NavBarGroup x)
					{
						x.SelectedItem = null;
					});
				}
			}
			foreach (NavBarItem addedItem in enumerable2)
			{
				addedItem.IsSelected = true;
				addedItem.Group.Do(delegate(NavBarGroup x)
				{
					x.SelectedItem = addedItem;
				});
			}
			Owner.SelectedItems = SelectedItems.Select((NavBarItem x) => GetSourceIfExists(x)).AsEnumerable();
			pendingSelectedItems = new List<NavBarItem>();
			foreach (NavBarItem item2 in enumerable2)
			{
				RaiseItemSelected(item2, null);
			}
			commonLocker.Unlock();
		}

		public SelectionStrategy(NavBarControl owner)
		{
			//IL_0024: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002e: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_002f: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0039: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_003a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0044: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0045: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_004f: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0050: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_005a: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_005b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0065: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0066: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0070: Expected O, but got Unknown
			this.owner = owner;
			selectedItems = new List<NavBarItem>();
			pendingSelectedItems = new List<NavBarItem>();
			publicLocker = new Locker();
			multipleSelectionLocker = new Locker();
			commonLocker = new Locker();
			indexSelectionLocker = new Locker();
			singleSelectionLocker = new Locker();
			changingEventLocker = new Locker();
			groupChangingEventLocker = new Locker();
			multipleSelectionLocker.Unlocked += delegate
			{
				SelectedItems = pendingSelectedItems.Where((NavBarItem x) => x != null).ToList();
			};
		}

		public void Lock()
		{
			publicLocker.Lock();
		}

		public void Unlock()
		{
			publicLocker.Unlock();
			publicLocker.DoIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
			{
				SelectFirstVisibleGroup();
				UpdateSelectedItem();
			});
		}

		public void UpdateSelectedItem()
		{
			NavBarItem navBarItem = null;
			if (SelectedGroup != null)
			{
				navBarItem = SelectedGroup.SynchronizedItems.FirstOrDefault((NavBarItem x) => x.SourceObject != null && x.SourceObject == SelectedGroup.SelectedItem.With((NavBarItem i) => i.SourceObject)) ?? SelectedGroup.SelectedItem ?? SelectedGroup.SynchronizedItems.FirstOrDefault((NavBarItem x) => x.IsSelected);
			}
			navBarItem = navBarItem ?? GetItem(Owner.SelectedItem);
			if (navBarItem != null)
			{
				SelectItem(navBarItem, SelectedGroup);
			}
		}

		public void SelectFirstVisibleGroup(bool ifExists = false)
		{
			if (IsLocked)
			{
				return;
			}
			groupChangingEventLocker.Lock();
			SelectedGroup = (ifExists ? null : (GetGroup(Owner.SelectedGroup) ?? GetItem(Owner.SelectedItem).With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group) ?? (from x in Owner.SelectedItems.OfType<object>()
				select GetItem(x)).FirstOrDefault().With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group))) ?? Owner.With((NavBarControl x) => x.Groups).FirstOrDefault((NavBarGroup x) => x.ActualIsVisible);
			changingEventLocker.DoLockedAction((Action)delegate
			{
				if (Owner.EachGroupHasSelectedItem || SelectedGroup == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				foreach (NavBarGroup group in Owner.Groups)
				{
					if (group != SelectedGroup)
					{
						group.SelectedItemIndex = -1;
					}
				}
			});
			groupChangingEventLocker.Unlock();
		}

		public void SelectGroup(object element)
		{
			if (IsLocked)
			{
				return;
			}
			NavBarGroup navBarGroup = GetGroup(element);
			if (!Owner.CanActivateGroup(navBarGroup) && navBarGroup != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Locker val = groupChangingEventLocker.Lock();
			try
			{
				SelectedGroup = navBarGroup;
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		public void SelectItem(object element, NavBarGroup group = null)
		{
			if (IsLocked)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				multipleSelectionLocker.DoLockedActionIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
				{
					InitializePendingSelection();
					AddToSelection(element, group);
				});
			}
			else
			{
				singleSelectionLocker.DoLockedActionIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
				{
					ReplaceSelection(element, group);
				});
			}
			group = group ?? GetItem(element).With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group);
			if (group != null && element != null)
			{
				SelectGroup(group);
			}
		}

		private void ReplaceSelection(object element, NavBarGroup group)
		{
			if (AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				return;
			}
			NavBarItem navBarItem = SelectedItem;
			NavBarGroup navBarGroup = SelectedItem.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group);
			NavBarItem item = GetItem(element, group);
			if (item == SelectedItem)
			{
				return;
			}
			group = group ?? item.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group);
			if (group == null && item == null)
			{
				SelectedItem = null;
				return;
			}
			if (item == null && group != SelectedGroup)
			{
				if (group.With((NavBarGroup x) => x.SelectedItem) != null)
				{
					ClearItemSelection(group.SelectedItem);
				}
				return;
			}
			SelectedItem = item;
			if (navBarGroup != group && item != null)
			{
				SelectedGroup = group;
			}
			if (SelectedItem != null)
			{
				SelectedItem.RaiseSelectEvent();
			}
		}

		private void AddToSelection(object element, NavBarGroup group = null)
		{
			if (!AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (group != null)
			{
				RemoveFromSelection(SelectedItems.FirstOrDefault((NavBarItem x) => GetItem(x).Group == group), group);
			}
			SelectedItems.Add(GetItem(element, group) ?? GetItem(element));
		}

		private void RemoveFromSelection(object element, NavBarGroup group = null)
		{
			if (group != null || element != null)
			{
				NavBarItem item = ((element == null) ? group.With((NavBarGroup x) => x.SelectedItem) : (GetItem(element, group) ?? GetItem(element)));
				SelectedItems.Remove(item);
			}
		}

		public void SelectItems(IEnumerable elements)
		{
			if (!IsLocked && AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				multipleSelectionLocker.DoLockedActionIfNotLocked((Action)delegate
				{
					SelectItemsImpl(elements);
				});
			}
		}

		private void SelectItemsImpl(IEnumerable elements)
		{
			InitializePendingSelection();
			NavBarItem[] array = SelectedItems.ToArray();
			foreach (NavBarItem element in array)
			{
				RemoveFromSelection(element);
			}
			foreach (object element2 in elements)
			{
				AddToSelection(element2);
			}
		}

		private void InitializePendingSelection()
		{
			pendingSelectedItems.AddRange(selectedItems);
		}

		private object GetSourceIfExists(object element)
		{
			if (element is NavBarItem)
			{
				NavBarItem navBarItem = (NavBarItem)element;
				return navBarItem.SourceObject ?? navBarItem;
			}
			if (element is NavBarGroup)
			{
				NavBarGroup navBarGroup = (NavBarGroup)element;
				return navBarGroup.SourceObject ?? navBarGroup;
			}
			return element;
		}

		private NavBarGroup GetGroup(object element)
		{
			if (element is NavBarGroup || element == null)
			{
				return element as NavBarGroup;
			}
			return Owner.Groups.FirstOrDefault((NavBarGroup x) => object.Equals(element, GetGroupSourceObject(x)));
		}

		private object GetGroupSourceObject(object element)
		{
			object obj = (element as NavBarGroup).With((NavBarGroup x) => x.SourceObject) ?? element;
			return (obj as CollectionViewGroup).If((CollectionViewGroup x) => !string.IsNullOrEmpty(Owner.GroupDescription)).With((CollectionViewGroup x) => x.Name) ?? obj;
		}

		private NavBarItem GetItem(object element, NavBarGroup group = null)
		{
			if (element is NavBarItem || element == null)
			{
				return element as NavBarItem;
			}
			if (group != null)
			{
				return group.SynchronizedItems.Where((NavBarItem x) => x.SourceObject == element).FirstOrDefault();
			}
			return (from x in Owner.Groups.SelectMany((NavBarGroup x) => x.SynchronizedItems)
				where x.SourceObject == element
				select x).FirstOrDefault();
		}

		public void UpdateSelectionSettings()
		{
			changingEventLocker.DoLockedAction((Action)delegate
			{
				AllowSelectItems = Owner.AllowSelectItem;
				AllowSelectDisabled = Owner.AllowSelectDisabledItem;
				AllowSelectMultiple = Owner.EachGroupHasSelectedItem;
			});
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowSelectItemsChanged()
		{
			if (!AllowSelectItems)
			{
				if (AllowSelectMultiple)
				{
					SelectItems((IEnumerable)EmptyEnumerable<object>.Instance);
				}
				else
				{
					SelectItem(null, SelectedGroup);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowSelectDisabledChanged()
		{
			if (AllowSelectDisabled)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				SelectItems(SelectedItems.Where((NavBarItem x) => GetItem(x).If((NavBarItem i) => i.IsEnabled).ReturnSuccess()).ToArray());
			}
			else if (GetItem(SelectedItem, SelectedGroup).If((NavBarItem x) => !x.IsEnabled).ReturnSuccess())
			{
				SelectItem(null, SelectedGroup);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnAllowSelectMultipleChanged()
		{
			if (AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				SelectItems((SelectedItem == null) ? new object[0] : new object[1] { SelectedItem });
			}
			else
			{
				SelectItem(SelectedItems.LastOrDefault());
			}
		}

		internal void SelectItem(NavBarGroup group, int newIndex)
		{
			if (IsValidIndex(group, newIndex) && !indexSelectionLocker.IsLocked)
			{
				SelectItem((newIndex == -1) ? null : group.SynchronizedItems[newIndex], group);
			}
		}

		private bool IsValidIndex(NavBarGroup group, int newIndex)
		{
			if (newIndex < 0 || group.SynchronizedItems.Count <= newIndex)
			{
				return newIndex == -1;
			}
			return true;
		}

		internal object CoerceSelection(object item)
		{
			NavBarItem item2 = GetItem(item);
			NavBarGroup navBarGroup = item2.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group);
			if (navBarGroup != null && item2 != null)
			{
				object obj = CoerceSelection(navBarGroup, item2);
				return (obj as NavBarItem).With((NavBarItem x) => x.SourceObject) ?? obj;
			}
			return item;
		}

		internal object CoerceSelection(NavBarGroup navBarGroup, NavBarItem navBarItem)
		{
			NavBarItem result = navBarItem;
			if (!AllowSelectItems)
			{
				result = null;
			}
			if (!AllowSelectDisabled && navBarItem.If((NavBarItem x) => !x.IsEnabled).ReturnSuccess())
			{
				result = navBarGroup.SelectedItem;
			}
			if (!navBarGroup.SynchronizedItems.Contains(navBarItem) && navBarItem != null)
			{
				result = navBarGroup.SelectedItem;
			}
			if (!changingEventLocker.IsLocked && navBarItem != (SelectedItem ?? SelectedItems.Where((NavBarItem x) => x.Group == navBarGroup).FirstOrDefault()) && View != null)
			{
				NavBarItemSelectingEventArgs e = new NavBarItemSelectingEventArgs(SelectedItem.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group) ?? SelectedGroup, SelectedItem ?? SelectedGroup.With((NavBarGroup x) => x.SelectedItem), navBarGroup, navBarItem)
				{
					RoutedEvent = NavBarViewBase.ItemSelectingEvent
				};
				View.RaiseEvent(e);
				if (e.Cancel)
				{
					result = SelectedGroup.With((NavBarGroup x) => x.SelectedItem);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		internal object CoerceSelection(NavBarGroup navBarGroup, int newIndex)
		{
			if (!AllowSelectItems)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (!IsValidIndex(navBarGroup, newIndex))
			{
				return navBarGroup.SelectedItemIndex;
			}
			NavBarItem navBarItem = ((newIndex == -1) ? null : navBarGroup.SynchronizedItems[newIndex]);
			object obj = CoerceSelection(navBarGroup, navBarItem);
			if (navBarItem == obj)
			{
				return newIndex;
			}
			return navBarGroup.SelectedItemIndex;
		}

		public void UpdateSelectedItemIndexByItem(NavBarGroup group, object item)
		{
			if (group != null)
			{
				NavBarItem item2 = GetItem(item, group);
				indexSelectionLocker.Lock();
				group.SelectedItemIndex = group.SynchronizedItems.IndexOf(item2);
				indexSelectionLocker.Unlock();
			}
		}

		public void OnGroupRemoved(NavBarGroup group)
		{
			if (AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				SelectItems(SelectedItems.Where((NavBarItem x) => x.Group != group).ToList());
			}
			else if (SelectedItem.With((NavBarItem x) => x.Group) == group)
			{
				SelectItem(null);
			}
			if (SelectedGroup == group)
			{
				SelectFirstVisibleGroup(ifExists: true);
			}
		}

		public void OnItemRemoved(NavBarGroup group, NavBarItem item)
		{
			if (AllowSelectMultiple)
			{
				SelectItems(SelectedItems.Where((NavBarItem x) => x != item));
			}
			else if (SelectedItem == item)
			{
				LastSelectedItem = SelectedItem;
				SelectItem(null, group);
			}
		}
	}
	public class CollapsedNavPaneItemsSelectionStrategy
	{
		private NavBarControl Owner { get; set; }

		private NavigationPaneView View => Owner.View as NavigationPaneView;

		public CollapsedNavPaneItemsSelectionStrategy(NavBarControl owner)
		{
			Owner = owner;
		}

		public void UnselectItem(NavBarGroup group)
		{
			group.CollapsedNavPaneItems.FirstOrDefault((NavBarItem x) => x.IsSelected).Do(delegate(NavBarItem x)
			{
				x.IsSelected = false;
			});
			group.CollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem = null;
		}

		public void SelectItem(object obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			NavBarItem item;
			NavBarGroup group;
			if (obj is NavBarItem)
			{
				item = (NavBarItem)obj;
				group = Owner.Groups.FirstOrDefault((NavBarGroup x) => x.CollapsedNavPaneItems.Contains(item));
			}
			else
			{
				group = Owner.Groups.FirstOrDefault((NavBarGroup x) => x.CollapsedNavPaneItems.Any((NavBarItem i) => i.SourceObject == obj));
				item = ((group == null) ? null : group.CollapsedNavPaneItems.FirstOrDefault((NavBarItem x) => x.SourceObject == obj));
			}
			if (group == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (!item.IsSelected)
			{
				UnselectItem(group);
				item.IsSelected = true;
			}
			if (!View.EachCollapsedGroupHasSelectedItem)
			{
				foreach (NavBarGroup item2 in Owner.Groups.Where((NavBarGroup x) => x != group))
				{
					UnselectItem(item2);
				}
			}
			group.CollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem = item.With((NavBarItem x) => x.SourceObject) ?? item;
		}

		public void UpdateSelectedItem(NavBarGroup group)
		{
			NavBarItem navBarItem = group.CollapsedNavPaneItems.FirstOrDefault((NavBarItem x) => x.IsSelected);
			group.CollapsedNavPaneSelectedItem = ((navBarItem == null) ? null : (navBarItem.SourceObject ?? navBarItem));
		}

		public bool IsValidItem(object item)
		{
			if (item == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (item is NavBarItem)
			{
				return Owner.Groups.Any((NavBarGroup x) => x.CollapsedNavPaneItems.Contains(item));
			}
			return Owner.Groups.Any((NavBarGroup x) => x.CollapsedNavPaneItems.Any((NavBarItem i) => i.SourceObject == item));
		}
	}
}
namespace DevExpress.Xpf.NavBar.Automation

	public class NavBarAutomationPeerBase : BaseNavigationAutomationPeer
	{
		public NavBarAutomationPeerBase(FrameworkElement element)
			: base(element)
		{
		}

		protected DependencyObject FindObjectInVisualTree(DependencyObject root, string name)
		{
			//IL_0001: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0007: Expected O, but got Unknown
			VisualTreeEnumerator val = new VisualTreeEnumerator(root);
			while (((NestedObjectEnumeratorBase)val).MoveNext())
			{
				if (val.Current is FrameworkElement && (val.Current as FrameworkElement).Name == name)
				{
					return val.Current;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected internal AutomationPeer FindParentAutomationPeerByType(Type type)
		{
			AutomationPeer automationPeer = (AutomationPeer)(object)this;
			while (automationPeer != null && automationPeer.GetType() != type)
			{
				automationPeer = automationPeer.GetParent();
			}
			return automationPeer;
		}
	}
	public class NavBarAutomationPeerHelper
	{
	}
	public class NavBarGroupContentAutomationPeer : NavBarAutomationPeerBase
	{
		public NavBarGroupContentAutomationPeer(FrameworkElement element)
			: base(element)
		{
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			BarsAutomationHelper.GetChildrenRecursive(list, (DependencyObject)((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner);
			return list;
		}

		protected override AutomationControlType GetAutomationControlTypeCore()
		{
			return AutomationControlType.Group;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			bool flag = default(bool);
			object obj = ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).TryGetAutomationPropertyValue(AutomationProperties.NameProperty, ref flag);
			if (flag)
			{
				return (string)obj;
			}
			return "GroupContent";
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<DependencyObject> GetAttachedAutomationPropertySource()
		{
			yield return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner;
		}
	}
	public class NavBarGroupControlAutomationPeer : NavBarImageTextAutomationPeerBase, IInvokeProvider, IExpandCollapseProvider, IRawElementProviderSimple
	{
		internal NavBarGroupControl groupControl => ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner as NavBarGroupControl;

		internal NavBarGroup group
		{
			get
			{
				if (groupControl == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return groupControl.DataContext as NavBarGroup;
			}
		}

		ExpandCollapseState IExpandCollapseProvider.ExpandCollapseState
		{
			get
			{
				if (group == null)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.LeafNode;
				}
				if (!group.IsExpanded)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.Collapsed;
				}
				return ExpandCollapseState.Expanded;
			}
		}

		public IRawElementProviderSimple HostRawElementProvider => null;

		public ProviderOptions ProviderOptions => ProviderOptions.ClientSideProvider;

		public NavBarGroupControlAutomationPeer(NavBarGroupControl groupControl)
			: base(groupControl)
		{
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			//IL_00ac: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00b3: Expected O, but got Unknown
			if (groupControl == null || group == null)
			{
				return new List<AutomationPeer>();
			}
			List<AutomationPeer> list = CreateChildren();
			bool flag = group.NavBar.View.NavBarViewKind == NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar;
			Button button = (Button)FindObjectInVisualTree(groupControl, flag ? "explorerBarExpandButton" : "navPaneExpandButton");
			if (button != null)
			{
				list.Add(new NavBarButtonAutomationPeer(button));
			}
			if (group.DisplaySource == DisplaySource.Items)
			{
				ItemsControl itemsControl = (ItemsControl)FindObjectInVisualTree(groupControl, "groupItemsPresenter");
				if (itemsControl != null)
				{
					list.Add((AutomationPeer)(object)new NavBarGroupItemsControlAutomationPeer(itemsControl));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				DXContentPresenter val = (DXContentPresenter)FindObjectInVisualTree(groupControl, "groupContainerPresenter");
				if (val != null && val.Content is FrameworkElement)
				{
					list.Add((AutomationPeer)(object)new NavBarGroupContentAutomationPeer((FrameworkElement)val.Content));
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			bool flag = default(bool);
			object obj = ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).TryGetAutomationPropertyValue(AutomationProperties.NameProperty, ref flag);
			if (flag)
			{
				return (string)obj;
			}
			if (group == null || group.Header == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return group.Header.ToString();
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Invoke && group != null && group.NavBar != null && group.NavBar.View != null && group.NavBar.View.NavBarViewKind != NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar)
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.ExpandCollapse && group != null && group.NavBar != null && group.NavBar.View != null && group.NavBar.View.NavBarViewKind == NavBarViewKind.ExplorerBar)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return null;
		}

		void IInvokeProvider.Invoke()
		{
			if (group != null && group.NavBar != null)
			{
				group.NavBar.SelectionStrategy.SelectGroup(group);
			}
		}

		void IExpandCollapseProvider.Collapse()
		{
			if (group != null)
			{
				group.IsExpanded = false;
			}
		}

		void IExpandCollapseProvider.Expand()
		{
			if (group != null)
			{
				group.IsExpanded = true;
			}
		}

		public object GetPatternProvider(int patternId)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public object GetPropertyValue(int propertyId)
		{
			if (propertyId == AutomationElementIdentifiers.NameProperty.Id)
			{
				return "";
			}
			if (propertyId == AutomationElementIdentifiers.ClassNameProperty.Id)
			{
				return "NavBarGroup";
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<DependencyObject> GetAttachedAutomationPropertySource()
		{
			yield return (DependencyObject)(object)group;
			yield return groupControl;
		}
	}

	public class NavBarImageTextAutomationPeerBase : NavBarAutomationPeerBase
	{
		public NavBarImageTextAutomationPeerBase(FrameworkElement element)
			: base(element)
		{
		}

		protected virtual List<AutomationPeer> CreateChildren()
		{
			TextBlock textBlock = FindTextBlock();
			if (textBlock == null)
			{
				return ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).GetChildrenCore();
			}
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			if (FindObjectInVisualTree(((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner, "PART_Image") is Image owner)
			{
				list.Add(new ImageAutomationPeer(owner));
			}
			list.Add(new ControlElementTextBlockAutomationPeer(textBlock));
			return list;
		}

		private TextBlock FindTextBlock()
		{
			if (!(FindObjectInVisualTree(((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner, "PART_Content") is FrameworkElement reference) || VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(reference) == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (!(VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(reference, 0) is FrameworkElement reference2) || VisualTreeHelper.GetChildrenCount(reference2) == 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(reference2, 0) as TextBlock;
		}
	}
	internal class ControlElementTextBlockAutomationPeer : TextBlockAutomationPeer
	{
		public ControlElementTextBlockAutomationPeer(TextBlock block)
			: base(block)
		{
		}

		protected override bool IsControlElementCore()
		{
			return true;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			bool flag = default(bool);
			object obj = BarsAutomationHelper.TryGetAutomationPropertyValue(AutomationProperties.NameProperty, ref flag, (IEnumerable<DependencyObject>)new DependencyObject[1] { base.Owner });
			if (flag)
			{
				return (string)obj;
			}
			return base.GetNameCore();
		}

		protected override string GetAutomationIdCore()
		{
			bool flag = default(bool);
			object obj = BarsAutomationHelper.TryGetAutomationPropertyValue(AutomationProperties.AutomationIdProperty, ref flag, (IEnumerable<DependencyObject>)new DependencyObject[1] { base.Owner });
			return BarsAutomationHelper.CreateAutomationID((DependencyObject)base.Owner, (AutomationPeer)this, flag ? ((string)obj) : base.GetAutomationIdCore());
		}
	}
	public class NavBarItemControlAutomationPeer : NavBarImageTextAutomationPeerBase, IInvokeProvider, ISelectionItemProvider
	{
		private NavBarItemControl itemControl => ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner as NavBarItemControl;

		private NavBarItem item
		{
			get
			{
				if (itemControl != null)
				{
					return itemControl.DataContext as NavBarItem;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public bool IsSelected => item.IsSelected;

		public IRawElementProviderSimple SelectionContainer => FindParentAutomationPeerByType(typeof(NavBarGroupControlAutomationPeer)) as IRawElementProviderSimple;

		public NavBarItemControlAutomationPeer(NavBarItemControl navBarItemControl)
			: base(navBarItemControl)
		{
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			return CreateChildren();
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			bool flag = default(bool);
			object obj = ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).TryGetAutomationPropertyValue(AutomationProperties.NameProperty, ref flag);
			if (flag)
			{
				return (string)obj;
			}
			if (item == null || item.Content == null)
			{
				return string.Empty;
			}
			return item.Content.ToString();
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.Invoke || patternInterface == PatternInterface.SelectionItem)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void Invoke()
		{
			if (item != null && item.Group != null)
			{
				item.Group.SelectedItem = item;
			}
		}

		public void AddToSelection()
		{
			Select();
		}

		public void RemoveFromSelection()
		{
			if (item != null && item.Group != null)
			{
				item.Group.SelectedItem = null;
			}
		}

		public void Select()
		{
			if (item != null && item.Group != null)
			{
				item.Group.SelectedItem = item;
			}
		}

		protected override IEnumerable<DependencyObject> GetAttachedAutomationPropertySource()
		{
			yield return (DependencyObject)(object)item;
			yield return itemControl;
		}
	}
	public class NavBarItemsControlAutomationPeer : NavBarAutomationPeerBase
	{
		private ItemsControl itemsControl => ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner as ItemsControl;

		public NavBarItemsControlAutomationPeer(ItemsControl itemsControl)
			: base(itemsControl)
		{
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			if (itemsControl == null)
			{
				return list;
			}
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)itemsControl.Items)
			{
				list.Add(UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((UIElement)itemsControl.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(item)));
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			bool flag = default(bool);
			object obj = ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).TryGetAutomationPropertyValue(AutomationProperties.NameProperty, ref flag);
			if (flag)
			{
				return (string)obj;
			}
			if (itemsControl != null)
			{
				return itemsControl.Name;
			}
			return "";
		}
	}
	public class NavBarGroupItemsControlAutomationPeer : NavBarItemsControlAutomationPeer
	{
		private ItemsControl itemsControl => ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner as ItemsControl;

		public NavBarGroupItemsControlAutomationPeer(ItemsControl itemsControl)
			: base(itemsControl)
		{
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			if (itemsControl == null)
			{
				return list;
			}
			foreach (object item in (IEnumerable)itemsControl.Items)
			{
				UIElement reference = (UIElement)itemsControl.ItemContainerGenerator.ContainerFromItem(item);
				AutomationPeer automationPeer = UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement(VisualTreeHelper.GetChild(reference, 0) as UIElement);
				if (automationPeer != null)
				{
					list.Add(automationPeer);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected override string GetNameCore()
		{
			bool flag = default(bool);
			object obj = ((BaseNavigationAutomationPeer)this).TryGetAutomationPropertyValue(AutomationProperties.NameProperty, ref flag);
			if (flag)
			{
				return (string)obj;
			}
			if (itemsControl != null)
			{
				return itemsControl.Name;
			}
			return "";
		}
	}

	public class NavigationPaneViewAutomationPeer : NavBarViewAutomationPeer, IExpandCollapseProvider
	{
		private NavigationPaneView view => ((UIElementAutomationPeer)(object)this).Owner as NavigationPaneView;

		public ExpandCollapseState ExpandCollapseState
		{
			get
			{
				if (view == null)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.LeafNode;
				}
				if (!view.IsExpanded)
				{
					return ExpandCollapseState.Collapsed;
				}
				return ExpandCollapseState.Expanded;
			}
		}

		public NavigationPaneViewAutomationPeer(NavigationPaneView view)
			: base(view)
		{
		}

		protected override List<AutomationPeer> GetChildrenCore()
		{
			List<AutomationPeer> list = new List<AutomationPeer>();
			if (view == null)
			{
				return list;
			}
			if (view.NavBar.ActiveGroup != null)
			{
				list.Add(UIElementAutomationPeer.CreatePeerForElement((UIElement)FindObjectInVisualTree(view, "activeGroup")));
			}
			if (view.ItemsControlGroupCount > 0)
			{
				ItemsControl itemsControl = (ItemsControl)FindObjectInVisualTree(view, "groupButtonPanel");
				if (itemsControl != null)
				{
					list.Add((AutomationPeer)(object)new NavBarItemsControlAutomationPeer(itemsControl));
				}
			}
			if (view.IsOverflowPanelVisible)
			{
				ItemsControl itemsControl2 = (ItemsControl)FindObjectInVisualTree(view, "overflowPanel");
				if (itemsControl2 != null)
				{
					list.Add((AutomationPeer)(object)new NavBarItemsControlAutomationPeer(itemsControl2));
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		public override object GetPattern(PatternInterface patternInterface)
		{
			if (patternInterface == PatternInterface.ExpandCollapse)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void Collapse()
		{
			if (view != null)
			{
				view.IsExpanded = false;
			}
		}

		public void Expand()
		{
			if (view != null)
			{
				view.IsExpanded = true;
			}
		}
	}
}
